# file opened: zpolice.asm
   1  0000              ;==============================================================================
   2  0000              ; Z-POLICE  -  3D vector city flight for the ZX Spectrum Next
   3  0000              ;
   4  0000              ;  Z80N assembler (sjasmplus --zxnext).  Layer 2 256x192x8bpp, double buffered.
   5  0000              ;  The 3D viewport is the top 256x128; the bottom third is an instrument panel.
   6  0000              ;  Maths: world positions are 16-bit integers (one city cell = 256 units),
   7  0000              ;         trig values are S7.8 fixed point (1.0 = $0100).
   8  0000              ;  Hidden line removal: painter's algorithm. Buildings are walked over the
   9  0000              ;  grid far-to-near (no sort needed). Each visible face is filled in its
  10  0000              ;  shade (roof lighter, side turned away darker) and optionally outlined, so
  11  0000              ;  nearer buildings overwrite those behind them. Faces with every corner on
  12  0000              ;  screen use cached projections; faces crossing the view frustum are
  13  0000              ;  clipped in 3D (Sutherland-Hodgman, 5 planes). Rasterisation then only
  14  0000              ;  ever sees on-screen 8-bit coordinates.
  15  0000              ;
  16  0000              ;  Controls: Q/A speed up/down   Z stop      O/P turn left/right
  17  0000              ;            W/S climb/descend   E/D nose up/down
  18  0000              ;            SPACE fire          1-5 select weapon slot
  19  0000              ;            0 change the centre slot's weapon
  20  0000              ;
  21  0000              ;  Source files: zpolice.asm (resident main code), memory_bank_defs.asm
  22  0000              ;  (slots and pages).  Game logic pages, swapped into slot 6 at $C000:
  23  0000              ;  player.asm (input/movement), weapons.asm, menu.asm (main menu).  Core
  24  0000              ;  function pages, swapped into slot 7 at $E000: city.asm (Layer 2
  25  0000              ;  drawing), hud.asm (ULA HUD), keys.asm (redefinable keys), sprites.asm
  26  0000              ;  (hardware sprites).  Always mapped: zombie.asm at $2000, human.asm and
  27  0000              ;  map.asm (the city map) at $7000.
  28  0000              ;
  29  0000              ;  Memory while running:
  30  0000              ;    $0000-$1FFF  Layer 2 back buffer, one 8K section at a time (slot 0)
  31  0000              ;    $2000-$3FFF  zombie page (slot 1)
  32  0000              ;    $4000-$7FFF  occlusion data; cell colours, humans, map (slots 2-3)
  33  0000              ;    $8000-$9FFF  ULA screen (slot 4)
  34  0000              ;    $A000-$BFFF  resident code, tables, variables, stack (slot 5)
  35  0000              ;    $C000-$DFFF  game logic page (slot 6)
  36  0000              ;    $E000-$FFFF  core function page (slot 7)
  37  0000              ;==============================================================================
  38  0000
  39  0000                      DEVICE ZXSPECTRUMNEXT
  40  0000                      SLDOPT COMMENT WPMEM, LOGPOINT, ASSERTION
  41  0000                      CSPECTMAP zpolice.map
  42  0000                      OPT --zxnext=cspect --syntax=a --reversepop
  43  0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  44  0000              ; Build options
  45  0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  46  0000              ; DRAW_LINES: outline building faces with EDGE_COL vectors.  Comment it out for solid-colour buildings only; the HUD
  47  0000              ; is always drawn.  Can also be set from the command line: -DDRAW_LINES
  48  0000                      ;DEFINE DRAW_LINES
  49  0000
  50  0000              ; WAIT_FOR_VBLANK: wait for the raster to leave the display area before
  51  0000              ; flipping the Layer 2 buffers (no tearing).  Comment it out to flip as soon
  52  0000              ; as a frame is drawn: faster, but the flip may tear mid-screen.
  53  0000              ; Command line: -DWAIT_FOR_VBLANK
  54  0000                      ;DEFINE WAIT_FOR_VBLANK
  55  0000
  56  0000              ; DRAW_FPS: measure frames per second from the raster (vtick) and show it at
  57  0000              ; the top left of the ULA screen.  Comment it out to drop the measuring and
  58  0000              ; the display.  Command line: -DDRAW_FPS
  59  0000                      ;DEFINE DRAW_FPS
  60  0000
  61  0000              ; SKY_IS_WALL: the camera can never rise above the perimeter wall, so fill
  62  0000              ; the sky in the wall's colour and draw wall cells only up to camera height
  63  0000              ; (their top edge is then exactly the horizon).  No sky is ever seen.
  64  0000              ; Command line: -DSKY_IS_WALL
  65  0000                      DEFINE SKY_IS_WALL
  66  0000
  67  0000              ; OCCLUSION_CULL: in level flight, record the buildings, then cull those
  68  0000              ; completely hidden behind nearer ones (a per-column cover buffer, front to
  69  0000              ; back) before drawing the rest.  Uses pages PAGE_OCC0/1 at $4000-$7FFF.
  70  0000              ; Command line: -DOCCLUSION_CULL
  71  0000                      DEFINE OCCLUSION_CULL
  72  0000
  73  0000              ; LEVEL_FAST_PATH: while pitch is 0 (level flight) a building's top corners
  74  0000              ; have the same camera X and Z as the bottom corners below them, so each top
  75  0000              ; corner reuses its bottom corner's screen x and 1/Z and only works out its
  76  0000              ; screen y.  Comment it out to project every corner in full.
  77  0000              ; Command line: -DLEVEL_FAST_PATH
  78  0000                      DEFINE LEVEL_FAST_PATH
  79  0000
  80  0000                      INCLUDE "memory_bank_defs.asm"  ; MMU slots, registers, addresses, pages
# file opened: memory_bank_defs.asm
   1+ 0000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ 0000              ; Z-POLICE - memory bank definitions
   3+ 0000              ;
   4+ 0000              ; The Next maps its RAM into the Z80's 64K as eight 8K MMU slots, each set by
   5+ 0000              ; a NextReg ($50-$57) to an 8K page (page n = half of 16K bank n/2).  This
   6+ 0000              ; file names the slots, their registers and addresses, what each slot is
   7+ 0000              ; used for, and which page holds what.  Use:
   8+ 0000              ;   nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_CITY            ; page the city code in at $E000
   9+ 0000              ;   MMU CODE_SLOT e, PAGE_CITY, CODE_ADDR ; (sjasmplus) assemble into it
  10+ 0000              ;
  11+ 0000              ; Memory map (see README.md):
  12+ 0000              ;   slot 0 $0000  Layer 2 sections (32 rows each) while drawing; the sine
  13+ 0000              ;                 table (PAGE_SIN) whenever get_sin reads it
  14+ 0000              ;   slot 1 $2000  zombie page, always mapped
  15+ 0000              ;   slot 2 $4000  occlusion data
  16+ 0000              ;   slot 3 $6000  city_col; $7000 humans, map (always mapped)
  17+ 0000              ;   slot 4 $8000  ULA screen (page 10, the first half of bank 5)
  18+ 0000              ;   slot 5 $A000  resident main code, variables, stack (page 11, the unused
  19+ 0000              ;                 second half of bank 5)
  20+ 0000              ;   slot 6 $C000  game logic pages, swapped: player, weapons, menu
  21+ 0000              ;   slot 7 $E000  core function pages, swapped: city (Layer 2 drawing), HUD,
  22+ 0000              ;                 keys, sprites; a sprite bank while it is loaded; the
  23+ 0000              ;                 start-up set-up (initialise.asm) once
  24+ 0000              ; So game logic in slot 6 can call any core page in slot 7: main_loop pages
  25+ 0000              ; the keys in at $E000 while the player and weapons pages run at $C000.
  26+ 0000              ; At start-up the NEX loader's default mapping is in place (slot 6 = page
  27+ 0000              ; 0), so the entry point is a stub in PAGE_BOOT at $C000 (boot, zpolice.asm)
  28+ 0000              ; that maps the resident page into slot 5 and jumps to start.
  29+ 0000              ;==============================================================================
  30+ 0000
  31+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  32+ 0000              ; MMU slots: number, NextReg, address
  33+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  34+ 0000              MEMBANK_SIZE            equ $2000       ; 8K a slot / page
  35+ 0000
  36+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_0              equ 0
  37+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_1              equ 1
  38+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_2              equ 2
  39+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_3              equ 3
  40+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_4              equ 4
  41+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_5              equ 5
  42+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_6              equ 6
  43+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_7              equ 7
  44+ 0000
  45+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER     equ $50
  46+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_1_REGISTER     equ $51
  47+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_2_REGISTER     equ $52
  48+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_3_REGISTER     equ $53
  49+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_4_REGISTER     equ $54
  50+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_5_REGISTER     equ $55
  51+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_6_REGISTER     equ $56
  52+ 0000              MMU_SLOT_7_REGISTER     equ $57
  53+ 0000
  54+ 0000              membank0                equ $0000
  55+ 0000              membank1                equ $2000
  56+ 0000              membank2                equ $4000
  57+ 0000              membank3                equ $6000
  58+ 0000              membank4                equ $8000
  59+ 0000              membank5                equ $A000
  60+ 0000              membank6                equ $C000
  61+ 0000              membank7                equ $E000
  62+ 0000
  63+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  64+ 0000              ; What each slot is used for: <use>_SLOT, <use>_MMU (its NextReg) and
  65+ 0000              ; <use>_ADDR (the ORG of code or data assembled for it)
  66+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  67+ 0000              ; slot 0: one 32-row section of a Layer 2 buffer at a time (seg_map etc.)
  68+ 0000              L2SEC_SLOT              equ MMU_SLOT_0
  69+ 0000              L2SEC_MMU               equ MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER
  70+ 0000              L2SEC_ADDR              equ membank0
  71+ 0000              ; slot 0 also: the sine table, paged in by get_sin (resident).  Only the
  72+ 0000              ; game logic calls it, never the drawing, and every Layer 2 writer maps its
  73+ 0000              ; own section first, so the two never meet.  Nothing may write to
  74+ 0000              ; $0000-$1FFF while it is mapped.
  75+ 0000              SIN_SLOT                equ MMU_SLOT_0
  76+ 0000              SIN_MMU                 equ MMU_SLOT_0_REGISTER
  77+ 0000              SIN_ADDR                equ membank0
  78+ 0000
  79+ 0000              ; slot 1: zombie.asm, always mapped
  80+ 0000              ZOMBIE_SLOT             equ MMU_SLOT_1
  81+ 0000              ZOMBIE_MMU              equ MMU_SLOT_1_REGISTER
  82+ 0000              ZOMBIE_ADDR             equ membank1
  83+ 0000
  84+ 0000              ; slot 2: occlusion buffer, draw list and building records (OCCLUSION_CULL)
  85+ 0000              OCC_SLOT                equ MMU_SLOT_2
  86+ 0000              OCC_MMU                 equ MMU_SLOT_2_REGISTER
  87+ 0000              OCC_ADDR                equ membank2
  88+ 0000
  89+ 0000              ; slot 3: city_col (tables_col.inc) at $6000, then human.asm and map.asm
  90+ 0000              ; from $7000; always mapped
  91+ 0000              TABLES_SLOT             equ MMU_SLOT_3
  92+ 0000              TABLES_MMU              equ MMU_SLOT_3_REGISTER
  93+ 0000              TABLES_ADDR             equ membank3            ; city_col
  94+ 0000              HUMAN_ADDR              equ membank3+$1000      ; human.asm onwards
  95+ 0000
  96+ 0000              ; slot 4: the ULA screen (bank 5's first 8K: bitmap and attributes)
  97+ 0000              ULA_SLOT                equ MMU_SLOT_4
  98+ 0000              ULA_MMU                 equ MMU_SLOT_4_REGISTER
  99+ 0000              ULA_ADDR                equ membank4
 100+ 0000
 101+ 0000              ; slot 5: resident main code (zpolice.asm), tables, variables, stack
 102+ 0000              MAIN_SLOT               equ MMU_SLOT_5
 103+ 0000              MAIN_MMU                equ MMU_SLOT_5_REGISTER
 104+ 0000              MAIN_ADDR               equ membank5
 105+ 0000
 106+ 0000              ; slot 6: game logic pages, swapped (player, weapons, menu)
 107+ 0000              LOGIC_SLOT              equ MMU_SLOT_6
 108+ 0000              LOGIC_MMU               equ MMU_SLOT_6_REGISTER
 109+ 0000              LOGIC_ADDR              equ membank6
 110+ 0000              ; slot 6 at start-up: the boot stub (the NEX loader maps page 0 there)
 111+ 0000              BOOT_SLOT               equ MMU_SLOT_6
 112+ 0000              BOOT_ADDR               equ membank6
 113+ 0000
 114+ 0000              ; slot 7: core function pages, swapped (city / Layer 2, HUD, keys, sprites)
 115+ 0000              CODE_SLOT               equ MMU_SLOT_7
 116+ 0000              CODE_MMU                equ MMU_SLOT_7_REGISTER
 117+ 0000              CODE_ADDR               equ membank7
 118+ 0000              ; slot 7 also: a sprite pattern bank (sprite_data.asm), paged in to run
 119+ 0000              ; its own loader, spr_load_bank
 120+ 0000              SPRBANK_SLOT            equ MMU_SLOT_7
 121+ 0000              SPRBANK_MMU             equ MMU_SLOT_7_REGISTER
 122+ 0000              SPRBANK_ADDR            equ membank7
 123+ 0000
 124+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 125+ 0000              ; 8K pages
 126+ 0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 127+ 0000              PAGE_BOOT               equ 0           ; the NEX entry stub (slot 6 at load)
 128+ 0000              PAGE_CITY               equ 1           ; city.asm: Layer 2 drawing (slot 7)
 129+ 0000              PAGE_HUD                equ 2           ; hud.asm: ULA (Layer 1) HUD (slot 7)
 130+ 0000              PAGE_PLAYER             equ 3           ; player.asm: input, movement,
 131+ 0000                                                      ; collision (slot 6)
 132+ 0000              PAGE_WEAPONS            equ 4           ; weapons.asm: player weapons (slot 6)
 133+ 0000              PAGE_ZOMBIE             equ 5           ; zombie.asm (slot 1, always)
 134+ 0000              PAGE_OCC0               equ 6           ; occlusion buffer, draw list and
 135+ 0000                                                      ; building records (slot 2)
 136+ 0000              PAGE_OCC1               equ 7           ; city_col, humans, map (slot 3,
 137+ 0000                                                      ; always)
 138+ 0000              PAGE_MENU               equ 8           ; menu.asm: main menu (slot 6)
 139+ 0000              PAGE_SPR0               equ 9           ; sprite_data.asm: sprite pattern
 140+ 0000                                                      ; bank 0 (slot 7 while loading);
 141+ 0000                                                      ; more banks: the next free pages
 142+ 0000              PAGE_ULA0               equ 10          ; the ULA screen, bank 5 (slot 4)
 143+ 0000              PAGE_MAIN               equ 11          ; zpolice.asm, resident (slot 5): the
 144+ 0000                                                      ; second half of bank 5
 145+ 0000              PAGE_KEYS               equ 12          ; keys.asm: keyboard (slot 7)
 146+ 0000              PAGE_SPRITES            equ 13          ; sprites.asm: hardware sprites
 147+ 0000                                                      ; (slot 7)
 148+ 0000              PAGE_SIN                equ 14          ; sin_tables.asm: the sine table
 149+ 0000                                                      ; (slot 0, read by get_sin)
 150+ 0000              PAGE_INIT               equ 15          ; initialise.asm: start-up set-up,
 151+ 0000                                                      ; initgame (slot 7, once)
 152+ 0000
 153+ 0000              ; Layer 2 double buffer, in 16K banks (NextReg $12 / $13 take banks; the
 154+ 0000              ; pages are bank * 2).  Layer 2 is clipped to 192 rows, so each buffer
 155+ 0000              ; uses three banks.
 156+ 0000              L2_BANK_A               equ 9           ; 16K banks 9-11 (pages 18-23)
 157+ 0000              L2_BANK_B               equ 12          ; 16K banks 12-14 (pages 24-29)
 158+ 0000              PAGE_L2_A               equ L2_BANK_A*2
 159+ 0000              PAGE_L2_B               equ L2_BANK_B*2
 160+ 0000
 161+ 0000              PAGE_ROM                equ $FF         ; MMU value: ROM in slots 0-1
 162+ 0000
# file closed: memory_bank_defs.asm
  81  0000
  82  0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  83  0000              ; Tuning constants
  84  0000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  85  0000              ; Scale: one map tile (cell) is 20 m x 20 m = 256 world units, and one
  86  0000              ; height unit (building heights, the ceiling, the pad) is 10 m = 128 world
  87  0000              ; units.  World units are the same size on every axis, so 1 m = 12.8 units
  88  0000              ; and 1 unit = 7.8 cm.  The city is 64 x 64 tiles = 1280 m.
  89  0000              ; Speeds are per rendered frame, so real speed depends on the frame rate
  90  0000              ; (figures below are at 14 fps).
  91  0000              GRID            equ 64          ; the city is GRID x GRID tiles (gentables.py)
  92  0000              TILE_M          equ 20          ; metres per tile (reference scale)
  93  0000              TILE_UNITS      equ 256         ; world units per tile
  94  0000              HEIGHT_M        equ 10          ; metres per height unit
  95  0000              HEIGHT_UNITS    equ 128         ; world units per height unit
  96  0000              NEAR            equ 16          ; near clip plane: 1.25 m
  97  0000              VIEW_R          equ 6           ; buildings drawn within +/- 6 tiles (120 m)
  98  0000              DOT_R           equ 5           ; street intersection markers radius (tiles)
  99  0000              PMAX            equ 24          ; pitch limit (256 = 360 deg) - match gentables.py
 100  0000              SPEED_MAX       equ 28          ; units/frame: 2.2 m/frame, ~31 m/s (110 km/h)
 101  0000              SPEED_MIN       equ -6          ; reverse: 0.47 m/frame, ~6.6 m/s
 102  0000              YAW_RATE        equ $0180       ; turn per frame, 8.8 fixed (1.5 units;
 103  0000                                              ; 256 units = 360 degrees)
 104  0000              CLIMB_RATE      equ 4           ; units/frame: 0.31 m/frame, ~4.4 m/s
 105  0000              ; Pitch damper: with neither E nor D held, the nose eases back to level.
 106  0000              ; Every PDAMP_FRAMES frames the 8.8 pitch moves towards 0 by
 107  0000              ; max(PDAMP_MIN, |pitch| * PDAMP_K) in 1/256ths of a pitch unit, i.e. by a
 108  0000              ; fixed share of what is left, so it slows down as it nears level.
 109  0000              PDAMP_FRAMES    equ 1           ; frames between damping steps
 110  0000              PDAMP_K         equ 43          ; 43/256: about 1/6 of the pitch per step
 111  0000              PDAMP_MIN       equ 32          ; smallest step, 1/8 unit, so it reaches 0
 112  0000              MIN_Y           equ 8           ; lowest flight: 0.6 m
 113  0000              MAX_Y           equ 3*HEIGHT_UNITS/2  ; ceiling 1.5 height units: 15 m
 114  0000              BLD_MAX_H       equ 7*HEIGHT_UNITS  ; tallest building 7.0 height units: 70 m
 115  0000                                              ; (htab level 15, gentables.py; 4.7 m a level)
 116  0000              WORLD_MIN       equ 64
 117  0000              WORLD_MAX       equ GRID*256-64
 118  0000              DMA_SPAN_MIN    equ 20          ; spans this wide or wider are filled by DMA
 119  0000              ; The VTOL (the player's craft): 10 m long by 3 m wide.  Its position
 120  0000              ; (cam_x, cam_z, the camera) is its centre; the hit box against buildings is
 121  0000              ; that rectangle turned to the heading (vtol_ground), and the camera stays
 122  0000              ; COLL_H above any roof under it.
 123  0000              VTOL_LEN_M      equ 10
 124  0000              VTOL_WID_M      equ 3
 125  0000              VTOL_HL         equ VTOL_LEN_M*TILE_UNITS/(TILE_M*2)  ; half length: 64 units
 126  0000              VTOL_HW         equ VTOL_WID_M*TILE_UNITS/(TILE_M*2)  ; half width: 19 units
 127  0000              COLL_H          equ 12          ; clearance above a roof: 0.94 m
 128  0000
 129  0000              ; Weapons: 5 slots, 2 bytes each (type, ammo) in wpn_slots.  Slot 0 is the
 130  0000              ; centre gun; slots 1-4 are the wing pylon hard points (limited ammo).
 131  0000              WPN_SLOTS       equ 5
 132  0000              WPN_CENTRE      equ 0           ; slot index of the centre gun
 133  0000              WPN_PYLON1      equ 1           ; first of the 4 wing pylons (slots 1-4)
 134  0000              WPN_TYPE        equ 0           ; offset of the type byte in a slot
 135  0000              WPN_AMMO        equ 1           ; offset of the ammo counter in a slot
 136  0000              AMMO_INF        equ 255         ; ammo counter 255 = infinite ammo (never
 137  0000                                              ; decremented); 0 = empty
 138  0000              ; weapon types (placeholders until the weapons exist)
 139  0000              WPN_NONE        equ 0           ; empty slot / nothing fitted
 140  0000              WPN_GUN         equ 1           ; machine gun (centre slot)
 141  0000              WPN_LASER       equ 2           ; pulse laser (centre slot)
 142  0000              WPN_CHAIN       equ 3           ; chain gun (centre slot)
 143  0000              WPN_ROCKETS     equ 4           ; unguided rocket pod (wing pylons)
 144  0000              WPN_MISSILE     equ 5           ; guided missile (placeholder)
 145  0000              WPN_BOMB        equ 6           ; bomb (placeholder)
 146  0000
 147  0000              ; Machine gun rounds: NBULLETS records of BREC bytes in `bullets`
 148  0000              NBULLETS        equ 24          ; rounds in flight (the chain gun needs most)
 149  0000              BREC            equ 27
 150  0000              B_STATE         equ 0           ; 0 free, 1 in flight, 2 hit (shown, then freed),
 151  0000                                              ; 3 laser beam; bit 7: fresh (fired this frame)
 152  0000              B_X             equ 1           ; position, world units
 153  0000              B_Y             equ 3
 154  0000              B_Z             equ 5
 155  0000              B_SIN           equ 7           ; heading sinY, cosY (S7.8) when fired
 156  0000              B_COS           equ 9
 157  0000              B_SLOPE         equ 11          ; tan(pitch) when fired (S7.8)
 158  0000              B_S             equ 13          ; horizontal distance per frame
 159  0000              B_PX            equ 15          ; start of this frame's path (drawn from here
 160  0000              B_PY            equ 17          ; to the position; the muzzle on its first)
 161  0000              B_PZ            equ 19
 162  0000              B_TDX           equ 21          ; path distance between x / z cell boundaries
 163  0000              B_TDZ           equ 23          ; (0 = not worked out yet)
 164  0000              B_DMG           equ 25          ; damage to a zombie it hits directly
 165  0000              B_KIND          equ 26          ; 0 = round or beam, BK_ROCKET = rocket
 166  0000              BK_ROCKET       equ 1
 167  0000              NOMINAL_FPS     equ 14          ; frame rate the per-frame speeds assume
 168  0000              BULLET_MS       equ 500         ; muzzle velocity, m/s
 169  0000              BULLET_STEP     equ BULLET_MS*TILE_UNITS/(TILE_M*NOMINAL_FPS)  ; 457 units/frame
 170  0000              GUN_DY          equ -6          ; muzzle 0.5 m below the camera
 171  0000              GUN_INTERVAL    equ 4           ; machine gun: frames between rounds
 172  0000              ; Chain gun: 4x the machine gun's rate, one round every CHAIN_INTERVAL
 173  0000              ; frames.  Each round's heading and climb are turned by a random
 174  0000              ; -CHAIN_DEV..+CHAIN_DEV steps of 1/256 rad (0.22 deg): +/-2 degrees.
 175  0000              CHAIN_INTERVAL  equ 1           ; chain gun: frames between rounds
 176  0000              CHAIN_DEV       equ 9           ; spread: 9/256 rad = 2.01 degrees
 177  0000              ; Pulse laser: the beam is on for LASER_ON frames (0.1 s), then cools for
 178  0000              ; LASER_COOL frames (0.25 s) before it can fire again; holding SPACE repeats.
 179  0000              ; (Frame counts as specified; at 14 fps they last 0.21 s and 0.57 s.)  Each
 180  0000              ; beam frame it is traced from the muzzle along the view direction to the
 181  0000              ; first thing it meets, at most LASER_RANGE away.
 182  0000              LASER_ON        equ 3           ; beam frames
 183  0000              LASER_COOL      equ 8           ; cool-down frames
 184  0000              LASER_RANGE     equ 1536        ; 120 m (the draw distance)
 185  0000              ZHIT_R          equ 3           ; zombie hit box: 0.5 m wide (half 3.2 units)
 186  0000              ZHIT_H          equ 26          ; and 2 m tall
 187  0000
 188  0000              ; Damage, in hit points (zombies: ZHP / ZHP_FAST in zombie.asm; the base
 189  0000              ; tiles TERRACE_HP / PAD_HP; humans HUMAN_HP)
 190  0000              GUN_DMG         equ 4           ; machine gun, per round
 191  0000              CHAIN_DMG       equ 2           ; chain gun, per round
 192  0000              LASER_DMG       equ 1           ; pulse laser, per beam frame on a target
 193  0000
 194  0000              ; Rocket pod (WPN_ROCKETS, on a wing pylon): ROCKET_SHOTS rockets, one every
 195  0000              ; ROCKET_INTERVAL frames while SPACE is held, flying straight along the view
 196  0000              ; direction at 150 m/s.  Where one hits anything (a building, the ground, a
 197  0000              ; zombie) it bursts: BLAST_DMG to every zombie, human and base tile within
 198  0000              ; BLAST_R on each of the x, y and z axes (a box, not a sphere: cheaper, and
 199  0000              ; near enough).  A direct hit does no extra damage.
 200  0000              ROCKET_SHOTS    equ 12          ; a full pod
 201  0000              ROCKET_MS       equ 150         ; m/s
 202  0000              ROCKET_STEP     equ ROCKET_MS*TILE_UNITS/(TILE_M*NOMINAL_FPS)  ; 137 units/frame
 203  0000              ROCKET_INTERVAL equ 7           ; frames between rockets (0.5 s)
 204  0000              ROCKET_LEN      equ 13          ; drawn 1 m long (white),
 205  0000              ROCKET_TRAIL    equ 19          ; then a yellow trail to 1.5 m behind it
 206  0000              BLAST_R         equ 64          ; 5 m
 207  0000              BLAST_DMG       equ 20
 208  0000
 209  0000              ; Humans to rescue (human.asm)
 210  0000              HUMAN_MAX       equ 20          ; room for this many
 211  0000              HUMANS_INIT     equ 5           ; placed at start
 212  0000              PASSENGER_MAX   equ 5           ; the craft carries this many
 213  0000              RESCUE_R        equ 512         ; 2.0 tiles (40 m): a landed craft this close
 214  0000              HUMAN_ROOF_MAX  equ MAX_Y-HEIGHT_UNITS  ; humans aren't placed on roofs
 215  0000                                              ; taller than the ceiling - 1.0 height unit
 216  0000              HUMAN_RUN       equ TILE_UNITS*10/(TILE_M*NOMINAL_FPS)  ; 10 m/s: 9 units/frame
 217  0000              HUMAN_HP        equ 10          ; hit points (a rocket blast kills)
 218  0000              DISEMBARK       equ NOMINAL_FPS ; landed on the base pad: one off a second
 219  0000
 220  0000              ; Zombie burst: a killed zombie sprays NPART_BURST particles for PART_LIFE
 221  0000              ; frames, bright green fading to dark (weapons.asm moves them, city.asm
 222  0000              ; draws them).  Particle record: +0 age (0 = free, 1..PART_LIFE), +1..+6
 223  0000              ; x, y, z (world units), +7..+9 vx, vy, vz (signed units per frame).
 224  0000              NPART           equ 40          ; pool: room for 4 bursts at once
 225  0000              NPART_BURST     equ 10
 226  0000              PART_LIFE       equ 8
 227  0000              PREC            equ 10
 228  0000              PART_Y          equ 13          ; bursts from 1 m up
 229  0000              PART_GRAV       equ 1           ; vy lost per frame (they arc up and fall)
 230  0000
 231  0000              ; The level a new game starts on.  Normally 1; DEBUG: 20, to test the
 232  0000              ; zombies' attacks on the base (junction bias 48 + 2 * level = 88%).
 233  0000              START_LEVEL     equ 20
 234  0000
 235  0000              ; Zombies against the base (zombie.asm)
 236  0000              ZBIAS_BASE      equ 48          ; junction bias towards the pad, percent:
 237  0000              ZBIAS_STEP      equ 2           ; 48 + 2 * level (50% on level 1),
 238  0000              ZBIAS_MAX       equ 95          ; at most 95%
 239  0000              ATTACK_R        equ 26          ; a zombie attacks a base tile from 0.1 tile (2 m)
 240  0000              ZHIT_FRAMES     equ 5           ; one hit every 5 frames, 1 damage each
 241  0000              TERRACE_HP      equ 100         ; hit points of each green terrace tile
 242  0000              PAD_HP          equ 200         ; and of the landing pad (destroyed: game over)
 243  0000              GAME_OVER_FR    equ 500         ; game over screen: 10 s of 50 Hz frames
 244  0000
 245  0000              BLD_SIZE        equ 256         ; buildings fill their whole grid cell;
 246  0000                                              ; streets are the empty cells
 247  0000
 248  0000              ; Layer 2 colours (RRRGGGBB). Avoid $E3 (global transparency).
 249  0000              ; Layer 2 uses a custom palette (l2_palette from gentables.py) arranged in
 250  0000              ; families of four: family*4 + SHADE_*.  The +3 slots hold the fixed colours.
 251  0000              SKY_COL         equ 3
 252  0000              GROUND_COL      equ 7
 253  0000              EDGE_COL        equ 11
 254  0000              HUD_COL         equ 15
 255  0000              HUD_WARN        equ 19
 256  0000              DOT_COL         equ 23
 257  0000              ROCKET_COL      equ EDGE_COL    ; rockets: white streak,
 258  0000              TRAIL_COL       equ BULLET_COL  ; yellow trail
 259  0000              BULLET_COL      equ 31
 260  0000              LASER_COL       equ 35
 261  0000              MAP_BG          equ 39
 262  0000              MAP_ZOMBIE      equ HUD_COL     ; zombie blips on the city map: bright green
 263  0000              HUMAN_COL       equ 43
 264  0000              HUMAN_BLIP      equ 47
 265  0000              MAP_ZOMBIE_MID  equ 51          ; zombie blip shades (bright = MAP_ZOMBIE)
 266  0000              MAP_ZOMBIE_DARK equ 55
 267  0000              MAP_ZOMBIE_ATK  equ HUD_WARN    ; a zombie attacking the base: red
 268  0000              ZOMBIE_COL      equ 27
 269  0000              PANEL_L2        equ 91          ; the ULA panel colour, on Layer 2 (compass)
 270  0000              GAUGE_AMBER     equ 95          ; ground height gauge (Layer 2)
 271  0000              PART_COL0       equ 59          ; zombie burst particles: age 1 (bright) ..
 272  0000              PART_COL1       equ 63
 273  0000              PART_COL2       equ 67
 274  0000              PART_COL3       equ 71
 275  0000              PART_COL4       equ 75
 276  0000              PART_COL5       equ 79
 277  0000              PART_COL6       equ 83
 278  0000              PART_COL7       equ 87          ; .. age 8 (dark); a step of 4 per age
 279  0000
 280  0000              ; Screen split: the 3D viewport is Layer 2, top two thirds (256x128, rows
 281  0000              ; 0-127).  The bottom third (rows 128-191) is an instrument panel drawn on the
 282  0000              ; ULA (Layer 1), which sits above Layer 2.  The ULA screen (bank 5) stays
 283  0000              ; paged in at $8000 (slot 4): bitmap ULA_BMP, attributes ULA_ATTR.
 284  0000              VIEW_H          equ 128         ; viewport height
 285  0000              VIEW_CY         equ 64          ; viewport centre row
 286  0000              ULA_BMP         equ $8000
 287  0000              ULA_ATTR        equ $9800
 288  0000
 289  0000              ; ULA palette indices used by the panel (custom colours set in ula_init)
 290  0000              P_PANEL         equ 1           ; paper: panel background
 291  0000              C_WARN          equ 3           ; ink/paper: red (reversing)
 292  0000              C_OK            equ 4           ; ink/paper: green
 293  0000              C_YEL           equ 2           ; ink: yellow (weapon slot flash)
 294  0000              C_BLUE          equ 5           ; ink: light blue (empty weapon slot)
 295  0000              ATTR_PANEL      equ P_PANEL<<3 | 7
 296  0000
 297  0000              ; Gauges: vertical bars on Layer 2 behind transparent ULA cells, filled
 298  0000              ; from the bottom, pixel rows GAUGE_TOP..GAUGE_TOP+GAUGE_H-1 (character rows
 299  0000              ; 17-22), labelled on character row 23.  Speed ("S") is 8 pixels wide at
 300  0000              ; character column SPD_GCOL; altitude and ground height ("A") share the cell
 301  0000              ; column ALT_GCOL, 4 pixels each.
 302  0000              GAUGE_TOP       equ 136
 303  0000              GAUGE_H         equ 48
 304  0000              GAUGE_LROW      equ 23              ; label row
 305  0000              SPD_GCOL        equ 1
 306  0000              ALT_GCOL        equ 3
 307  0000              PAX_COL         equ 5               ; passengers: first character column
 308  0000              ; Compass: a 32x8 strip at pixel (COMP_X, COMP_Y) in a 2-pixel frame, on
 309  0000              ; Layer 2 behind transparent ULA cells (columns 15-20, character rows 19-20)
 310  0000              COMP_X          equ 128
 311  0000              COMP_Y          equ 156
 312  0000              COMP_BGX        equ 120             ; its 48x16 background patch
 313  0000              COMP_BGY        equ 152
 314  0000              PAX_ROW         equ 17              ; passengers aboard (pixel row 136), and
 315  0000              WPN_LBL         equ 11              ; the weapon slots: "WPN:" at this column,
 316  0000              WPN_COL         equ 15              ; slot digits 1-5 from this one
 317  0000              WFLASH          equ 5               ; empty slot requested: flash frames
 318  0000
 319  0000              ; City map on the right of the panel: 32x32 cells at 2x2 pixels = 64x64,
 320  0000              ; pixel columns MAP_X..MAP_X+63, rows 128..191.  North (+Z) is up.
 321  0000              MAP_X           equ 192
 322  0000              MAP_COL         equ MAP_X/8
 323  0000              SHADE_DARK      equ 0           ; side turned away from the player
 324  0000              SHADE_FACE      equ 1           ; side facing the player (base colour)
 325  0000              SHADE_ROOF      equ 2           ; roof, lighter
 326  0000
 327  0000              ; Layer 2 banks (L2_BANK_A / _B) and the 8K pages: memory_bank_defs.asm
 328  0000
 329  0000
 330  0000              ; zxnDMA WR0 (transfer A->B) with only the length low / high byte following
 331  0000              DMA_WR0_LEN_LO  equ %00100101
 332  0000              DMA_WR0_LEN_HI  equ %01000101
 333  0000
 334  0000              ; Self-modified opcodes for the line drawer
 335  0000              OP_INC_L        equ $2C
 336  0000              OP_DEC_L        equ $2D
 337  0000              OP_INC_H        equ $24
 338  0000              OP_DEC_H        equ $25
 339  0000
 340  0000              ; HL = HL * GRID  (a map row offset)
 341  0000                      MACRO ROWOFS
 342  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 343  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 344  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 345  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 346  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 347  0000 ~                    add hl,hl
 348  0000                      ENDM
 349  0000
 350  0000              ; dst = src + vec  (three 16-bit components)
 351  0000                      MACRO VADD QD, QS, QV
 352  0000 ~                    ld hl,(QS)
 353  0000 ~                    ld de,(QV)
 354  0000 ~                    add hl,de
 355  0000 ~                    ld (QD),hl
 356  0000 ~                    ld hl,(QS+2)
 357  0000 ~                    ld de,(QV+2)
 358  0000 ~                    add hl,de
 359  0000 ~                    ld (QD+2),hl
 360  0000 ~                    ld hl,(QS+4)
 361  0000 ~                    ld de,(QV+4)
 362  0000 ~                    add hl,de
 363  0000 ~                    ld (QD+4),hl
 364  0000                      ENDM
 365  0000
 366  0000              ; Copy cached screen corner of face vertex (HL)+ into p2buf (DE)+ with the
 367  0000              ; outline flag for edge bit EB taken from df_mask.  B = high vtx.
 368  0000                      MACRO FACEVTX EB
 369  0000 ~                    ld a,(hl)
 370  0000 ~                    inc hl
 371  0000 ~                    add a,7
 372  0000 ~                    ld c,a
 373  0000 ~                    ld a,(bc)
 374  0000 ~                    ld (de),a
 375  0000 ~                    inc e
 376  0000 ~                    inc c
 377  0000 ~                    ld a,(bc)
 378  0000 ~                    ld (de),a
 379  0000 ~                    inc e
 380  0000 ~                    ld a,(df_mask)
 381  0000 ~                    and EB
 382  0000 ~                    ld (de),a
 383  0000 ~                    inc e
 384  0000 ~                    inc e
 385  0000                      ENDM
 386  0000
 387  0000              ;==============================================================================
 388  0000              ; NEX entry: the loader leaves its default mapping (slot 6 = page 0, slot 5
 389  0000              ; = page 5), so the entry point is this stub in PAGE_BOOT at $C000.  It maps
 390  0000              ; the resident page into slot 5 and jumps to start.
 391  0000              ;==============================================================================
 392  0000                      MMU BOOT_SLOT e, PAGE_BOOT, BOOT_ADDR
 393  C000                      ORG BOOT_ADDR
 394  C000              boot:
 395  C000 F3                   di
 396  C001 31 1B C0             ld sp,boot_stack
 397  C004 ED 91 55 0B          nextreg MAIN_MMU,PAGE_MAIN
 398  C008 C3 55 B2             jp start
 399  C00B 00 00 00...              ds 16                       ; the loader's stack, until start
 400  C01B              boot_stack:
 401  C01B
 402  C01B                      MMU MAIN_SLOT e, PAGE_MAIN, MAIN_ADDR
 403  A000                      ORG MAIN_ADDR
 404  A000                      ; the generated tables (gentables.py); the sine table is in its own
 405  A000                      ; page (PAGE_SIN, slot 0), below

 411  B255              ; Entry
 412  B255              ;==============================================================================
 413  B255              start:
 414  B255 F3                   di
 415  B256 31 DD BB             ld sp,stack_top
 416  B259 ED 91 57 0F          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INIT      ; hardware set-up and memory map
 417  B25D CD 00 E0             call initgame                   ; (initialise.asm)
 418  B260
 419  B260                      ; load the shaded Layer 2 palette (first L2 palette, 9-bit entries)
 420  B260 ED 91 43 10          nextreg $43,%00010000           ; edit Layer 2 palette 0, auto-inc
 421  B264 ED 91 40 00          nextreg $40,0
 422  B268 21 55 B0             ld hl,l2_palette
 423  B26B 06 00                ld b,0                          ; 256 entries
 424  B26D 7E           .pal:   ld a,(hl)
 425  B26E 23                   inc hl
 426  B26F ED 92 44             nextreg $44,a                   ; RRRGGGBB
 427  B272 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 428  B273 23                   inc hl
 429  B274 ED 92 44             nextreg $44,a                   ; low blue bit
 430  B277 10 F4                djnz .pal
 431  B279
 432  B279 ED 5F                ld a,r                          ; random seed (xorshift: non-zero);
 433  B27B 6F                   ld l,a                          ; the menu stirs it every frame
 434  B27C 26 A5                ld h,$A5
 435  B27E 22 DC 3D             ld (zseed),hl
 436  B281 ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_KEYS
 437  B285 CD 36 E0             call kbd_double_off             ; NextReg $68 bit 4 = KBD_STOP_DOUBLING
 438  B288 ED 91 57 02          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_HUD
 439  B28C CD 15 E0             call ula_palette                ; ULA inks and papers (menu and HUD)
 440  B28F ED 91 57 0D          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPRITES
 441  B293 CD 00 E0             call sprites_init               ; (sprites.asm) on, all hidden
 442  B296 ED 91 57 09          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPR0      ; sprite bank 0: the human status
 443  B29A CD 00 E0             call spr_load_bank              ; sprites (sprite_data.asm)
 444  B29D ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU     ; (the menu pages the keys in)
 445  B2A1 C3 00 C0             jp main_menu                    ; (menu.asm) -> newGame
 446  B2A4
 447  B2A4              ;==============================================================================
 448  B2A4              ; New game: start level 1.  Start-up has set the hardware (speed, layers,
 449  B2A4              ; clip window, palettes, memory map), which the menu leaves alone; here the
 450  B2A4              ; game state and the screen contents are set up from scratch, so this also
 451  B2A4              ; works for a second game.
 452  B2A4              ;==============================================================================
 453  B2A4              newGame:
 454  B2A4 31 DD BB             ld sp,stack_top                 ; (entered by jp from the menu)
 455  B2A7 21 63 B6             ld hl,start_state               ; player, weapon slots, selection
 456  B2AA 11 37 B6             ld de,cam_x
 457  B2AD 01 1A 00             ld bc,start_state_end-start_state
 458  B2B0 ED B0                ldir
 459  B2B2 21 7D B6             ld hl,bullets                   ; no rounds or beam in flight
 460  B2B5 11 7E B6             ld de,bullets+1
 461  B2B8 01 32 04             ld bc,(NBULLETS+1)*BREC+NPART*PREC-1    ; and no particles
 462  B2BB 36 00                ld (hl),0
 463  B2BD ED B0                ldir
 464  B2BF 3E 14                ld a,START_LEVEL
 465  B2C1 32 51 B6             ld (level),a
 466  B2C4 AF                   xor a
 467  B2C5 32 52 B6             ld (game_over),a
 468  B2C8 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 469  B2CB 22 53 B6             ld (salvage_points),hl          ; no salvage yet
 470  B2CE CD 28 38             call base_init                  ; the pad and terrace, at full strength
 471  B2D1 ED 91 56 04          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_WEAPONS
 472  B2D5 CD 15 C2             call weapons_reset
 473  B2D8
 474  B2D8                      ; clear both buffers
 475  B2D8 ED 91 57 01          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_CITY
 476  B2DC 3E 40                ld a,VIEW_CY
 477  B2DE 32 B0 BA             ld (horizon),a
 478  B2E1 3E 12                ld a,PAGE_L2_A
 479  B2E3 32 D7 BA             ld (seg_base),a
 480  B2E6 CD 2F EE             call clear_screen
 481  B2E9 3E 18                ld a,PAGE_L2_B
 482  B2EB 32 D7 BA             ld (seg_base),a
 483  B2EE CD 2F EE             call clear_screen
 484  B2F1 ED 91 12 09          nextreg $12,L2_BANK_A           ; Layer 2 active (displayed) bank
 485  B2F5 ED 91 13 0C          nextreg $13,L2_BANK_B           ; Layer 2 shadow bank (drawn into)
 486  B2F9 01 3B 12             ld bc,$123B
 487  B2FC 3E 02                ld a,%00000010                  ; Layer 2 visible (the menu hid it)
 488  B2FE ED 79                out (c),a
 489  B300 ED 91 57 02          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_HUD
 490  B304 CD 6A E0             call ula_init                   ; ULA: FPS overlay, panel, city map
 491  B307 CD 00 35             call zombie_init                ; place the zombies on the streets
 492  B30A CD 80 73             call human_init                 ; and the humans to rescue
 493  B30D
 494  B30D              ;==============================================================================
 495  B30D              ; Main loop
 496  B30D              ;==============================================================================
 497  B30D              main_loop:
 498  B30D ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_KEYS      ; keys at $E000, while the logic runs
 499  B311 CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan                   ; all key banks
 500  B314 ED 91 56 03          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_PLAYER   ; input/movement logic at $C000
 501  B318 CD 00 C0             call update_player
 502  B31B CD CB B4             call setup_frame
 503  B31E CD D2 35             call zombie_update              ; walk the zombies (page at $2000)
 504  B321 ED 91 56 04          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_WEAPONS  ; weapons logic at $C000
 505  B325 CD 00 C0             call update_weapons             ; fire, move rounds, hits
 506  B328 CD 01 75             call human_update               ; map dots, rescues (page at $7000)
 507  B32B 3A 52 B6             ld a,(game_over)
 508  B32E B7                   or a
 509  B32F 28 07                jr z,.play
 510  B331 ED 91 56 08          nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_MENU     ; the pad is destroyed: game over
 511  B335 C3 40 C0             jp game_over_screen             ; (menu.asm) -> main menu
 512  B338              .play:
 513  B338 CD 98 B3             call map_shadow
 514  B33B                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 515  B33B ~                    call vtick
 516  B33B                  ENDIF
 517  B33B ED 91 57 01          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_CITY      ; Layer 2 drawing code at $E000
 518  B33F CD 2F EE             call clear_screen
 519  B342                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 520  B342 ~                    call vtick
 521  B342                  ENDIF
 522  B342 CD 40 F4             call draw_dots
 523  B345                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 524  B345 ~                    call vtick
 525  B345                  ENDIF
 526  B345 CD 92 EE             call draw_city                  ; also calls vtick once per building
 527  B348 CD F0 F4             call draw_bullets               ; machine gun tracers
 528  B34B CD FF F5             call draw_particles             ; zombie bursts
 529  B34E CD 34 F7             call draw_gunsight
 530  B351 ED 91 57 02          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_HUD       ; ULA HUD code at $E000
 531  B355 CD 4B E2             call draw_hud
 532  B358                  IFDEF WAIT_FOR_VBLANK
 533  B358 ~                    call wait_vblank
 534  B358                  ENDIF
 535  B358 CD A2 B3             call l2_flip
 536  B35B                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 537  B35B ~                    call fps_tick                   ; HUD page is still at $E000
 538  B35B                  ENDIF
 539  B35B C3 0D B3             jp main_loop
 540  B35E
 541  B35E              ;==============================================================================
 542  B35E              ; Frames per second, shown on the ULA layer above Layer 2.
 543  B35E              ;
 544  B35E              ; Interrupts stay off (the span filler borrows SP), so time is taken from the
 545  B35E              ; raster: vtick reads the current video line and counts a new 50Hz video
 546  B35E              ; frame whenever the line number has gone backwards.  It must be called at
 547  B35E              ; least once per video frame (~560K T at 28MHz); the main loop and draw_cell
 548  B35E              ; do that.  Every 50 video frames (1 second) the number of frames rendered
 549  B35E              ; becomes the FPS figure.
 550  B35E              ;==============================================================================
 551  B35E              vtick:
 552  B35E 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 553  B361 3E 1E                ld a,$1E
 554  B363 ED 79                out (c),a
 555  B365 04                   inc b
 556  B366 ED 50                in d,(c)                        ; line MSB
 557  B368 05                   dec b
 558  B369 3E 1F                ld a,$1F
 559  B36B ED 79                out (c),a
 560  B36D 04                   inc b
 561  B36E ED 58                in e,(c)                        ; line LSB
 562  B370 05                   dec b
 563  B371 3E 1E                ld a,$1E
 564  B373 ED 79                out (c),a
 565  B375 04                   inc b
 566  B376 ED 78                in a,(c)
 567  B378 BA                   cp d
 568  B379 20 E3                jr nz,vtick                     ; MSB changed mid-read: try again
 569  B37B E6 01                and 1
 570  B37D 57                   ld d,a                          ; DE = video line 0..311
 571  B37E 2A D8 BA             ld hl,(fps_line)
 572  B381 ED 53 D8 BA          ld (fps_line),de
 573  B385 B7                   or a
 574  B386 ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; previous - current
 575  B388 D8                   ret c
 576  B389 C8                   ret z
 577  B38A 21 DA BA             ld hl,fps_vframes               ; line went backwards: new frame
 578  B38D 34                   inc (hl)
 579  B38E C9                   ret
 580  B38F
 581  B38F                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 582  B38F ~            ; Once per rendered frame, after the flip
 583  B38F ~            fps_tick:
 584  B38F ~                    call vtick
 585  B38F ~                    ld hl,fps_count
 586  B38F ~                    inc (hl)
 587  B38F ~                    ld a,(fps_vframes)
 588  B38F ~                    cp 50
 589  B38F ~                    ret c
 590  B38F ~                    sub 50
 591  B38F ~                    ld (fps_vframes),a
 592  B38F ~                    ld a,(fps_count)
 593  B38F ~                    ld (fps_value),a
 594  B38F ~                    xor a
 595  B38F ~                    ld (fps_count),a
 596  B38F ~                    jp fps_draw                     ; HUD page (paged in by main_loop)
 597  B38F                  ENDIF
 598  B38F
 599  B38F              ; A = NextReg number -> A = its value
 600  B38F              read_nextreg:
 601  B38F 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 602  B392 ED 79                out (c),a
 603  B394 04                   inc b
 604  B395 ED 78                in a,(c)
 605  B397 C9                   ret
 606  B398
 607  B398              ; Select the shadow Layer 2 buffer for drawing: remember the 8K page of its
 608  B398              ; first row.  Sections are then paged in on demand by seg_map / line_seg.
 609  B398              map_shadow:
 610  B398 3E 13                ld a,$13
 611  B39A CD 8F B3             call read_nextreg
 612  B39D 87                   add a,a                         ; 16K bank -> 8K page
 613  B39E 32 D7 BA             ld (seg_base),a
 614  B3A1 C9                   ret
 615  B3A2
 616  B3A2              ; Show the shadow buffer and make the old active buffer the new shadow
 617  B3A2              l2_flip:
 618  B3A2 3E 12                ld a,$12
 619  B3A4 CD 8F B3             call read_nextreg
 620  B3A7 5F                   ld e,a                          ; old active
 621  B3A8 3E 13                ld a,$13
 622  B3AA CD 8F B3             call read_nextreg
 623  B3AD ED 92 12             nextreg $12,a                   ; shadow -> active
 624  B3B0 7B                   ld a,e
 625  B3B1 ED 92 13             nextreg $13,a                   ; old active -> shadow
 626  B3B4 C9                   ret
 627  B3B5
 628  B3B5              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 629  B3B5              ; Layer 2 writes go through MMU slot 0 ($0000-$1FFF) only.  Each 8K page of
 630  B3B5              ; the buffer is a 32-row section, so row y is page seg_base + y/32 and its
 631  B3B5              ; pixels are at (y & 31)*256 + x.  Sections are paged in as drawing reaches
 632  B3B5              ; them.  Slots 4-5 keep the ULA screen.
 633  B3B5              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 634  B3B5
 635  B3B5              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 636  B3B5              ; Wait until the raster is outside the display area (line >= 192)
 637  B3B5              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 638  B3B5              wait_vblank:
 639  B3B5 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 640  B3B8 3E 1E                ld a,$1E
 641  B3BA ED 79                out (c),a
 642  B3BC 04                   inc b
 643  B3BD ED 78                in a,(c)
 644  B3BF E6 01                and 1
 645  B3C1 C0                   ret nz                          ; line >= 256
 646  B3C2 05                   dec b
 647  B3C3 3E 1F                ld a,$1F
 648  B3C5 ED 79                out (c),a
 649  B3C7 04                   inc b
 650  B3C8 ED 78                in a,(c)
 651  B3CA FE C0                cp 192
 652  B3CC 38 E7                jr c,wait_vblank
 653  B3CE C9                   ret
 654  B3CF
 655  B3CF              ; HL -> map byte for the cell the camera is in
 656  B3CF              cam_cell_ptr:
 657  B3CF 3A 3C B6             ld a,(cam_z+2)
 658  B3D2 6F                   ld l,a
 659  B3D3 26 00                ld h,0
 660  B3D5                      ROWOFS
 660  B3D5 29          >        add hl,hl
 660  B3D6 29          >        add hl,hl
 660  B3D7 29          >        add hl,hl
 660  B3D8 29          >        add hl,hl
 660  B3D9 29          >        add hl,hl
 660  B3DA 29          >        add hl,hl
 661  B3DB 3A 39 B6             ld a,(cam_x+2)
 662  B3DE ED 31                add hl,a
 663  B3E0 11 55 A0             ld de,city_map
 664  B3E3 19                   add hl,de
 665  B3E4 C9                   ret
 666  B3E5
 667  B3E5              ; HL = the height the VTOL rests at: MIN_Y when its whole footprint is over
 668  B3E5              ; road, or just clear (COLL_H) of the highest roof under it
 669  B3E5              floor_y:
 670  B3E5 CD F4 B3             call vtol_ground
 671  B3E8 21 08 00             ld hl,MIN_Y
 672  B3EB C8                   ret z
 673  B3EC 2A 61 B6             ld hl,(vg_max)
 674  B3EF 11 0D 00             ld de,COLL_H+1
 675  B3F2 19                   add hl,de
 676  B3F3 C9                   ret
 677  B3F4
 678  B3F4              ; The VTOL's footprint: the corners and the long-side midpoints of its
 679  B3F4              ; VTOL_HL x VTOL_HW (half size) rectangle, turned to the heading, round
 680  B3F4              ; (cam_x, cam_z).  The rectangle is smaller than a tile, so these six points
 681  B3F4              ; find every cell it overlaps (to within a tile corner nicking a long side).
 682  B3F4              ; HL = vg_max = the highest ground under them; Z if all road (0).
 683  B3F4              vtol_ground:
 684  B3F4 3A 40 B6             ld a,(yaw)                      ; forward (sin, cos), right (cos, -sin)
 685  B3F7 CD 47 B5             call get_sin
 686  B3FA 22 55 B6             ld (vg_sin),hl
 687  B3FD 3A 40 B6             ld a,(yaw)
 688  B400 C6 40                add a,64
 689  B402 CD 47 B5             call get_sin
 690  B405 22 57 B6             ld (vg_cos),hl
 691  B408 EB                   ex de,hl
 692  B409 21 40 00             ld hl,VTOL_HL
 693  B40C CD FE B5             call fmul
 694  B40F 22 5B B6             ld (vg_fz),hl                   ; nose offset z = HL cos
 695  B412 ED 5B 57 B6          ld de,(vg_cos)
 696  B416 21 13 00             ld hl,VTOL_HW
 697  B419 CD FE B5             call fmul
 698  B41C 22 5D B6             ld (vg_rx),hl                   ; side offset x = HW cos
 699  B41F ED 5B 55 B6          ld de,(vg_sin)
 700  B423 21 40 00             ld hl,VTOL_HL
 701  B426 CD FE B5             call fmul
 702  B429 22 59 B6             ld (vg_fx),hl                   ; nose offset x = HL sin
 703  B42C ED 5B 55 B6          ld de,(vg_sin)
 704  B430 21 13 00             ld hl,VTOL_HW
 705  B433 CD FE B5             call fmul
 706  B436 CD F2 B5             call neg_hl
 707  B439 22 5F B6             ld (vg_rz),hl                   ; side offset z = -HW sin
 708  B43C 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 709  B43F 22 61 B6             ld (vg_max),hl
 710  B442 2A 59 B6             ld hl,(vg_fx)                   ; nose: the front corners
 711  B445 ED 5B 5B B6          ld de,(vg_fz)
 712  B449 CD 6D B4             call .side
 713  B44C 21 00 00             ld hl,0                         ; the long-side midpoints
 714  B44F 11 00 00             ld de,0
 715  B452 CD 6D B4             call .side
 716  B455 2A 59 B6             ld hl,(vg_fx)                   ; tail: the back corners
 717  B458 CD F2 B5             call neg_hl
 718  B45B E5                   push hl
 719  B45C 2A 5B B6             ld hl,(vg_fz)
 720  B45F CD F2 B5             call neg_hl
 721  B462 EB                   ex de,hl
 722  B463 E1                   pop hl
 723  B464 CD 6D B4             call .side
 724  B467 2A 61 B6             ld hl,(vg_max)
 725  B46A 7C                   ld a,h
 726  B46B B5                   or l
 727  B46C C9                   ret
 728  B46D              ; HL, DE = an offset along the length (x, z): the points it +- the side offset
 729  B46D E5           .side:  push hl
 730  B46E D5                   push de
 731  B46F ED 4B 5D B6          ld bc,(vg_rx)
 732  B473 09                   add hl,bc
 733  B474 EB                   ex de,hl
 734  B475 ED 4B 5F B6          ld bc,(vg_rz)
 735  B479 09                   add hl,bc
 736  B47A EB                   ex de,hl
 737  B47B CD 90 B4             call .pt
 738  B47E D1                   pop de
 739  B47F E1                   pop hl
 740  B480 ED 4B 5D B6          ld bc,(vg_rx)
 741  B484 B7                   or a
 742  B485 ED 42                sbc hl,bc
 743  B487 EB                   ex de,hl
 744  B488 ED 4B 5F B6          ld bc,(vg_rz)
 745  B48C B7                   or a
 746  B48D ED 42                sbc hl,bc
 747  B48F EB                   ex de,hl
 748  B490              ; HL, DE = offset (x, z) from the centre: vg_max = max(vg_max, its ground)
 749  B490 ED 4B 38 B6  .pt:    ld bc,(cam_x+1)
 750  B494 09                   add hl,bc
 751  B495 7C                   ld a,h                          ; column
 752  B496 EB                   ex de,hl
 753  B497 ED 4B 3B B6          ld bc,(cam_z+1)
 754  B49B 09                   add hl,bc
 755  B49C 6C                   ld l,h                          ; row
 756  B49D 26 00                ld h,0
 757  B49F                      ROWOFS
 757  B49F 29          >        add hl,hl
 757  B4A0 29          >        add hl,hl
 757  B4A1 29          >        add hl,hl
 757  B4A2 29          >        add hl,hl
 757  B4A3 29          >        add hl,hl
 757  B4A4 29          >        add hl,hl
 758  B4A5 ED 31                add hl,a
 759  B4A7 11 55 A0             ld de,city_map
 760  B4AA 19                   add hl,de
 761  B4AB 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 762  B4AC B7                   or a
 763  B4AD C8                   ret z                           ; road
 764  B4AE CD BE B4             call bld_height
 765  B4B1 ED 5B 61 B6          ld de,(vg_max)
 766  B4B5 B7                   or a
 767  B4B6 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 768  B4B8 D8                   ret c                           ; lower than the highest so far
 769  B4B9 19                   add hl,de
 770  B4BA 22 61 B6             ld (vg_max),hl
 771  B4BD C9                   ret
 772  B4BE
 773  B4BE              ; A = map byte -> HL = height in world units (levels 0-15, PAD_CODE = the
 774  B4BE              ; base landing pad, TERRACE_CODE = the terrace round it)
 775  B4BE              bld_height:
 776  B4BE E6 1F                and 31
 777  B4C0 87                   add a,a
 778  B4C1 21 31 A0             ld hl,htab
 779  B4C4 ED 31                add hl,a
 780  B4C6 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 781  B4C7 23                   inc hl
 782  B4C8 66                   ld h,(hl)
 783  B4C9 6F                   ld l,a
 784  B4CA C9                   ret
 785  B4CB
 786  B4CB              ;==============================================================================
 787  B4CB              ; Per-frame view setup: trig, rotated building edge vectors, horizon
 788  B4CB              ;==============================================================================
 789  B4CB              setup_frame:
 790  B4CB 3A 40 B6             ld a,(yaw)
 791  B4CE CD 47 B5             call get_sin
 792  B4D1 22 B1 BA             ld (sin_y),hl
 793  B4D4 3A 40 B6             ld a,(yaw)
 794  B4D7 C6 40                add a,64
 795  B4D9 CD 47 B5             call get_sin
 796  B4DC 22 B3 BA             ld (cos_y),hl
 797  B4DF 3A 42 B6             ld a,(pitch)
 798  B4E2 CD 47 B5             call get_sin
 799  B4E5 22 B5 BA             ld (sin_p),hl
 800  B4E8 3A 42 B6             ld a,(pitch)
 801  B4EB C6 40                add a,64
 802  B4ED CD 47 B5             call get_sin
 803  B4F0 22 B7 BA             ld (cos_p),hl
 804  B4F3
 805  B4F3                      ; ground plane relative to camera: dy = -camY
 806  B4F3 2A 3D B6             ld hl,(cam_y)
 807  B4F6 CD F2 B5             call neg_hl
 808  B4F9 E5                   push hl
 809  B4FA ED 5B B7 BA          ld de,(cos_p)
 810  B4FE CD FE B5             call fmul
 811  B501 22 B9 BA             ld (yb),hl                      ; Y contribution of ground
 812  B504 E1                   pop hl
 813  B505 ED 5B B5 BA          ld de,(sin_p)
 814  B509 CD FE B5             call fmul
 815  B50C 22 BB BA             ld (zb),hl                      ; Z contribution of ground
 816  B50F
 817  B50F                      ; frustum cull factor c = cosP - 0.5|sinP|
 818  B50F 2A B5 BA             ld hl,(sin_p)
 819  B512 CD F9 B5             call abs_hl
 820  B515 CD 32 B6             call zhalf
 821  B518 EB                   ex de,hl
 822  B519 2A B7 BA             ld hl,(cos_p)
 823  B51C B7                   or a
 824  B51D ED 52                sbc hl,de
 825  B51F 22 CD BA             ld (cull_c),hl
 826  B522
 827  B522 21 00 01             ld hl,BLD_SIZE
 828  B525 DD 21 BD BA          ld ix,vecs0
 829  B529 CD 53 B5             call make_vecs
 830  B52C
 831  B52C 3A 39 B6             ld a,(cam_x+2)
 832  B52F 32 D5 BA             ld (cx),a
 833  B532 3A 3C B6             ld a,(cam_z+2)
 834  B535 32 D6 BA             ld (cz),a
 835  B538
 836  B538 3A 42 B6             ld a,(pitch)
 837  B53B C6 18                add a,PMAX
 838  B53D 21 00 A0             ld hl,hztab
 839  B540 ED 31                add hl,a
 840  B542 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 841  B543 32 B0 BA             ld (horizon),a
 842  B546 C9                   ret
 843  B547
 844  B547              ; A = angle -> HL = sin (S7.8).  Pages the sine table into slot 0 (PAGE_SIN):
 845  B547              ; call it only outside the Layer 2 drawing (it is: setup_frame, the player,
 846  B547              ; vtol_ground), never between a section's paging and its writes.
 847  B547              get_sin:
 848  B547 ED 91 50 0E          nextreg SIN_MMU,PAGE_SIN        ; the table at $0000 (slot 0)
 849  B54B 6F                   ld l,a
 850  B54C 26 00                ld h,high sin_lo
 851  B54E 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 852  B54F 24                   inc h
 853  B550 66                   ld h,(hl)
 854  B551 6F                   ld l,a
 855  B552 C9                   ret
 856  B553
 857  B553              ; Rotate the building edge vectors (s,0,0) and (0,0,s) into camera space.
 858  B553              ; HL = s, IX -> 16 bytes: VX(X,Y,Z) VZ(X,Y,Z) corner offset (X,Z1)
 859  B553              make_vecs:
 860  B553 22 CF BA             ld (mv_s),hl
 861  B556 ED 5B B3 BA          ld de,(cos_y)
 862  B55A CD FE B5             call fmul
 863  B55D 22 D1 BA             ld (mv_c),hl                    ; s*cosY
 864  B560 2A CF BA             ld hl,(mv_s)
 865  B563 ED 5B B1 BA          ld de,(sin_y)
 866  B567 CD FE B5             call fmul
 867  B56A 22 D3 BA             ld (mv_n),hl                    ; s*sinY
 868  B56D                      ; VX = ( s cosY, -s sinY sinP, s sinY cosP )
 869  B56D 2A D1 BA             ld hl,(mv_c)
 870  B570 DD 75 00             ld (ix+0),l
 871  B573 DD 74 01             ld (ix+1),h
 872  B576 2A D3 BA             ld hl,(mv_n)
 873  B579 ED 5B B5 BA          ld de,(sin_p)
 874  B57D CD FE B5             call fmul
 875  B580 CD F2 B5             call neg_hl
 876  B583 DD 75 02             ld (ix+2),l
 877  B586 DD 74 03             ld (ix+3),h
 878  B589 2A D3 BA             ld hl,(mv_n)
 879  B58C ED 5B B7 BA          ld de,(cos_p)
 880  B590 CD FE B5             call fmul
 881  B593 DD 75 04             ld (ix+4),l
 882  B596 DD 74 05             ld (ix+5),h
 883  B599                      ; VZ = ( -s sinY, -s cosY sinP, s cosY cosP )
 884  B599 2A D3 BA             ld hl,(mv_n)
 885  B59C CD F2 B5             call neg_hl
 886  B59F DD 75 06             ld (ix+6),l
 887  B5A2 DD 74 07             ld (ix+7),h
 888  B5A5 2A D1 BA             ld hl,(mv_c)
 889  B5A8 ED 5B B5 BA          ld de,(sin_p)
 890  B5AC CD FE B5             call fmul
 891  B5AF CD F2 B5             call neg_hl
 892  B5B2 DD 75 08             ld (ix+8),l
 893  B5B5 DD 74 09             ld (ix+9),h
 894  B5B8 2A D1 BA             ld hl,(mv_c)
 895  B5BB ED 5B B7 BA          ld de,(cos_p)
 896  B5BF CD FE B5             call fmul
 897  B5C2 DD 75 0A             ld (ix+10),l
 898  B5C5 DD 74 0B             ld (ix+11),h
 899  B5C8                      ; cell centre -> footprint corner (-s/2,-s/2) in yaw space
 900  B5C8 2A D3 BA             ld hl,(mv_n)
 901  B5CB ED 5B D1 BA          ld de,(mv_c)
 902  B5CF B7                   or a
 903  B5D0 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 904  B5D2 CB 2C                sra h
 905  B5D4 CB 1D                rr l
 906  B5D6 DD 75 0C             ld (ix+12),l                    ; X: s/2 (sinY - cosY)
 907  B5D9 DD 74 0D             ld (ix+13),h
 908  B5DC 2A D3 BA             ld hl,(mv_n)
 909  B5DF ED 5B D1 BA          ld de,(mv_c)
 910  B5E3 19                   add hl,de
 911  B5E4 CB 2C                sra h
 912  B5E6 CB 1D                rr l
 913  B5E8 CD F2 B5             call neg_hl
 914  B5EB DD 75 0E             ld (ix+14),l                    ; Z1: -s/2 (sinY + cosY)
 915  B5EE DD 74 0F             ld (ix+15),h
 916  B5F1 C9                   ret
 917  B5F2
 918  B5F2              ;==============================================================================
 919  B5F2              ; Fixed point maths
 920  B5F2              ;==============================================================================
 921  B5F2
 922  B5F2              ; HL = -HL
 923  B5F2              neg_hl:
 924  B5F2 AF                   xor a
 925  B5F3 95                   sub l
 926  B5F4 6F                   ld l,a
 927  B5F5 9F                   sbc a,a
 928  B5F6 94                   sub h
 929  B5F7 67                   ld h,a
 930  B5F8 C9                   ret
 931  B5F9
 932  B5F9              ; HL = |HL|
 933  B5F9              abs_hl:
 934  B5F9 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 935  B5FB C8                   ret z
 936  B5FC 18 F4                jr neg_hl
 937  B5FE
 938  B5FE              ; fmul: HL = HL * DE >> 8
 939  B5FE              ;   HL = signed 16-bit integer, DE = S7.8 factor in -1.0..+1.0
 940  B5FE              ;   Sign/magnitude using two 8x8 MUL D,E.  Preserves IX, IY.
 941  B5FE              fmul:
 942  B5FE 7C                   ld a,h
 943  B5FF AA                   xor d
 944  B600 F5                   push af                         ; result sign in bit 7
 945  B601 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 946  B603 28 06                jr z,.ap
 947  B605 AF                   xor a
 948  B606 95                   sub l
 949  B607 6F                   ld l,a
 950  B608 9F                   sbc a,a
 951  B609 94                   sub h
 952  B60A 67                   ld h,a
 953  B60B CB 7A        .ap:    bit 7,d
 954  B60D 28 06                jr z,.bp
 955  B60F AF                   xor a
 956  B610 93                   sub e
 957  B611 5F                   ld e,a
 958  B612 9F                   sbc a,a
 959  B613 92                   sub d
 960  B614 57                   ld d,a
 961  B615 7A           .bp:    ld a,d
 962  B616 B7                   or a
 963  B617 20 10                jr nz,.one                      ; |factor| == 1.0
 964  B619 7B                   ld a,e
 965  B61A 55                   ld d,l
 966  B61B ED 30                mul d,e                         ; lo * f
 967  B61D 4A                   ld c,d
 968  B61E 54                   ld d,h
 969  B61F 5F                   ld e,a
 970  B620 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; hi * f
 971  B622 7B                   ld a,e
 972  B623 81                   add a,c
 973  B624 6F                   ld l,a
 974  B625 7A                   ld a,d
 975  B626 CE 00                adc a,0
 976  B628 67                   ld h,a
 977  B629 F1           .one:   pop af
 978  B62A F0                   ret p
 979  B62B AF                   xor a
 980  B62C 95                   sub l
 981  B62D 6F                   ld l,a
 982  B62E 9F                   sbc a,a
 983  B62F 94                   sub h
 984  B630 67                   ld h,a
 985  B631 C9                   ret
 986  B632
 987  B632              ; HL = 0.5 * HL (signed): the top/bottom frustum slope, VIEW_CY/128
 988  B632              zhalf:
 989  B632 CB 2C                sra h
 990  B634 CB 1D                rr l
 991  B636 C9                   ret
 992  B637
 993  B637              ;==============================================================================
 994  B637              ; Shared data (resident: used by the main code and by both pages)
 995  B637              ;==============================================================================
 996  B637              ; --- player state ---
 997  B637              ; (cam_x .. wflash_t are set from start_state by newGame)
 998  B637 00 00 00     cam_x:      ds 3                        ; frac, integer lo, hi
 999  B63A 00 00 00     cam_z:      ds 3
1000  B63D 00 00        cam_y:      ds 2
1001  B63F 00           yaw_frac:   ds 1                        ; fraction below yaw (keep together:
1002  B640 00           yaw:        ds 1                        ; yaw_frac/yaw is one 8.8 word)
1003  B641 00           pitch_frac: ds 1                        ; fraction below pitch (keep together:
1004  B642 00           pitch:      ds 1                        ; pitch_frac/pitch is one signed 8.8
1005  B643                                                      ; word; drawing uses pitch only)
1006  B643 00           speed:      ds 1
1007  B644              ; weapon slots: type, ammo (AMMO_INF = 255 = infinite).  Address of slot n
1008  B644              ; is wpn_slots + n*2.
1009  B644 00 00 00...  wpn_slots:  ds WPN_SLOTS*2
1010  B64E 00           wpn_sel:    ds 1                        ; selected slot (keys 1-5), fires on SPACE
1011  B64F 00           wflash_slot: ds 1                       ; empty slot requested: which, and
1012  B650 00           wflash_t:   ds 1                        ; frames of yellow/blue flash left
1013  B651 14           level:      db START_LEVEL              ; current level (newGame: START_LEVEL)
1014  B652 00           game_over:  db 0                        ; 1: the landing pad was destroyed
1015  B653 00 00        salvage_points: dw 0                    ; salvage points, 16-bit (newGame: 0)
1016  B655 00 00        vg_sin:     dw 0                        ; vtol_ground: heading sin, cos,
1017  B657 00 00        vg_cos:     dw 0
1018  B659 00 00        vg_fx:      dw 0                        ; nose offset (x, z),
1019  B65B 00 00        vg_fz:      dw 0
1020  B65D 00 00        vg_rx:      dw 0                        ; side offset (x, z),
1021  B65F 00 00        vg_rz:      dw 0
1022  B661 00 00        vg_max:     dw 0                        ; and the highest ground found
1023  B663
1024  B663              ; The start of a game: copied over cam_x .. wflash_t by newGame
1025  B663              start_state:
1026  B663 00 80 20                 db 0, $80, PAD_X            ; cam_x: landed on the base pad
1027  B666 00 80 20                 db 0, $80, PAD_Z            ; cam_z  (gentables.py)
1028  B669 4D 00                    dw PAD_H+COLL_H+1           ; cam_y: resting on it
1029  B66B 00 00                    db 0, BASE_YAW              ; yaw: facing the city centre
1030  B66D 00 00                    db 0, 0                     ; pitch: level
1031  B66F 00                       db 0                        ; speed
1032  B670 01 FF                    db WPN_GUN, AMMO_INF        ; slot 0: centre gun, infinite
1033  B672 04 0C                    db WPN_ROCKETS, ROCKET_SHOTS  ; 1: left outer pylon: rocket pod
1034  B674 00 00                    db WPN_NONE, 0              ; 2: left inner pylon
1035  B676 00 00                    db WPN_NONE, 0              ; 3: right inner pylon
1036  B678 00 00                    db WPN_NONE, 0              ; 4: right outer pylon
1037  B67A 00                       db WPN_CENTRE               ; wpn_sel
1038  B67B 00 00                    db 0, 0                     ; wflash_slot, wflash_t
1039  B67D              start_state_end:
1040  B67D                          ASSERT start_state_end - start_state == wflash_t + 1 - cam_x
1041  B67D 00 00 00...  bullets:    ds NBULLETS*BREC            ; machine gun rounds (weapons.asm)
1042  B905 00 00 00...  laser_rec:  ds BREC                     ; laser beam, drawn like a round
1043  B920                                                      ; (state 3); must follow bullets
1044  B920 00 00 00...  particles:  ds NPART*PREC               ; zombie burst particles (must follow
1045  BAB0                                                      ; laser_rec: newGame clears them all)
1046  BAB0
1047  BAB0              ; --- view, set up each frame by setup_frame ---
1048  BAB0 40           horizon:    db VIEW_CY
1049  BAB1 00 00        sin_y:      dw 0
1050  BAB3 00 00        cos_y:      dw 0
1051  BAB5 00 00        sin_p:      dw 0
1052  BAB7 00 00        cos_p:      dw 0
1053  BAB9 00 00        yb:         dw 0
1054  BABB 00 00        zb:         dw 0
1055  BABD 00 00 00...  vecs0:      ds 16
1056  BACD 00 00        cull_c:     dw 0
1057  BACF 00 00        mv_s:       dw 0
1058  BAD1 00 00        mv_c:       dw 0
1059  BAD3 00 00        mv_n:       dw 0
1060  BAD5 00           cx:         db 0
1061  BAD6 00           cz:         db 0
1062  BAD7 00           seg_base:   db 0                        ; 8K page of row 0 of the draw buffer
1063  BAD8
1064  BAD8              ; --- fps ---
1065  BAD8 00 00        fps_line:   dw 0
1066  BADA 00           fps_vframes: db 0
1067  BADB 00           fps_count:  db 0
1068  BADC 00           fps_value:  db 0
1069  BADD
1070  BADD
1071  BADD 00 00 00...              ds 256                      ; stack (must stay resident)
1072  BBDD              stack_top:
1073  BBDD
1074  BBDD                      DISPLAY "Main end:  ",/A,$
1075  BBDD                      ASSERT $ <= MAIN_ADDR+MEMBANK_SIZE
1076  BBDD
1077  BBDD              ;==============================================================================
1078  BBDD              ; Core function pages: assembled at $E000, swapped into slot 7
1079  BBDD              ;==============================================================================
1080  BBDD                      MMU CODE_SLOT e, PAGE_CITY, CODE_ADDR
1081  E000                      ORG CODE_ADDR

   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - city page (8K page PAGE_CITY, runs at $E000)

  42+ E720              ; A = row y (0..VIEW_H-1): page in its section, return A = y & 31.
  43+ E720              ; Preserves BC, DE, L.
  44+ E720              seg_map:
  45+ E720 C5                   push bc

  60+ E737
  61+ E737              ; Line drawer stepped H off the section (H = 32 going down, $FF going up):
  62+ E737              ; page in the neighbouring section and wrap H.  Preserves A, BC, DE, L.
  63+ E737              line_seg:
  64+ E737 F5                   push af
  65+ E738 7C                   ld a,h
  66+ E739 FE 20                cp 32
  67+ E73B 3A 69 FC             ld a,(seg_page)
  68+ E73E 20 05                jr nz,.up
  69+ E740 3C                   inc a                           ; down into the next section
  70+ E741 26 00                ld h,0
  71+ E743 18 03                jr .set
  72+ E745 3D           .up:    dec a                           ; up into the previous section
  73+ E746 26 1F                ld h,31
  74+ E748 32 69 FC     .set:   ld (seg_page),a
  75+ E74B ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a
  76+ E74E F1                   pop af
  77+ E74F C9                   ret
