 100+ E76B D5                   push de
 101+ E76C 50                   ld d,b
 102+ E76D ED 30                mul d,e                         ; ah*tl
 103+ E76F 19                   add hl,de
 104+ E770 CE 00                adc a,0                         ; A:HL = middle sum
 105+ E772 6C                   ld l,h
 106+ E773 67                   ld h,a
 107+ E774 D1                   pop de
 108+ E775 5A                   ld e,d
 109+ E776 50                   ld d,b
 110+ E777 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; ah*th
 111+ E779 19                   add hl,de
 112+ E77A 3A 80 FB             ld a,(mf_sign)
 113+ E77D CB 7F                bit 7,a
 114+ E77F C8                   ret z
 115+ E780 C3 F2 B5             jp neg_hl
 116+ E783
 117+ E783              ; IX = (HL << 16) / DE   with HL < DE (unsigned) -> 0.16 fraction
 118+ E783              div_frac:
 119+ E783 DD 21 00 00          ld ix,0
 120+ E787 3E 10                ld a,16
 121+ E789 DD 29        .lp:    add ix,ix
 122+ E78B 29                   add hl,hl
 123+ E78C 38 08                jr c,.sub
 124+ E78E B7                   or a
 125+ E78F ED 52                sbc hl,de
 126+ E791 30 06                jr nc,.set
 127+ E793 19                   add hl,de
 128+ E794 18 05                jr .nx
 129+ E796 B7           .sub:   or a
 130+ E797 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 131+ E799 DD 23        .set:   inc ix
 132+ E79B 3D           .nx:    dec a
 133+ E79C 20 EB                jr nz,.lp
 134+ E79E C9                   ret
 135+ E79F
 136+ E79F              ; E = A / C, A = A mod C  (8-bit unsigned)
 137+ E79F              div8:
 138+ E79F 5F                   ld e,a
 139+ E7A0 AF                   xor a
 140+ E7A1 06 08                ld b,8
 141+ E7A3 CB 23        .lp:    sla e
 142+ E7A5 17                   rla
 143+ E7A6 38 03                jr c,.s
 144+ E7A8 B9                   cp c
 145+ E7A9 38 02                jr c,.n
 146+ E7AB 91           .s:     sub c
 147+ E7AC 1C                   inc e
 148+ E7AD 10 F4        .n:     djnz .lp
 149+ E7AF C9                   ret
 150+ E7B0
 151+ E7B0              ;==============================================================================
 152+ E7B0              ; Perspective projection
 153+ E7B0              ;   in : HL -> X,Y,Z (camera space, inside the view frustum, Z > 0)
 154+ E7B0              ;   out: A = screen x (0..255), C = screen y (0..VIEW_H-1)
 155+ E7B0              ;   sx = 128 + 128*X/Z,  sy = 64 - 128*Y/Z   (90 x 53 degree field of view)
 156+ E7B0              ;   Z is normalised to 9 bits (256..511) with a barrel shift (count from a
 157+ E7B0              ;   log2 table) that is also applied to X and Y, which are then multiplied
 158+ E7B0              ;   by a 16-bit reciprocal from rtab.  |X| <= Z keeps Xn within 9 bits.
 159+ E7B0              ;==============================================================================
 160+ E7B0              project:
 161+ E7B0 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 162+ E7B1 23                   inc hl
 163+ E7B2 46                   ld b,(hl)
 164+ E7B3 23                   inc hl                          ; BC = X
 165+ E7B4 5E                   ld e,(hl)
 166+ E7B5 23                   inc hl
 167+ E7B6 56                   ld d,(hl)
 168+ E7B7 23                   inc hl                          ; DE = Y
 169+ E7B8 D5                   push de
 170+ E7B9 C5                   push bc
 171+ E7BA 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 172+ E7BB 23                   inc hl
 173+ E7BC 66                   ld h,(hl)
 174+ E7BD 6F                   ld l,a                          ; HL = Z
 175+ E7BE CD 01 E8             call zpatch
 176+ E7C1
 177+ E7C1 E1                   pop hl                          ; X
 178+ E7C2 7C                   ld a,h
 179+ E7C3 F5                   push af
 180+ E7C4 CD 45 E8             call scale_comp
 181+ E7C7 4F                   ld c,a
 182+ E7C8 F1                   pop af
 183+ E7C9 17                   rla
 184+ E7CA 38 09                jr c,.xn
 185+ E7CC 3E 80                ld a,128
 186+ E7CE 81                   add a,c
 187+ E7CF 30 0A                jr nc,.xd
 188+ E7D1 3E FF                ld a,255
 189+ E7D3 18 06                jr .xd
 190+ E7D5 3E 80        .xn:    ld a,128
 191+ E7D7 91                   sub c
 192+ E7D8 30 01                jr nc,.xd
 193+ E7DA AF                   xor a
 194+ E7DB 32 7F FB     .xd:    ld (pj_sx),a
 195+ E7DE
 196+ E7DE E1                   pop hl                          ; Y
 197+ E7DF                      ; fall through
 198+ E7DF
 199+ E7DF              ; HL = Y -> C = screen y, A = screen x of the last project.  Uses the Z
 200+ E7DF              ; patched into scale_comp by the last project, so on its own it projects a
 201+ E7DF              ; point straight above or below that one when pitch is 0 (same X and Z).
 202+ E7DF              proj_y:
 203+ E7DF 7C                   ld a,h
 204+ E7E0 F5                   push af
 205+ E7E1 CD 45 E8             call scale_comp
 206+ E7E4 4F                   ld c,a
 207+ E7E5 F1                   pop af
 208+ E7E6 17                   rla
 209+ E7E7 38 08                jr c,.yn
 210+ E7E9 3E 40                ld a,VIEW_CY
 211+ E7EB 91                   sub c
 212+ E7EC 30 0E                jr nc,.yd
 213+ E7EE AF                   xor a
 214+ E7EF 18 0B                jr .yd
 215+ E7F1 3E 40        .yn:    ld a,VIEW_CY
 216+ E7F3 81                   add a,c
 217+ E7F4 38 04                jr c,.yc
 218+ E7F6 FE 80                cp VIEW_H
 219+ E7F8 38 02                jr c,.yd
 220+ E7FA 3E 7F        .yc:    ld a,VIEW_H-1
 221+ E7FC 4F           .yd:    ld c,a
 222+ E7FD 3A 7F FB             ld a,(pj_sx)
 223+ E800 C9                   ret
 224+ E801
 225+ E801              ; HL = Z (camera space): normalise it and patch its shift and reciprocal
 226+ E801              ; into scale_comp, which then gives A = |component| * 128 / Z
 227+ E801              zpatch:
 228+ E801 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 229+ E803 20 03                jr nz,.z1
 230+ E805 B4                   or h
 231+ E806 20 03                jr nz,.zok
 232+ E808 21 01 00     .z1:    ld hl,1
 233+ E80B 7C           .zok:   ld a,h
 234+ E80C FE 02                cp 2
 235+ E80E 38 0B                jr c,.lt512
 236+ E810 5F                   ld e,a                          ; Z >= 512: shift right by log2(H)
 237+ E811 16 E6                ld d,high lgtab
 238+ E813 1A                   ld a,(de)
 239+ E814 47                   ld b,a
 240+ E815 EB                   ex de,hl
 241+ E816 ED 2A                bsrl de,b
 242+ E818 EB                   ex de,hl
 243+ E819 18 14                jr .nrm
 244+ E81B 06 00        .lt512: ld b,0
 245+ E81D B7                   or a
 246+ E81E 20 0F                jr nz,.nrm                      ; 256..511 already normalised
 247+ E820 5D                   ld e,l                          ; Z < 256: shift left by 8-log2(L)
 248+ E821 16 E6                ld d,high lgtab
 249+ E823 1A                   ld a,(de)
 250+ E824 D6 08                sub 8
 251+ E826 4F                   ld c,a                          ; negative shift count
 252+ E827 ED 44                neg
 253+ E829 47                   ld b,a
 254+ E82A EB                   ex de,hl
 255+ E82B ED 28                bsla de,b
 256+ E82D EB                   ex de,hl
 257+ E82E 41                   ld b,c
 258+ E82F 78           .nrm:   ld a,b                          ; patch shift and reciprocal into
 259+ E830 32 50 E8             ld (scale_comp.sh+1),a                  ; scale_comp's immediates
 260+ E833 26 E4                ld h,high rtab_lo               ; L = Zn - 256
 261+ E835 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 262+ E836 32 6C E8             ld (scale_comp.rl+1),a
 263+ E839 32 82 E8             ld (scale_comp.r16+1),a
 264+ E83C 24                   inc h
 265+ E83D 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 266+ E83E 32 72 E8             ld (scale_comp.rh+1),a
 267+ E841 32 83 E8             ld (scale_comp.r16+2),a
 268+ E844 C9                   ret
 269+ E845
 270+ E845              ; HL = signed component -> A = round(|HL| * 128 / Z)
 271+ E845              ; The shift and reciprocal for this Z are patched in by project.
 272+ E845              scale_comp:
 273+ E845 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 274+ E847 28 06                jr z,.ap
 275+ E849 AF                   xor a
 276+ E84A 95                   sub l
 277+ E84B 6F                   ld l,a
 278+ E84C 9F                   sbc a,a
 279+ E84D 94                   sub h
 280+ E84E 67                   ld h,a
 281+ E84F              .ap:
 282+ E84F 3E 00        .sh:    ld a,0
 283+ E851 B7                   or a
 284+ E852 28 10                jr z,.sd
 285+ E854 EB                   ex de,hl
 286+ E855 FA 5E E8             jp m,.l
 287+ E858 47                   ld b,a
 288+ E859 ED 2A                bsrl de,b
 289+ E85B EB                   ex de,hl
 290+ E85C 18 06                jr .sd
 291+ E85E ED 44        .l:     neg
 292+ E860 47                   ld b,a
 293+ E861 ED 28                bsla de,b
 294+ E863 EB                   ex de,hl
 295+ E864 7C           .sd:    ld a,h
 296+ E865 FE 02                cp 2
 297+ E867 30 27                jr nc,.big
 298+ E869 44           .ok:    ld b,h                          ; bit 8 of Xn
 299+ E86A 55                   ld d,l
 300+ E86B 1E 00        .rl:    ld e,0
 301+ E86D ED 30                mul d,e                         ; Xl*Rl
 302+ E86F 4A                   ld c,d
 303+ E870 55                   ld d,l
 304+ E871 1E 00        .rh:    ld e,0
 305+ E873 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; Xl*Rh
 306+ E875 79                   ld a,c
 307+ E876 83                   add a,e
 308+ E877 6F                   ld l,a
 309+ E878 7A                   ld a,d
 310+ E879 CE 00                adc a,0
 311+ E87B 67                   ld h,a                          ; HL = (Xl*R) >> 8
 312+ E87C 0E 00                ld c,0
 313+ E87E 05                   dec b
 314+ E87F 20 06                jr nz,.nh
 315+ E881 11 00 00     .r16:   ld de,0
 316+ E884 19                   add hl,de                       ; + (256*R) >> 8
 317+ E885 CB 11                rl c
 318+ E887 24           .nh:    inc h                           ; + rounding (0.5 after >>9)
 319+ E888 20 01                jr nz,.nr
 320+ E88A 0C                   inc c
 321+ E88B 79           .nr:    ld a,c
 322+ E88C 1F                   rra
 323+ E88D 7C                   ld a,h
 324+ E88E 1F                   rra                             ; A = (C:H) >> 1  = P >> 9
 325+ E88F C9                   ret
 326+ E890 21 FF 01     .big:   ld hl,511                       ; clamp (rounding at frustum edge)
 327+ E893 18 D4                jr .ok
 328+ E895
 329+ E895              ;==============================================================================
 330+ E895              ; Outcode: HL -> vertex (X,Y,Z) -> A = bits
 331+ E895              ;   1 near (Z<NEAR)  2 left (X<-Z)  4 right (X>Z)
 332+ E895              ;   8 top (Y>0.5Z)   16 bottom (Y<-0.5Z)
 333+ E895              ;==============================================================================
 334+ E895              calc_oc:
 335+ E895 5E                   ld e,(hl)
 336+ E896 23                   inc hl
 337+ E897 56                   ld d,(hl)                       ; DE = X
 338+ E898 23                   inc hl
 339+ E899 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 340+ E89A 23                   inc hl
 341+ E89B 46                   ld b,(hl)                       ; BC = Y
 342+ E89C 23                   inc hl
 343+ E89D 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 344+ E89E 23                   inc hl
 345+ E89F 66                   ld h,(hl)
 346+ E8A0 6F                   ld l,a                          ; HL = Z
 347+ E8A1 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 348+ E8A3 20 0D                jr nz,.nr
 349+ E8A5 7C                   ld a,h
 350+ E8A6 B7                   or a
 351+ E8A7 3E 00                ld a,0
 352+ E8A9 20 09                jr nz,.n1
 353+ E8AB 7D                   ld a,l
 354+ E8AC FE 10                cp NEAR
 355+ E8AE 3E 00                ld a,0
 356+ E8B0 30 02                jr nc,.n1
 357+ E8B2 3E 01        .nr:    ld a,1
 358+ E8B4 E5           .n1:    push hl
 359+ E8B5 19                   add hl,de                       ; Z+X
 360+ E8B6 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 361+ E8B8 28 02                jr z,.n2
 362+ E8BA F6 02                or 2
 363+ E8BC E1           .n2:    pop hl
 364+ E8BD E5                   push hl
 365+ E8BE B7                   or a
 366+ E8BF ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; Z-X
 367+ E8C1 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 368+ E8C3 28 02                jr z,.n3
 369+ E8C5 F6 04                or 4
 370+ E8C7 E1           .n3:    pop hl
 371+ E8C8 CB 2C                sra h
 372+ E8CA CB 1D                rr l                            ; 0.5Z
 373+ E8CC E5                   push hl
 374+ E8CD B7                   or a
 375+ E8CE ED 42                sbc hl,bc                       ; 0.5Z-Y
 376+ E8D0 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 377+ E8D2 28 02                jr z,.n4
 378+ E8D4 F6 08                or 8
 379+ E8D6 E1           .n4:    pop hl
 380+ E8D7 09                   add hl,bc                       ; 0.5Z+Y
 381+ E8D8 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 382+ E8DA C8                   ret z
 383+ E8DB F6 10                or 16
 384+ E8DD C9                   ret
 385+ E8DE
 386+ E8DE              ; Signed distance of vertex IX to clip plane (clip_plane bit). >= 0 is inside.
 387+ E8DE              plane_dist:
 388+ E8DE DD 6E 04             ld l,(ix+4)
 389+ E8E1 DD 66 05             ld h,(ix+5)
 390+ E8E4 3A 90 FB             ld a,(clip_plane)
 391+ E8E7 FE 01                cp 1
 392+ E8E9 20 05                jr nz,.p2
 393+ E8EB 11 F0 FF             ld de,-NEAR
 394+ E8EE 19                   add hl,de
 395+ E8EF C9                   ret
 396+ E8F0 DD 5E 00     .p2:    ld e,(ix+0)
 397+ E8F3 DD 56 01             ld d,(ix+1)
 398+ E8F6 FE 02                cp 2
 399+ E8F8 20 02                jr nz,.p3
 400+ E8FA 19                   add hl,de
 401+ E8FB C9                   ret
 402+ E8FC FE 04        .p3:    cp 4
 403+ E8FE 20 04                jr nz,.p4
 404+ E900 B7                   or a
 405+ E901 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 406+ E903 C9                   ret
 407+ E904 F5           .p4:    push af
 408+ E905 CD 32 B6             call zhalf
 409+ E908 F1                   pop af
 410+ E909 DD 5E 02             ld e,(ix+2)
 411+ E90C DD 56 03             ld d,(ix+3)
 412+ E90F FE 08                cp 8
 413+ E911 20 04                jr nz,.p5
 414+ E913 B7                   or a
 415+ E914 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 416+ E916 C9                   ret
 417+ E917 19           .p5:    add hl,de
 418+ E918 C9                   ret
 419+ E919
 420+ E919              ;==============================================================================
 421+ E919              ; Sutherland-Hodgman clip of clip_src (clip_n verts) against clip_plane into
 422+ E919              ; clip_dst.  Vertex = X,Y,Z,flag,corner (8 bytes).  flag=1 means the edge from
 423+ E919              ; this vertex to the next is a real building edge (gets outlined); edges
 424+ E919              ; created along a clip plane get flag=0 so the screen border isn't outlined.
 425+ E919              ; corner = vtx offset of an unclipped building corner that is on screen, so
 426+ E919              ; its cached projection can be reused; $80 + plane bit for a vertex the
 427+ E919              ; clipper put on that plane; $FF for any other vertex.
 428+ E919              ;==============================================================================
 429+ E919              clip_poly:
 430+ E919 3A 8D FB             ld a,(clip_n)
 431+ E91C 3D                   dec a
 432+ E91D 87                   add a,a
 433+ E91E 87                   add a,a
 434+ E91F 87                   add a,a
 435+ E920 2A 91 FB             ld hl,(clip_src)
 436+ E923 ED 31                add hl,a
 437+ E925 22 95 FB             ld (clip_s),hl
 438+ E928 E5                   push hl
 439+ E929 DD E1                pop ix
 440+ E92B CD DE E8             call plane_dist
 441+ E92E 22 99 FB             ld (clip_ds),hl
 442+ E931 2A 91 FB             ld hl,(clip_src)
 443+ E934 22 97 FB             ld (clip_e),hl
 444+ E937 FD 2A 93 FB          ld iy,(clip_dst)
 445+ E93B AF                   xor a
 446+ E93C 32 8E FB             ld (clip_on),a
 447+ E93F 3A 8D FB             ld a,(clip_n)
 448+ E942 32 8F FB             ld (clip_cnt),a
 449+ E945 DD 2A 97 FB  .loop:  ld ix,(clip_e)
 450+ E949 CD DE E8             call plane_dist
 451+ E94C 22 9B FB             ld (clip_de),hl
 452+ E94F CB 7C                bit 7,h
 453+ E951 20 2C                jr nz,.eout
 454+ E953 3A 9A FB             ld a,(clip_ds+1)
 455+ E956 CB 7F                bit 7,a
 456+ E958 28 0D                jr z,.ein
 457+ E95A                      ; S out, E in: intersection carries S's edge flag
 458+ E95A DD 2A 95 FB          ld ix,(clip_s)
 459+ E95E DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 460+ E961 32 9D FB             ld (clip_if),a
 461+ E964 CD AD E9             call clip_isect
 462+ E967 DD 2A 97 FB  .ein:   ld ix,(clip_e)
 463+ E96B 06 08                ld b,8                          ; whole vertex, with its corner byte
 464+ E96D DD 7E 00     .cpy:   ld a,(ix+0)
 465+ E970 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a
 466+ E973 DD 23                inc ix
 467+ E975 FD 23                inc iy
 468+ E977 10 F4                djnz .cpy
 469+ E979 21 8E FB             ld hl,clip_on
 470+ E97C 34                   inc (hl)
 471+ E97D 18 0E                jr .next
 472+ E97F 3A 9A FB     .eout:  ld a,(clip_ds+1)
 473+ E982 CB 7F                bit 7,a
 474+ E984 20 07                jr nz,.next
 475+ E986                      ; S in, E out: intersection starts a clip-plane edge
 476+ E986 AF                   xor a
 477+ E987 32 9D FB             ld (clip_if),a
 478+ E98A CD AD E9             call clip_isect
 479+ E98D 2A 97 FB     .next:  ld hl,(clip_e)
 480+ E990 22 95 FB             ld (clip_s),hl
 481+ E993 11 08 00             ld de,8
 482+ E996 19                   add hl,de
 483+ E997 22 97 FB             ld (clip_e),hl
 484+ E99A 2A 9B FB             ld hl,(clip_de)
 485+ E99D 22 99 FB             ld (clip_ds),hl
 486+ E9A0 21 8F FB             ld hl,clip_cnt
 487+ E9A3 35                   dec (hl)
 488+ E9A4 20 9F                jr nz,.loop
 489+ E9A6 3A 8E FB             ld a,(clip_on)
 490+ E9A9 32 8D FB             ld (clip_n),a
 491+ E9AC C9                   ret
 492+ E9AD
 493+ E9AD              ; Output S + t*(E-S) at IY, t = dS/(dS-dE)
 494+ E9AD              clip_isect:
 495+ E9AD                  IFDEF LEVEL_FAST_PATH
 496+ E9AD                      ; Level flight: a vertical building edge (S and E share X and Z)
 497+ E9AD                      ; meets the top or bottom plane at (X, +-0.5Z, Z) - no divide or
 498+ E9AD                      ; interpolation.  At pitch 0 vertical edges can't cross the near or
 499+ E9AD                      ; side planes, so this covers every plane they do cross.
 500+ E9AD 3A 42 B6             ld a,(pitch)
 501+ E9B0 B7                   or a
 502+ E9B1 20 49                jr nz,.gen
 503+ E9B3 3A 90 FB             ld a,(clip_plane)
 504+ E9B6 FE 08                cp 8
 505+ E9B8 38 42                jr c,.gen                       ; not top or bottom
 506+ E9BA 2A 95 FB             ld hl,(clip_s)
 507+ E9BD ED 5B 97 FB          ld de,(clip_e)
 508+ E9C1 1A                   ld a,(de)
 509+ E9C2 BE                   cp (hl)
 510+ E9C3 20 37                jr nz,.gen
 511+ E9C5 23                   inc hl
 512+ E9C6 13                   inc de
 513+ E9C7 1A                   ld a,(de)
 514+ E9C8 BE                   cp (hl)
 515+ E9C9 20 31                jr nz,.gen                      ; X differs
 516+ E9CB 23                   inc hl
 517+ E9CC 23                   inc hl
 518+ E9CD 23                   inc hl
 519+ E9CE 13                   inc de
 520+ E9CF 13                   inc de
 521+ E9D0 13                   inc de
 522+ E9D1 1A                   ld a,(de)
 523+ E9D2 BE                   cp (hl)
 524+ E9D3 20 27                jr nz,.gen
 525+ E9D5 23                   inc hl
 526+ E9D6 13                   inc de
 527+ E9D7 1A                   ld a,(de)
 528+ E9D8 BE                   cp (hl)
 529+ E9D9 20 21                jr nz,.gen                      ; Z differs
 530+ E9DB DD 2A 95 FB          ld ix,(clip_s)
 531+ E9DF DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)                     ; X and Z from S (= E)
 532+ E9E2 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a
 533+ E9E5 DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)
 534+ E9E8 FD 77 01             ld (iy+1),a
 535+ E9EB DD 6E 04             ld l,(ix+4)
 536+ E9EE DD 66 05             ld h,(ix+5)
 537+ E9F1 FD 75 04             ld (iy+4),l
 538+ E9F4 FD 74 05             ld (iy+5),h
 539+ E9F7 3A 90 FB             ld a,(clip_plane)
 540+ E9FA 18 6D                jr .ty                          ; Y = +-0.5Z
 541+ E9FC              .gen:
 542+ E9FC                  ENDIF
 543+ E9FC 2A 99 FB             ld hl,(clip_ds)
 544+ E9FF CD F9 B5             call abs_hl
 545+ EA02 22 9E FB             ld (ci_a),hl
 546+ EA05 2A 9B FB             ld hl,(clip_de)
 547+ EA08 CD F9 B5             call abs_hl
 548+ EA0B ED 5B 9E FB          ld de,(ci_a)
 549+ EA0F 19                   add hl,de
 550+ EA10 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; DE = |dS|+|dE|, HL = |dS|
 551+ EA11 E5                   push hl
 552+ EA12 B7                   or a
 553+ EA13 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 554+ EA15 E1                   pop hl
 555+ EA16 38 06                jr c,.dv
 556+ EA18 DD 21 FF FF          ld ix,$FFFF
 557+ EA1C 18 03                jr .t
 558+ EA1E CD 83 E7     .dv:    call div_frac
 559+ EA21 DD 22 A0 FB  .t:     ld (ci_t),ix
 560+ EA25 AF                   xor a
 561+ EA26 CD 9B EA             call lerp_c
 562+ EA29 FD 75 00             ld (iy+0),l
 563+ EA2C FD 74 01             ld (iy+1),h
 564+ EA2F 3E 02                ld a,2
 565+ EA31 CD 9B EA             call lerp_c
 566+ EA34 FD 75 02             ld (iy+2),l
 567+ EA37 FD 74 03             ld (iy+3),h
 568+ EA3A 3E 04                ld a,4
 569+ EA3C CD 9B EA             call lerp_c
 570+ EA3F FD 75 04             ld (iy+4),l
 571+ EA42 FD 74 05             ld (iy+5),h
 572+ EA45                      ; snap the point exactly onto the plane so rounding can't leave a
 573+ EA45                      ; gap between the polygon and the screen edge
 574+ EA45 FD 6E 04             ld l,(iy+4)
 575+ EA48 FD 66 05             ld h,(iy+5)                     ; Z
 576+ EA4B 3A 90 FB             ld a,(clip_plane)
 577+ EA4E FE 01                cp 1
 578+ EA50 20 0A                jr nz,.s2
 579+ EA52 FD 36 04 10          ld (iy+4),NEAR                  ; near:   Z = NEAR
 580+ EA56 FD 36 05 00          ld (iy+5),0
 581+ EA5A 18 27                jr .nz
 582+ EA5C FE 02        .s2:    cp 2
 583+ EA5E 20 05                jr nz,.s4
 584+ EA60 CD F2 B5             call neg_hl                     ; left:   X = -Z
 585+ EA63 18 18                jr .sx
 586+ EA65 FE 04        .s4:    cp 4
 587+ EA67 28 14                jr z,.sx                        ; right:  X = Z
 588+ EA69 F5           .ty:    push af
 589+ EA6A CD 32 B6             call zhalf
 590+ EA6D F1                   pop af
 591+ EA6E FE 08                cp 8
 592+ EA70 28 03                jr z,.sy                        ; top:    Y = 0.5Z
 593+ EA72 CD F2 B5             call neg_hl                     ; bottom: Y = -0.5Z
 594+ EA75 FD 75 02     .sy:    ld (iy+2),l
 595+ EA78 FD 74 03             ld (iy+3),h
 596+ EA7B 18 06                jr .nz
 597+ EA7D FD 75 00     .sx:    ld (iy+0),l
 598+ EA80 FD 74 01             ld (iy+1),h
 599+ EA83 3A 9D FB     .nz:    ld a,(clip_if)
 600+ EA86 FD 77 06             ld (iy+6),a
 601+ EA89 3A 90 FB             ld a,(clip_plane)               ; new vertex: no cached projection,
 602+ EA8C F6 80                or $80                          ; but remember the plane it is on
 603+ EA8E FD 77 07             ld (iy+7),a
 604+ EA91 01 08 00             ld bc,8
 605+ EA94 FD 09                add iy,bc
 606+ EA96 21 8E FB             ld hl,clip_on
 607+ EA99 34                   inc (hl)
 608+ EA9A C9                   ret
 609+ EA9B
 610+ EA9B              ; A = coordinate offset -> HL = S.c + (E.c - S.c) * t
 611+ EA9B              lerp_c:
 612+ EA9B 2A 97 FB             ld hl,(clip_e)
 613+ EA9E ED 31                add hl,a
 614+ EAA0 5E                   ld e,(hl)
 615+ EAA1 23                   inc hl
 616+ EAA2 56                   ld d,(hl)
 617+ EAA3 2A 95 FB             ld hl,(clip_s)
 618+ EAA6 ED 31                add hl,a
 619+ EAA8 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 620+ EAA9 23                   inc hl
 621+ EAAA 66                   ld h,(hl)
 622+ EAAB 6F                   ld l,a
 623+ EAAC E5                   push hl
 624+ EAAD EB                   ex de,hl
 625+ EAAE B7                   or a
 626+ EAAF ED 52                sbc hl,de
 627+ EAB1 ED 5B A0 FB          ld de,(ci_t)
 628+ EAB5 CD 50 E7             call mul_frac
 629+ EAB8 D1                   pop de
 630+ EAB9 19                   add hl,de
 631+ EABA C9                   ret
 632+ EABB
 633+ EABB              ;==============================================================================
 634+ EABB              ; Draw one face of the current building
 635+ EABB              ;   HL -> 4 vertex offsets into vtx[] (index*16), df_mask = edges to outline
 636+ EABB              ;   (bit k = edge from corner k to corner k+1)
 637+ EABB              ;==============================================================================
 638+ EABB              draw_face:
 639+ EABB 22 81 FB             ld (df_ptr),hl
 640+ EABE 01 00 FF             ld bc,$FF00                     ; B = AND of outcodes, C = OR
 641+ EAC1 3A 3C FB             ld a,(vtx_hi)
 642+ EAC4 57                   ld d,a
 643+ EAC5                      REPT 4
 644+ EAC5 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 645+ EAC6 23          >        inc hl
 646+ EAC7 C6 06       >        add a,6
 647+ EAC9 5F          >        ld e,a
 648+ EACA 1A          >        ld a,(de)
 649+ EACB 5F          >        ld e,a
 650+ EACC A0          >        and b
 651+ EACD 47          >        ld b,a
 652+ EACE 7B          >        ld a,e
 653+ EACF B1          >        or c
 654+ EAD0 4F          >        ld c,a
 644+ EAD1 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 645+ EAD2 23          >        inc hl
 646+ EAD3 C6 06       >        add a,6
 647+ EAD5 5F          >        ld e,a
 648+ EAD6 1A          >        ld a,(de)
 649+ EAD7 5F          >        ld e,a
 650+ EAD8 A0          >        and b
 651+ EAD9 47          >        ld b,a
 652+ EADA 7B          >        ld a,e
 653+ EADB B1          >        or c
 654+ EADC 4F          >        ld c,a
 644+ EADD 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 645+ EADE 23          >        inc hl
 646+ EADF C6 06       >        add a,6
 647+ EAE1 5F          >        ld e,a
 648+ EAE2 1A          >        ld a,(de)
 649+ EAE3 5F          >        ld e,a
 650+ EAE4 A0          >        and b
 651+ EAE5 47          >        ld b,a
 652+ EAE6 7B          >        ld a,e
 653+ EAE7 B1          >        or c
 654+ EAE8 4F          >        ld c,a
 644+ EAE9 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 645+ EAEA 23          >        inc hl
 646+ EAEB C6 06       >        add a,6
 647+ EAED 5F          >        ld e,a
 648+ EAEE 1A          >        ld a,(de)
 649+ EAEF 5F          >        ld e,a
 650+ EAF0 A0          >        and b
 651+ EAF1 47          >        ld b,a
 652+ EAF2 7B          >        ld a,e
 653+ EAF3 B1          >        or c
 654+ EAF4 4F          >        ld c,a
 655+ EAF5                      ENDR
 656+ EAF5 78                   ld a,b
 657+ EAF6 B7                   or a
 658+ EAF7 C0                   ret nz                          ; wholly outside one plane
 659+ EAF8 79                   ld a,c
 660+ EAF9 B7                   or a
 661+ EAFA 20 62                jr nz,.clip
 662+ EAFC
 663+ EAFC                      ; fast path - every corner on screen, use cached projections

 666+ EB02 3A 3C FB             ld a,(vtx_hi)
 667+ EB05 47                   ld b,a
 668+ EB06                      FACEVTX 1
 668+ EB06 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 668+ EB07 23          >        inc hl
 668+ EB08 C6 07       >        add a,7
 668+ EB0A 4F          >        ld c,a
 668+ EB0B 0A          >        ld a,(bc)

 668+ EB0F 0A          >        ld a,(bc)

 668+ EB12 3A 84 FB    >        ld a,(df_mask)
 668+ EB15 E6 01       >        and 1
 668+ EB17 12          >        ld (de),a
 668+ EB18 1C          >        inc e
 668+ EB19 1C          >        inc e
 669+ EB1A                      FACEVTX 2
 669+ EB1A 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 669+ EB1B 23          >        inc hl
 669+ EB1C C6 07       >        add a,7
 669+ EB1E 4F          >        ld c,a
 669+ EB1F 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 669+ EB20 12          >        ld (de),a
 669+ EB21 1C          >        inc e
 669+ EB22 0C          >        inc c
 669+ EB23 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 669+ EB24 12          >        ld (de),a
 669+ EB25 1C          >        inc e
 669+ EB26 3A 84 FB    >        ld a,(df_mask)
 669+ EB29 E6 02       >        and 2
 669+ EB2B 12          >        ld (de),a
 669+ EB2C 1C          >        inc e
 669+ EB2D 1C          >        inc e
 670+ EB2E                      FACEVTX 4
 670+ EB2E 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 670+ EB2F 23          >        inc hl
 670+ EB30 C6 07       >        add a,7
 670+ EB32 4F          >        ld c,a
 670+ EB33 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 670+ EB34 12          >        ld (de),a
 670+ EB35 1C          >        inc e
 670+ EB36 0C          >        inc c
 670+ EB37 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 670+ EB38 12          >        ld (de),a
 670+ EB39 1C          >        inc e
 670+ EB3A 3A 84 FB    >        ld a,(df_mask)
 670+ EB3D E6 04       >        and 4
 670+ EB3F 12          >        ld (de),a
 670+ EB40 1C          >        inc e
 670+ EB41 1C          >        inc e
 671+ EB42                      FACEVTX 8
 671+ EB42 7E          >        ld a,(hl)
 671+ EB43 23          >        inc hl
 671+ EB44 C6 07       >        add a,7
 671+ EB46 4F          >        ld c,a
 671+ EB47 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 671+ EB48 12          >        ld (de),a
 671+ EB49 1C          >        inc e
 671+ EB4A 0C          >        inc c
 671+ EB4B 0A          >        ld a,(bc)
 671+ EB4C 12          >        ld (de),a
 671+ EB4D 1C          >        inc e
 671+ EB4E 3A 84 FB    >        ld a,(df_mask)
 671+ EB51 E6 08       >        and 8
 671+ EB53 12          >        ld (de),a
 671+ EB54 1C          >        inc e
 671+ EB55 1C          >        inc e
 672+ EB56 3E 04                ld a,4
 673+ EB58 32 62 FC             ld (p2n),a
 674+ EB5B C3 48 EC             jp .render
 675+ EB5E
 676+ EB5E 32 83 FB     .clip:  ld (df_or),a
 677+ EB61 3A 84 FB             ld a,(df_mask)
 678+ EB64 32 85 FB             ld (df_m),a
 679+ EB67 2A 81 FB             ld hl,(df_ptr)
 680+ EB6A FD 21 A2 FB          ld iy,clipA
 681+ EB6E 06 04                ld b,4
 682+ EB70 7E           .cp:    ld a,(hl)
 683+ EB71 23                   inc hl
 684+ EB72 32 86 FB             ld (df_cur),a

 686+ EB77 3A 3C FB             ld a,(vtx_hi)
 687+ EB7A DD 67                ld ixh,a
 688+ EB7C 0E 06                ld c,6
 689+ EB7E DD 7E 00     .cpb:   ld a,(ix+0)
 690+ EB81 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a
 691+ EB84 DD 23                inc ix
 692+ EB86 FD 23                inc iy
 693+ EB88 0D                   dec c
 694+ EB89 20 F3                jr nz,.cpb
 695+ EB8B 3A 85 FB             ld a,(df_m)
 696+ EB8E 5F                   ld e,a
 697+ EB8F E6 01                and 1
 698+ EB91 FD 77 00             ld (iy+0),a                     ; edge flag from mask
 699+ EB94 7B                   ld a,e
 700+ EB95 0F                   rrca
 701+ EB96 32 85 FB             ld (df_m),a
 702+ EB99 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)                     ; the corner's outcode (IX at +6)
 703+ EB9C B7                   or a
 704+ EB9D 3E FF                ld a,$FF
 705+ EB9F 20 03                jr nz,.cn
 706+ EBA1 3A 86 FB             ld a,(df_cur)                   ; on screen: projection is cached
 707+ EBA4 FD 77 01     .cn:    ld (iy+1),a
 708+ EBA7 FD 23                inc iy
 709+ EBA9 FD 23                inc iy
 710+ EBAB 10 C3                djnz .cp
 711+ EBAD 3E 04                ld a,4
 712+ EBAF 32 8D FB             ld (clip_n),a
 713+ EBB2 21 A2 FB             ld hl,clipA
 714+ EBB5 22 91 FB             ld (clip_src),hl
 715+ EBB8 21 02 FC             ld hl,clipB
 716+ EBBB 22 93 FB             ld (clip_dst),hl
 717+ EBBE 3E 01                ld a,1
 718+ EBC0 32 90 FB     .pl:    ld (clip_plane),a
 719+ EBC3 21 83 FB             ld hl,df_or
 720+ EBC6 A6                   and (hl)
 721+ EBC7 28 17                jr z,.skip
 722+ EBC9 CD 19 E9             call clip_poly

 724+ EBCF FE 03                cp 3
 725+ EBD1 D8                   ret c
 726+ EBD2 2A 91 FB             ld hl,(clip_src)
 727+ EBD5 ED 5B 93 FB          ld de,(clip_dst)
 728+ EBD9 ED 53 91 FB          ld (clip_src),de
 729+ EBDD 22 93 FB             ld (clip_dst),hl
 730+ EBE0 3A 90 FB     .skip:  ld a,(clip_plane)
 731+ EBE3 87                   add a,a
 732+ EBE4 FE 20                cp 32
 733+ EBE6 20 D8                jr nz,.pl
 734+ EBE8
 735+ EBE8 DD 2A 91 FB          ld ix,(clip_src)
 736+ EBEC FD 21 80 E0          ld iy,p2buf
 737+ EBF0 3A 8D FB             ld a,(clip_n)
 738+ EBF3 32 62 FC             ld (p2n),a
 739+ EBF6 47                   ld b,a
 740+ EBF7 C5           .pj:    push bc
 741+ EBF8 DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)
 742+ EBFB FE 80                cp $80
 743+ EBFD 30 0C                jr nc,.pf
 744+ EBFF C6 07                add a,7                         ; unclipped on-screen corner:
 745+ EC01 6F                   ld l,a                          ; reuse its cached sx, sy
 746+ EC02 3A 3C FB             ld a,(vtx_hi)
 747+ EC05 67                   ld h,a
 748+ EC06 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 749+ EC07 2C                   inc l
 750+ EC08 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 751+ EC09 18 24                jr .ps
 752+ EC0B DD E5        .pf:    push ix
 753+ EC0D E1                   pop hl
 754+ EC0E CD B0 E7             call project
 755+ EC11 5F                   ld e,a
 756+ EC12 DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)
 757+ EC15 3C                   inc a
 758+ EC16 28 16                jr z,.p0                        ; $FF: an ordinary vertex
 759+ EC18 3D                   dec a
 760+ EC19                      ; On a clip plane: put it exactly on that screen edge (projection
 761+ EC19                      ; rounding could leave it a pixel inside, opening a 1-pixel gap).
 762+ EC19 1F                   rra                             ; near: nothing
 763+ EC1A 1F                   rra
 764+ EC1B 30 02                jr nc,.e1
 765+ EC1D 1E 00                ld e,0                          ; left
 766+ EC1F 1F           .e1:    rra
 767+ EC20 30 02                jr nc,.e2
 768+ EC22 1E FF                ld e,255                        ; right
 769+ EC24 1F           .e2:    rra
 770+ EC25 30 02                jr nc,.e3
 771+ EC27 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; top
 772+ EC29 1F           .e3:    rra
 773+ EC2A 30 02                jr nc,.p0
 774+ EC2C 0E 7F                ld c,VIEW_H-1                   ; bottom
 775+ EC2E 7B           .p0:    ld a,e
 776+ EC2F FD 77 00     .ps:    ld (iy+0),a
 777+ EC32 FD 71 01             ld (iy+1),c
 778+ EC35 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
 779+ EC38 FD 77 02             ld (iy+2),a
 780+ EC3B 01 08 00             ld bc,8
 781+ EC3E DD 09                add ix,bc
 782+ EC40 01 04 00             ld bc,4
 783+ EC43 FD 09                add iy,bc
 784+ EC45 C1                   pop bc
 785+ EC46 10 AF                djnz .pj
 786+ EC48
 787+ EC48              .render:
 788+ EC48                      ; close the polygon: copy vertex 0 after the last
 789+ EC48 3A 62 FC             ld a,(p2n)
 790+ EC4B 87                   add a,a
 791+ EC4C 87                   add a,a
 792+ EC4D 21 80 E0             ld hl,p2buf
 793+ EC50 ED 31                add hl,a
 794+ EC52 3A 80 E0             ld a,(p2buf)
 795+ EC55 77                   ld (hl),a
 796+ EC56 23                   inc hl
 797+ EC57 3A 81 E0             ld a,(p2buf+1)
 798+ EC5A 77                   ld (hl),a
 799+ EC5B                  IFDEF DRAW_LINES
 800+ EC5B ~                    call fill_poly
 801+ EC5B ~                    ld a,EDGE_COL
 802+ EC5B ~                    ld (line_col),a
 803+ EC5B ~                    jp outline_poly
 804+ EC5B                  ELSE
 805+ EC5B C3 5E EC             jp fill_poly                    ; solid faces only
 806+ EC5E                  ENDIF
 807+ EC5E
 808+ EC5E              ;==============================================================================
 809+ EC5E              ; Convex polygon fill from p2buf (8-bit screen coords).
 810+ EC5E              ;   Each edge is walked with an exact integer DDA into one of two x tables:
 811+ EC5E              ;   downward edges -> TA, upward edges -> TB.  For a convex polygon every row
 812+ EC5E              ;   then has one entry in each, and the span between them is filled.
 813+ EC5E              ;   Winding doesn't matter.
 814+ EC5E              ;==============================================================================
 815+ EC5E              fill_poly:
 816+ EC5E 3A 62 FC             ld a,(p2n)
 817+ EC61 47                   ld b,a
 818+ EC62 21 81 E0             ld hl,p2buf+1
 819+ EC65 0E FF                ld c,255
 820+ EC67 16 00                ld d,0
 821+ EC69 7E           .mm:    ld a,(hl)
 822+ EC6A B9                   cp c
 823+ EC6B 30 01                jr nc,.m1
 824+ EC6D 4F                   ld c,a
 825+ EC6E BA           .m1:    cp d
 826+ EC6F 38 01                jr c,.m2
 827+ EC71 57                   ld d,a
 828+ EC72 23           .m2:    inc hl
 829+ EC73 23                   inc hl
 830+ EC74 23                   inc hl
 831+ EC75 23                   inc hl
 832+ EC76 10 F1                djnz .mm
 833+ EC78 79                   ld a,c
 834+ EC79 BA                   cp d
 835+ EC7A C8                   ret z                           ; zero height - outline only
 836+ EC7B 32 63 FC             ld (fy0),a
 837+ EC7E 7A                   ld a,d
 838+ EC7F 32 64 FC             ld (fy1),a
 839+ EC82
 840+ EC82 DD 21 80 E0          ld ix,p2buf
 841+ EC86 3A 62 FC             ld a,(p2n)
 842+ EC89 32 65 FC             ld (fe_cnt),a
 843+ EC8C DD 56 00     .ed:    ld d,(ix+0)
 844+ EC8F DD 5E 01             ld e,(ix+1)
 845+ EC92 DD 46 04             ld b,(ix+4)
 846+ EC95 DD 4E 05             ld c,(ix+5)
 847+ EC98 7B                   ld a,e
 848+ EC99 B9                   cp c
 849+ EC9A 28 15                jr z,.nx                        ; horizontal edge
 850+ EC9C 38 0A                jr c,.down
 851+ EC9E 7A                   ld a,d                          ; going up: swap ends, use TB
 852+ EC9F 50                   ld d,b
 853+ ECA0 47                   ld b,a
 854+ ECA1 7B                   ld a,e
 855+ ECA2 59                   ld e,c
 856+ ECA3 4F                   ld c,a
 857+ ECA4 3E E3                ld a,high TB
 858+ ECA6 18 02                jr .walk
 859+ ECA8 3E E2        .down:  ld a,high TA
 860+ ECAA DD E5        .walk:  push ix
 861+ ECAC CD C7 EC             call walk_edge
 862+ ECAF DD E1                pop ix
 863+ ECB1 01 04 00     .nx:    ld bc,4
 864+ ECB4 DD 09                add ix,bc
 865+ ECB6 21 65 FC             ld hl,fe_cnt
 866+ ECB9 35                   dec (hl)
 867+ ECBA 20 D0                jr nz,.ed
 868+ ECBC
 869+ ECBC 3A 24 FB             ld a,(face_col)                 ; shaded colour of this face
 870+ ECBF 32 6B FC             ld (l2_cls_byte),a              ; DMA source byte for long spans
 871+ ECC2 57                   ld d,a
 872+ ECC3 5F                   ld e,a                          ; PUSH pair for short spans
 873+ ECC4 C3 4C ED             jp fill_spans
 874+ ECC7
 875+ ECC7              ; Walk an edge (D=xa, E=ya) -> (B=xb, C=yb), yb > ya, into table page A.
 876+ ECC7              ; Bresenham style: x += q each row, fractional r/dy accumulated in a biased
 877+ ECC7              ; byte (carry == wrap), so the end point is hit exactly.
 878+ ECC7              walk_edge:
 879+ ECC7 67                   ld h,a
 880+ ECC8 6B                   ld l,e
 881+ ECC9 79                   ld a,c
 882+ ECCA 93                   sub e
 883+ ECCB 4F                   ld c,a                          ; C = dy (1..191)
 884+ ECCC 78                   ld a,b
 885+ ECCD 92                   sub d                           ; dx
 886+ ECCE 38 37                jr c,.neg
 887+ ECD0 B9                   cp c
 888+ ECD1 30 07                jr nc,.pdv
 889+ ECD3 DD 6F                ld ixl,a                        ; steep: q = 0, r = dx
 890+ ECD5 CD 40 ED             call .setup
 891+ ECD8 18 20                jr .p0
 892+ ECDA CD 9F E7     .pdv:   call div8                       ; E = q, A = r
 893+ ECDD DD 63                ld ixh,e
 894+ ECDF DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 895+ ECE1 CD 40 ED             call .setup
 896+ ECE4 DD 7C                ld a,ixh
 897+ ECE6 B7                   or a
 898+ ECE7 28 11                jr z,.p0
 899+ ECE9 72           .pl:    ld (hl),d
 900+ ECEA 2C                   inc l
 901+ ECEB 7A                   ld a,d
 902+ ECEC DD 84                add a,ixh
 903+ ECEE 57                   ld d,a
 904+ ECEF 7B                   ld a,e
 905+ ECF0 DD 85                add a,ixl
 906+ ECF2 30 02                jr nc,.pl1
 907+ ECF4 81                   add a,c
 908+ ECF5 14                   inc d
 909+ ECF6 5F           .pl1:   ld e,a
 910+ ECF7 10 F0                djnz .pl
 911+ ECF9 C9                   ret
 912+ ECFA 72           .p0:    ld (hl),d
 913+ ECFB 2C                   inc l
 914+ ECFC 7B                   ld a,e
 915+ ECFD DD 85                add a,ixl
 916+ ECFF 30 02                jr nc,.p01
 917+ ED01 81                   add a,c
 918+ ED02 14                   inc d
 919+ ED03 5F           .p01:   ld e,a
 920+ ED04 10 F4                djnz .p0
 921+ ED06 C9                   ret
 922+ ED07
 923+ ED07 ED 44        .neg:   neg
 924+ ED09 B9                   cp c
 925+ ED0A 30 07                jr nc,.ndv
 926+ ED0C DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 927+ ED0E CD 40 ED             call .setup
 928+ ED11 18 20                jr .n0
 929+ ED13 CD 9F E7     .ndv:   call div8
 930+ ED16 DD 63                ld ixh,e
 931+ ED18 DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 932+ ED1A CD 40 ED             call .setup
 933+ ED1D DD 7C                ld a,ixh
 934+ ED1F B7                   or a
 935+ ED20 28 11                jr z,.n0
 936+ ED22 72           .nl:    ld (hl),d
 937+ ED23 2C                   inc l
 938+ ED24 7A                   ld a,d
 939+ ED25 DD 94                sub ixh
 940+ ED27 57                   ld d,a
 941+ ED28 7B                   ld a,e
 942+ ED29 DD 85                add a,ixl
 943+ ED2B 30 02                jr nc,.nl1
 944+ ED2D 81                   add a,c
 945+ ED2E 15                   dec d
 946+ ED2F 5F           .nl1:   ld e,a
 947+ ED30 10 F0                djnz .nl
 948+ ED32 C9                   ret
 949+ ED33 72           .n0:    ld (hl),d
 950+ ED34 2C                   inc l
 951+ ED35 7B                   ld a,e
 952+ ED36 DD 85                add a,ixl
 953+ ED38 30 02                jr nc,.n01
 954+ ED3A 81                   add a,c
 955+ ED3B 15                   dec d
 956+ ED3C 5F           .n01:   ld e,a
 957+ ED3D 10 F4                djnz .n0
 958+ ED3F C9                   ret
 959+ ED40
 960+ ED40              ; B = dy+1 rows, E = biased accumulator (dy/2 - dy), C = -dy
 961+ ED40 41           .setup: ld b,c
 962+ ED41 04                   inc b
 963+ ED42 79                   ld a,c
 964+ ED43 CB 3F                srl a
 965+ ED45 91                   sub c
 966+ ED46 5F                   ld e,a
 967+ ED47 79                   ld a,c
 968+ ED48 ED 44                neg
 969+ ED4A 4F                   ld c,a
 970+ ED4B C9                   ret
 971+ ED4C
 972+ ED4C              ;==============================================================================
 973+ ED4C              ; Span filler.  Rows fy0..fy1, x from TA/TB.  DE = colour pair.
 974+ ED4C              ; Short spans use the stack pointer as a 2-byte write pointer into the frame
 975+ ED4C              ; buffer (interrupts are disabled), jumping into an unrolled block of PUSH DE.
 976+ ED4C              ; Spans of DMA_SPAN_MIN pixels or more are written by the zxnDMA, which only
 977+ ED4C              ; needs its length and destination reloaded (see clear_screen).
 978+ ED4C              ;==============================================================================
 979+ ED4C              fill_spans:
 980+ ED4C 3A 63 FC             ld a,(fy0)
 981+ ED4F CD 20 E7             call seg_map                    ; first row's section into slot 0
 982+ ED52 ED 73 67 FC          ld (saved_sp),sp
 983+ ED56 3A 63 FC             ld a,(fy0)
 984+ ED59 47                   ld b,a
 985+ ED5A 3A 64 FC             ld a,(fy1)
 986+ ED5D DD 6F                ld ixl,a
 987+ ED5F
 988+ ED5F              fs_loop:
 989+ ED5F 26 E2                ld h,high TA
 990+ ED61 68                   ld l,b
 991+ ED62 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 992+ ED63 24                   inc h
 993+ ED64 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 994+ ED65 B9                   cp c
 995+ ED66 38 03                jr c,.ord
 996+ ED68 69                   ld l,c
 997+ ED69 4F                   ld c,a
 998+ ED6A 7D                   ld a,l
 999+ ED6B 6F           .ord:   ld l,a                          ; A = left x, C = right x
1000+ ED6C 78                   ld a,b
1001+ ED6D E6 1F                and 31
1002+ ED6F 67                   ld h,a                          ; row within the paged section
1003+ ED70 79                   ld a,c
1004+ ED71 95                   sub l                           ; width-1
1005+ ED72 FE 13                cp DMA_SPAN_MIN-1
1006+ ED74 30 13                jr nc,.dma
1007+ ED76 CB 3F        .push:  srl a
1008+ ED78 38 03                jr c,.even
1009+ ED7A 73                   ld (hl),e                       ; odd width: single pixel first
1010+ ED7B 18 01                jr .go
1011+ ED7D 3C           .even:  inc a
1012+ ED7E 69           .go:    ld l,c
1013+ ED7F 23                   inc hl                          ; one past right edge (may be $C000)
1014+ ED80 F9                   ld sp,hl
1015+ ED81 6F                   ld l,a
1016+ ED82 3E 80                ld a,low push_end
1017+ ED84 95                   sub l
1018+ ED85 6F                   ld l,a
1019+ ED86 26 E1                ld h,high push_end
1020+ ED88 E9                   jp (hl)
1021+ ED89
1022+ ED89                      ; Long span: zxnDMA from the fixed colour byte (port A) to HL.
1023+ ED89                      ; Everything but length and destination was set up by clear_screen.
1024+ ED89 3C           .dma:   inc a
1025+ ED8A 20 03                jr nz,.dmal
1026+ ED8C 3D                   dec a                           ; 256 wide: length needs the high
1027+ ED8D 18 E7                jr .push                        ; byte, so PUSH it (A = width-1)
1028+ ED8F              .dmal:
1029+ ED8F 4F                   ld c,a
1030+ ED90 3E 25                ld a,DMA_WR0_LEN_LO
1031+ ED92 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1032+ ED94 79                   ld a,c
1033+ ED95 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a    ; length = width
1034+ ED97 3E AD                ld a,DMA_WR4_CONT_MODE
1035+ ED99 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1036+ ED9B 7D                   ld a,l
1037+ ED9C D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a    ; port B = y*256 + x
1038+ ED9E 7C                   ld a,h
1039+ ED9F D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1040+ EDA1 3E CF                ld a,DMA_LOAD
1041+ EDA3 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1042+ EDA5 3E 87                ld a,DMA_ENABLE
1043+ EDA7 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a    ; CPU waits until the span is written
1044+ EDA9 C3 80 E1             jp push_end
1045+ EDAC
1046+ EDAC              ;==============================================================================
1047+ EDAC              ; Outline the polygon in p2buf, skipping edges flagged 0 (clip edges)
1048+ EDAC              ;==============================================================================
1049+ EDAC              outline_poly:
1050+ EDAC DD 21 80 E0          ld ix,p2buf
1051+ EDB0 3A 62 FC             ld a,(p2n)
1052+ EDB3 47                   ld b,a
1053+ EDB4 C5           .lp:    push bc
1054+ EDB5 DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+2)
1055+ EDB8 B7                   or a
1056+ EDB9 28 0F                jr z,.sk
1057+ EDBB DD 6E 00             ld l,(ix+0)
1058+ EDBE DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
1059+ EDC1 DD 5E 04             ld e,(ix+4)
1060+ EDC4 DD 56 05             ld d,(ix+5)
1061+ EDC7 CD D3 ED             call draw_line
1062+ EDCA 01 04 00     .sk:    ld bc,4
1063+ EDCD DD 09                add ix,bc
1064+ EDCF C1                   pop bc
1065+ EDD0 10 E2                djnz .lp
1066+ EDD2 C9                   ret
1067+ EDD3
1068+ EDD3              ;==============================================================================
1069+ EDD3              ; Bresenham line, on-screen 8-bit coordinates.
1070+ EDD3              ;   L = x0, H = y0, E = x1, D = y1, colour in line_col.  Preserves IX, IY.
1071+ EDD3              ;   Pages Layer 2 sections into slot 0 as the line crosses them.
1072+ EDD3              ;==============================================================================
1073+ EDD3              draw_line:
1074+ EDD3 3A 66 FC             ld a,(line_col)
1075+ EDD6 4F                   ld c,a
1076+ EDD7 7B                   ld a,e
1077+ EDD8 95                   sub l
1078+ EDD9 30 06                jr nc,.xp
1079+ EDDB ED 44                neg
1080+ EDDD 06 2D                ld b,OP_DEC_L
1081+ EDDF 18 02                jr .xd
1082+ EDE1 06 2C        .xp:    ld b,OP_INC_L
1083+ EDE3 5F           .xd:    ld e,a                          ; |dx|
1084+ EDE4 78                   ld a,b
1085+ EDE5 32 0D EE             ld (.smx1),a
1086+ EDE8 32 2A EE             ld (.smx2),a
1087+ EDEB 7A                   ld a,d
1088+ EDEC 94                   sub h
1089+ EDED 30 06                jr nc,.yp
1090+ EDEF ED 44                neg
1091+ EDF1 06 25                ld b,OP_DEC_H
1092+ EDF3 18 02                jr .yd
1093+ EDF5 06 24        .yp:    ld b,OP_INC_H
1094+ EDF7 57           .yd:    ld d,a                          ; |dy|
1095+ EDF8 78                   ld a,b
1096+ EDF9 32 11 EE             ld (.smy1),a
1097+ EDFC 32 21 EE             ld (.smy2),a
1098+ EDFF 7C                   ld a,h
1099+ EE00 CD 20 E7             call seg_map                    ; page in the start row's section
1100+ EE03 67                   ld h,a                          ; y within the section
1101+ EE04 7B                   ld a,e
1102+ EE05 BA                   cp d
1103+ EE06 38 13                jr c,.ymaj
1104+ EE08 43                   ld b,e
1105+ EE09 04                   inc b
1106+ EE0A CB 3F                srl a
1107+ EE0C 71           .xl:    ld (hl),c
1108+ EE0D 2C           .smx1:  inc l
1109+ EE0E 92                   sub d
1110+ EE0F 30 07                jr nc,.xs
1111+ EE11 24           .smy1:  inc h
1112+ EE12 CB 6C                bit 5,h                         ; left the 32-row section?
1113+ EE14 C4 37 E7             call nz,line_seg
1114+ EE17 83                   add a,e
1115+ EE18 10 F2        .xs:    djnz .xl
1116+ EE1A C9                   ret
1117+ EE1B 42           .ymaj:  ld b,d
1118+ EE1C 04                   inc b
1119+ EE1D 7A                   ld a,d
1120+ EE1E CB 3F                srl a
1121+ EE20 71           .yl:    ld (hl),c
1122+ EE21 24           .smy2:  inc h
1123+ EE22 CB 6C                bit 5,h
1124+ EE24 C4 37 E7             call nz,line_seg
1125+ EE27 93                   sub e
1126+ EE28 30 02                jr nc,.ys
1127+ EE2A 2C           .smx2:  inc l
1128+ EE2B 82                   add a,d
1129+ EE2C 10 F2        .ys:    djnz .yl
1130+ EE2E C9                   ret
1131+ EE2F
1132+ EE2F              ;==============================================================================
1133+ EE2F              ; Clear the viewport (rows 0..VIEW_H-1) of the back buffer with the zxnDMA,
1134+ EE2F              ; one 8K section (32 rows) at a time through slot 0: sky above the horizon
1135+ EE2F              ; row, ground below.  The fills reuse the l2_fill command block from
1136+ EE2F              ; layer2_cls.asm, patching its destination (port B) and length; the source
1137+ EE2F              ; (port A) is the fixed byte l2_cls_byte.  Zero-length fills are skipped.
1138+ EE2F              ;==============================================================================
1139+ EE2F              clear_screen:
1140+ EE2F 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; C = first row of the section
1141+ EE31 79           .sec:   ld a,c
1142+ EE32 CD 20 E7             call seg_map
1143+ EE35 3A B0 BA             ld a,(horizon)                  ; sky rows here = clamp(hz - C, 0, 32)
1144+ EE38                  IFDEF SKY_IS_WALL
1145+ EE38 3C                   inc a                           ; the horizon row too: at the horizon
1146+ EE39                                                      ; it is all distant wall, and it hides
1147+ EE39                                                      ; a 1-row rounding gap under the
1148+ EE39                                                      ; wall's top edge when pitched
1149+ EE39                  ENDIF
1150+ EE39 91                   sub c
1151+ EE3A 30 01                jr nc,.s1
1152+ EE3C AF                   xor a
1153+ EE3D FE 21        .s1:    cp 33
1154+ EE3F 38 02                jr c,.s2
1155+ EE41 3E 20                ld a,32
1156+ EE43 32 6A FC     .s2:    ld (cs_sky),a
1157+ EE46 B7                   or a
1158+ EE47 28 0D                jr z,.gnd
1159+ EE49 C5                   push bc
1160+ EE4A 47                   ld b,a                          ; sky: section start, rows*256 bytes
1161+ EE4B 0E 00                ld c,0
1162+ EE4D 21 00 00             ld hl,0
1163+ EE50                  IFDEF SKY_IS_WALL
1164+ EE50 3E 01                ld a,WALL_FAMILY*4+SHADE_FACE   ; "sky" is the perimeter wall
1165+ EE52                  ELSE
1166+ EE52 ~                    ld a,SKY_COL
1167+ EE52                  ENDIF
1168+ EE52 CD 7E EE             call dma_fill
1169+ EE55 C1                   pop bc
1170+ EE56 3A 6A FC     .gnd:   ld a,(cs_sky)
1171+ EE59 FE 20                cp 32
1172+ EE5B 28 11                jr z,.nx
1173+ EE5D C5                   push bc
1174+ EE5E 67                   ld h,a                          ; ground: after the sky rows
1175+ EE5F 2E 00                ld l,0
1176+ EE61 ED 44                neg
1177+ EE63 C6 20                add a,32
1178+ EE65 47                   ld b,a
1179+ EE66 0E 00                ld c,0
1180+ EE68 3E 07                ld a,GROUND_COL
1181+ EE6A CD 7E EE             call dma_fill
1182+ EE6D C1                   pop bc
1183+ EE6E 79           .nx:    ld a,c
1184+ EE6F C6 20                add a,32
1185+ EE71 4F                   ld c,a
1186+ EE72 FE 80                cp VIEW_H
1187+ EE74 20 BB                jr nz,.sec
1188+ EE76                      ; Leave the DMA ready for fill_spans: length high byte 0, so each span
1189+ EE76                      ; only reloads length low, destination, LOAD and ENABLE.  fill_poly
1190+ EE76                      ; puts each building's colour in the source byte l2_cls_byte.
1191+ EE76 3E 45        .spans: ld a,DMA_WR0_LEN_HI
1192+ EE78 D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1193+ EE7A AF                   xor a
1194+ EE7B D3 6B                out (IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT),a
1195+ EE7D C9                   ret
1196+ EE7E
1197+ EE7E              ; DMA fill: HL = destination, BC = length (non-zero), A = colour
1198+ EE7E              dma_fill:
1199+ EE7E 32 6B FC             ld (l2_cls_byte),a
1200+ EE81 22 78 FC             ld (l2_fill_bstrt),hl
1201+ EE84 ED 43 73 FC          ld (l2_fill_length),bc
1202+ EE88 21 6C FC             ld hl,l2_fill
1203+ EE8B 06 12                ld b,l2_fill_cmd_len
1204+ EE8D 0E 6B                ld c,IO_DATAGEAR_DMA_PORT
1205+ EE8F ED B3                otir                            ; CPU waits while the DMA runs
1206+ EE91 C9                   ret
1207+ EE92
1208+ EE92              ;==============================================================================
1209+ EE92              ; City traversal - back to front without sorting.
1210+ EE92              ;   For grid-aligned boxes in separate cells, a box can only hide boxes whose
1211+ EE92              ;   cell offsets from the camera cell are larger (or equal) on both axes in
1212+ EE92              ;   the same quadrant.  So: rows from the far edges inwards, and within each
1213+ EE92              ;   row columns from the far edges inwards, the camera row/column last.
1214+ EE92              ;==============================================================================
1215+ EE92              draw_city:
1216+ EE92                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1217+ EE92 CD 5D F7             call occ_begin
1218+ EE95 3A 3E FB             ld a,(occ_on)
1219+ EE98 B7                   or a
1220+ EE99 28 0E                jr z,draw_cells                 ; pitched: the painter's order as is
1221+ EE9B CD 86 F7             call occ_cells                  ; front to back: keep what isn't hidden
1222+ EE9E 3A 3F FB             ld a,(occ_full)
1223+ EEA1 B7                   or a
1224+ EEA2 CA BB FA             jp z,occ_draw_list              ; back to front: draw it
1225+ EEA5 AF                   xor a                           ; too many to keep: draw this frame
1226+ EEA6 32 3E FB             ld (occ_on),a                   ; the plain way
1227+ EEA9                  ENDIF
1228+ EEA9              draw_cells:
1229+ EEA9 3A D6 BA             ld a,(cz)
1230+ EEAC D6 06                sub VIEW_R
1231+ EEAE 32 1F FB             ld (cell_j),a
1232+ EEB1 3A 1F FB     .b:     ld a,(cell_j)
1233+ EEB4 21 D6 BA             ld hl,cz
1234+ EEB7 BE                   cp (hl)
1235+ EEB8 28 09                jr z,.f
1236+ EEBA CD DD EE             call draw_row
1237+ EEBD 21 1F FB             ld hl,cell_j
1238+ EEC0 34                   inc (hl)
1239+ EEC1 18 EE                jr .b
1240+ EEC3 3A D6 BA     .f:     ld a,(cz)
1241+ EEC6 C6 06                add a,VIEW_R
1242+ EEC8 32 1F FB             ld (cell_j),a
1243+ EECB 3A 1F FB     .f2:    ld a,(cell_j)
1244+ EECE 21 D6 BA             ld hl,cz
1245+ EED1 BE                   cp (hl)
1246+ EED2 28 09                jr z,draw_row                   ; camera row last
1247+ EED4 CD DD EE             call draw_row
1248+ EED7 21 1F FB             ld hl,cell_j
1249+ EEDA 35                   dec (hl)
1250+ EEDB 18 EE                jr .f2
1251+ EEDD
1252+ EEDD              draw_row:
1253+ EEDD 3A 1F FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1254+ EEE0 FE 40                cp GRID
1255+ EEE2 D0                   ret nc
1256+ EEE3 3A D5 BA             ld a,(cx)
1257+ EEE6 D6 06                sub VIEW_R
1258+ EEE8 32 1E FB             ld (cell_i),a
1259+ EEEB CD 4D EF             call row_start
1260+ EEEE 3A 1E FB     .l:     ld a,(cell_i)
1261+ EEF1 21 D5 BA             ld hl,cx
1262+ EEF4 BE                   cp (hl)
1263+ EEF5 28 1F                jr z,.r
1264+ EEF7 CD 7A EF             call try_cell
1265+ EEFA 21 1E FB             ld hl,cell_i
1266+ EEFD 34                   inc (hl)
1267+ EEFE 2A 37 FB             ld hl,(row_x)                   ; one cell +x: (X,Z1) += (cosY,sinY)
1268+ EF01 ED 5B B3 BA          ld de,(cos_y)
1269+ EF05 19                   add hl,de
1270+ EF06 22 37 FB             ld (row_x),hl
1271+ EF09 2A 39 FB             ld hl,(row_z1)
1272+ EF0C ED 5B B1 BA          ld de,(sin_y)
1273+ EF10 19                   add hl,de
1274+ EF11 22 39 FB             ld (row_z1),hl
1275+ EF14 18 D8                jr .l
1276+ EF16 3A D5 BA     .r:     ld a,(cx)
1277+ EF19 C6 06                add a,VIEW_R
1278+ EF1B 32 1E FB             ld (cell_i),a
1279+ EF1E CD 4D EF             call row_start
1280+ EF21 3A 1E FB     .r2:    ld a,(cell_i)
1281+ EF24 21 D5 BA             ld hl,cx
1282+ EF27 BE                   cp (hl)
1283+ EF28 28 50                jr z,try_cell                   ; camera column last
1284+ EF2A CD 7A EF             call try_cell
1285+ EF2D 21 1E FB             ld hl,cell_i
1286+ EF30 35                   dec (hl)
1287+ EF31 2A 37 FB             ld hl,(row_x)                   ; one cell -x
1288+ EF34 ED 5B B3 BA          ld de,(cos_y)
1289+ EF38 B7                   or a
1290+ EF39 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1291+ EF3B 22 37 FB             ld (row_x),hl
1292+ EF3E 2A 39 FB             ld hl,(row_z1)
1293+ EF41 ED 5B B1 BA          ld de,(sin_y)
1294+ EF45 B7                   or a
1295+ EF46 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1296+ EF48 22 39 FB             ld (row_z1),hl
1297+ EF4B 18 D4                jr .r2
1298+ EF4D
1299+ EF4D              ; Yaw-rotated centre of cell (cell_i, cell_j) -> row_x, row_z1.  Moving one
1300+ EF4D              ; cell (256 units) in x then changes (X, Z1) by exactly the raw (cosY, sinY).
1301+ EF4D              row_start:
1302+ EF4D 3A 1E FB             ld a,(cell_i)
1303+ EF50 67                   ld h,a
1304+ EF51 2E 80                ld l,128
1305+ EF53 ED 5B 38 B6          ld de,(cam_x+1)
1306+ EF57 B7                   or a
1307+ EF58 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1308+ EF5A 22 2F FB             ld (t_dx),hl
1309+ EF5D 3A 1F FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1310+ EF60 67                   ld h,a
1311+ EF61 2E 80                ld l,128
1312+ EF63 ED 5B 3B B6          ld de,(cam_z+1)
1313+ EF67 B7                   or a
1314+ EF68 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1315+ EF6A 22 31 FB             ld (t_dz),hl
1316+ EF6D CD E8 F3             call rot_yaw
1317+ EF70 22 37 FB             ld (row_x),hl
1318+ EF73 2A 33 FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
1319+ EF76 22 39 FB             ld (row_z1),hl
1320+ EF79 C9                   ret
1321+ EF7A
1322+ EF7A              try_cell:
1323+ EF7A 3A 1E FB             ld a,(cell_i)
1324+ EF7D FE 40                cp GRID
1325+ EF7F D0                   ret nc
1326+ EF80                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
1327+ EF80 ~                    call vtick                      ; keep the FPS clock sampling
1328+ EF80                  ENDIF
1329+ EF80                      ; fall through
1330+ EF80
1331+ EF80              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1332+ EF80              ; Transform and draw the building in (cell_i, cell_j)
1333+ EF80              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1334+ EF80              draw_cell:
1335+ EF80 3A 1F FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1336+ EF83 6F                   ld l,a
1337+ EF84 26 00                ld h,0
1338+ EF86                      ROWOFS
1338+ EF86 29          >        add hl,hl
1338+ EF87 29          >        add hl,hl
1338+ EF88 29          >        add hl,hl
1338+ EF89 29          >        add hl,hl
1338+ EF8A 29          >        add hl,hl
1338+ EF8B 29          >        add hl,hl
1339+ EF8C 3A 1E FB             ld a,(cell_i)
1340+ EF8F ED 31                add hl,a
1341+ EF91 11 55 A0             ld de,city_map
1342+ EF94 19                   add hl,de
1343+ EF95 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1344+ EF96 B7                   or a
1345+ EF97 20 16                jr nz,.bld
1346+ EF99                      ; street: the zombies and humans standing there
1347+ EF99                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1348+ EF99 3A 3E FB             ld a,(occ_on)
1349+ EF9C B7                   or a
1350+ EF9D 28 08                jr z,.sdraw
1351+ EF9F E5                   push hl
1352+ EFA0 CD 54 F8             call occ_zombie                 ; level flight: list them
1353+ EFA3 E1                   pop hl
1354+ EFA4 C3 40 F8             jp occ_human
1355+ EFA7              .sdraw:
1356+ EFA7                  ENDIF
1357+ EFA7 E5                   push hl
1358+ EFA8 CD 0B 3A             call zombie_cell
1359+ EFAB E1                   pop hl
1360+ EFAC C3 85 74             jp human_cell
1361+ EFAF 4F           .bld:   ld c,a
1362+ EFB0 CD 74 74             call hum_here
1363+ EFB3 28 17                jr z,draw_bld                   ; nobody on the roof
1364+ EFB5                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
1365+ EFB5 3A 3E FB             ld a,(occ_on)
1366+ EFB8 B7                   or a
1367+ EFB9 28 09                jr z,.hdraw
1368+ EFBB C5                   push bc
1369+ EFBC E5                   push hl
1370+ EFBD CD 40 F8             call occ_human                  ; listed before the building, so
1371+ EFC0 E1                   pop hl                          ; drawn after it
1372+ EFC1 C1                   pop bc
1373+ EFC2 18 08                jr draw_bld
1374+ EFC4              .hdraw:
1375+ EFC4                  ENDIF
1376+ EFC4 E5                   push hl
1377+ EFC5 CD CC EF             call draw_bld                   ; the building, then the humans on
1378+ EFC8 E1                   pop hl                          ; its roof
1379+ EFC9 C3 85 74             jp human_cell
1380+ EFCC
1381+ EFCC              ; The building in the cell at HL (C = its map byte)
1382+ EFCC              draw_bld:
1383+ EFCC 11 AB BF             ld de,city_col-city_map         ; same index in city_col (page 7)
1384+ EFCF 19                   add hl,de
1385+ EFD0 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1386+ EFD1 E6 FC                and %11111100
1387+ EFD3 32 23 FB             ld (bld_col),a                  ; colour family (shade bits clear)
1388+ EFD6 79                   ld a,c
1389+ EFD7 CD BE B4             call bld_height
1390+ EFDA 22 20 FB             ld (bld_h),hl
1391+ EFDD                  IFDEF SKY_IS_WALL
1392+ EFDD                      ; Perimeter wall (outer row/column): everything above the horizon is
1393+ EFDD                      ; already wall-coloured, so draw it only up to camera height - its
1394+ EFDD                      ; top edge then lies exactly on the horizon line, at any pitch.
1395+ EFDD AF                   xor a
1396+ EFDE 32 22 FB             ld (bld_wall),a
1397+ EFE1 3A 1E FB             ld a,(cell_i)
1398+ EFE4 3C                   inc a
1399+ EFE5 E6 3E                and GRID-2                      ; zero for column 0 or GRID-1
1400+ EFE7 28 08                jr z,.wall
1401+ EFE9 3A 1F FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1402+ EFEC 3C                   inc a
1403+ EFED E6 3E                and GRID-2
1404+ EFEF 20 0B                jr nz,.nwall
1405+ EFF1 2A 3D B6     .wall:  ld hl,(cam_y)
1406+ EFF4 22 20 FB             ld (bld_h),hl
1407+ EFF7 3E 01                ld a,1
1408+ EFF9 32 22 FB             ld (bld_wall),a
1409+ EFFC              .nwall:
1410+ EFFC                  ENDIF
1411+ EFFC                      ; world corners: the building fills its cell
1412+ EFFC 3A 1E FB             ld a,(cell_i)
1413+ EFFF 67                   ld h,a
1414+ F000 2E 00                ld l,0
1415+ F002 22 27 FB             ld (bld_x0),hl
1416+ F005 24                   inc h
1417+ F006 22 29 FB             ld (bld_x1),hl
1418+ F009 3A 1F FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1419+ F00C 67                   ld h,a
1420+ F00D 22 2B FB             ld (bld_z0),hl
1421+ F010 24                   inc h
1422+ F011 22 2D FB             ld (bld_z1),hl
1423+ F014
1424+ F014 21 BD BA             ld hl,vecs0
1425+ F017 22 25 FB             ld (bld_vecs),hl
1426+ F01A 21 00 01             ld hl,256                       ; cull radius: 181 (half diagonal) * sqrt2
1427+ F01D 22 4D FB             ld (bld_r),hl
1428+ F020
1429+ F020                      ; yaw-space centre of this cell, kept up to date by draw_row
1430+ F020 2A 39 FB             ld hl,(row_z1)
1431+ F023 22 33 FB             ld (t_z1),hl
1432+ F026 2A 37 FB             ld hl,(row_x)
1433+ F029 22 35 FB             ld (t_x),hl
1434+ F02C                      ; Horizontal frustum cull, valid at any pitch: a visible point has
1435+ F02C                      ; |X|*c <= Z1 with c = cosP - 0.5|sinP|. Reject the bounding circle.
1436+ F02C CD F9 B5             call abs_hl
1437+ F02F ED 5B CD BA          ld de,(cull_c)
1438+ F033 CD FE B5             call fmul
1439+ F036 ED 5B 33 FB          ld de,(t_z1)

1491+ F183                      ; Back face culling is exact in world space for an axis aligned box.
1492+ F183                      ; C = visible faces: bit0 -X, bit1 +X, bit2 -Z, bit3 +Z, bit4 top

1540+ F1EA CD C3 F3             call nb_cover

1542+ F1EF CB 89                res 1,c
1543+ F1F1 CB 51        .w2:    bit 2,c
1544+ F1F3 28 0F                jr z,.w3

1546+ F1F8 47                   ld b,a
1547+ F1F9 3A 1F FB             ld a,(cell_j)
1548+ F1FC 3D                   dec a
1549+ F1FD CD C3 F3             call nb_cover

1551+ F202 CB 91                res 2,c
1552+ F204 CB 59        .w3:    bit 3,c
1553+ F206 28 0F                jr z,.w4

1555+ F20B 47                   ld b,a

1562+ F218 32 87 FB             ld (bld_vis),a

1564+ F21C C8                   ret z                           ; nothing of it can be seen

1570+ F229
1571+ F229                      ; outcodes, trivial reject, project the on-screen corners.  Each
1572+ F229                      ; bottom corner v (0-3) is done with the top corner above it (v+4).
1573+ F229 3E FF                ld a,$FF

1576+ F232 06 04                ld b,4

1598+ F262 21 50 FB             ld hl,bot_oc
1599+ F265 B6                   or (hl)
1600+ F266 20 11                jr nz,.full                     ; pitched, or bottom not projected
1601+ F268                      ; Level flight: the top corner has the bottom corner's X and Z, so
1602+ F268                      ; it shares its screen x and its 1/Z (still patched into
1603+ F268                      ; scale_comp); only the screen y is new.

1611+ F279                  ENDIF

1616+ F282 10 B0                djnz .ocl

1619+ F288 C0                   ret nz
1620+ F289
1621+ F289                      ; Shading: the roof is lighter; of the visible sides, the one the
1622+ F289                      ; camera sees most squarely (furthest from its plane) is the base
1623+ F289                      ; colour and the other is darker.
1624+ F289 3A 23 FB             ld a,(bld_col)

1637+ F2AA                      ; both an X and a Z side: compare camera distances to the planes

1675+ F2FA F6 01                or SHADE_FACE                   ; to match the wall-coloured sky

1677+ F2FF 32 8B FB             ld (col_z),a
1678+ F302              .nws:

1681+ F305 4F                   ld c,a
1682+ F306                      ; An edge shared with a face drawn later is left to that face:
1683+ F306                      ; X faces skip their top edge if the roof shows and the vertical
1684+ F306                      ; edge shared with a visible Z face; Z faces skip the top edge.

1686+ F308 CB 61                bit 4,c

1698+ F31F 32 88 FB             ld (bld_mx),a

1703+ F329                  ENDIF
1704+ F329
1705+ F329              ; Draw the visible faces of the building whose corners are at vtx_hi:00,
1706+ F329              ; from bld_vis, bld_mx, bld_mz, col_x, col_z and col_top
1707+ F329              draw_faces:

1738+ F37B 3A 89 FB             ld a,(bld_mz)

1754+ F3A4              ; IX -> vertex: outcode into (ix+6), ANDed into and_oc.  A = outcode.
1755+ F3A4              vtx_oc:
