1765+ F3B6
1766+ F3B6              ; IX -> vertex inside the frustum: project it into (ix+7) sx, (ix+8) sy
1767+ F3B6              vtx_proj:
1768+ F3B6 DD E5                push ix
1769+ F3B8 E1                   pop hl
1770+ F3B9 CD B0 E7             call project
1771+ F3BC DD 77 07             ld (ix+7),a
1772+ F3BF DD 71 08             ld (ix+8),c
1773+ F3C2 C9                   ret
1774+ F3C3
1775+ F3C3              ; B = i, A = j of a neighbouring cell (may be off the map) -> carry set if
1776+ F3C3              ; it holds a building at least as tall as this one (bld_h).  Keeps C.
1777+ F3C3              nb_cover:
1778+ F3C3 FE 40                cp GRID
1779+ F3C5 D0                   ret nc                          ; off the map (carry clear)
1780+ F3C6 6F                   ld l,a
1781+ F3C7 78                   ld a,b
1782+ F3C8 FE 40                cp GRID
1783+ F3CA D0                   ret nc
1784+ F3CB 26 00                ld h,0
1785+ F3CD                      ROWOFS
1785+ F3CD 29          >        add hl,hl
1785+ F3CE 29          >        add hl,hl
1785+ F3CF 29          >        add hl,hl
1785+ F3D0 29          >        add hl,hl
1785+ F3D1 29          >        add hl,hl
1785+ F3D2 29          >        add hl,hl
1786+ F3D3 ED 31                add hl,a
1787+ F3D5 11 55 A0             ld de,city_map
1788+ F3D8 19                   add hl,de
1789+ F3D9 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1790+ F3DA B7                   or a
1791+ F3DB C8                   ret z                           ; street (carry clear)
1792+ F3DC CD BE B4             call bld_height
1793+ F3DF ED 5B 20 FB          ld de,(bld_h)
1794+ F3E3 B7                   or a
1795+ F3E4 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1796+ F3E6 3F                   ccf                             ; carry = neighbour >= this building
1797+ F3E7 C9                   ret
1798+ F3E8
1799+ F3E8              ; Yaw rotation of (t_dx, t_dz):  Z1 = dx sinY + dz cosY -> t_z1
1800+ F3E8              ;                               X  = dx cosY - dz sinY -> HL
1801+ F3E8              rot_yaw:
1802+ F3E8 2A 2F FB             ld hl,(t_dx)
1803+ F3EB ED 5B B1 BA          ld de,(sin_y)
1804+ F3EF CD FE B5             call fmul
1805+ F3F2 E5                   push hl
1806+ F3F3 2A 31 FB             ld hl,(t_dz)
1807+ F3F6 ED 5B B3 BA          ld de,(cos_y)
1808+ F3FA CD FE B5             call fmul
1809+ F3FD D1                   pop de
1810+ F3FE 19                   add hl,de
1811+ F3FF 22 33 FB             ld (t_z1),hl
1812+ F402 2A 31 FB             ld hl,(t_dz)
1813+ F405 ED 5B B1 BA          ld de,(sin_y)
1814+ F409 CD FE B5             call fmul
1815+ F40C E5                   push hl
1816+ F40D 2A 2F FB             ld hl,(t_dx)
1817+ F410 ED 5B B3 BA          ld de,(cos_y)
1818+ F414 CD FE B5             call fmul
1819+ F417 D1                   pop de
1820+ F418 B7                   or a
1821+ F419 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1822+ F41B C9                   ret
1823+ F41C
1824+ F41C              ; Pitch rotation of a ground point at t_z1:
1825+ F41C              ;   HL = Y = yb - Z1 sinP     DE = Z = zb + Z1 cosP
1826+ F41C              rot_pitch:
1827+ F41C 2A 33 FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
1828+ F41F ED 5B B5 BA          ld de,(sin_p)
1829+ F423 CD FE B5             call fmul
1830+ F426 EB                   ex de,hl
1831+ F427 2A B9 BA             ld hl,(yb)
1832+ F42A B7                   or a
1833+ F42B ED 52                sbc hl,de
1834+ F42D E5                   push hl
1835+ F42E 2A 33 FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
1836+ F431 ED 5B B7 BA          ld de,(cos_p)
1837+ F435 CD FE B5             call fmul
1838+ F438 ED 5B BB BA          ld de,(zb)
1839+ F43C 19                   add hl,de
1840+ F43D EB                   ex de,hl
1841+ F43E E1                   pop hl
1842+ F43F C9                   ret
1843+ F440
1844+ F440              ;==============================================================================
1845+ F440              ; Street intersection markers - one dot per grid corner near the camera
1846+ F440              ;==============================================================================
1847+ F440              draw_dots:
1848+ F440 3A D6 BA             ld a,(cz)
1849+ F443 D6 05                sub DOT_R
1850+ F445 32 6D FB             ld (dot_j),a
1851+ F448 3E 0C                ld a,2*DOT_R+2
1852+ F44A 32 6E FB             ld (dot_rows),a
1853+ F44D 3A 6D FB     .row:   ld a,(dot_j)
1854+ F450 FE 41                cp GRID+1
1855+ F452 30 58                jr nc,.nrow
1856+ F454                      ; first corner of the row by full rotation, then step along x:
1857+ F454                      ; +256 world units in x adds exactly (cosY, sinY) to (X, Z1)
1858+ F454 3A D5 BA             ld a,(cx)
1859+ F457 D6 05                sub DOT_R
1860+ F459 32 6C FB             ld (dot_i),a
1861+ F45C 67                   ld h,a
1862+ F45D 2E 00                ld l,0
1863+ F45F ED 5B 38 B6          ld de,(cam_x+1)
1864+ F463 B7                   or a
1865+ F464 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1866+ F466 22 2F FB             ld (t_dx),hl
1867+ F469 3A 6D FB             ld a,(dot_j)
1868+ F46C 67                   ld h,a
1869+ F46D 2E 00                ld l,0
1870+ F46F ED 5B 3B B6          ld de,(cam_z+1)
1871+ F473 B7                   or a
1872+ F474 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1873+ F476 22 31 FB             ld (t_dz),hl
1874+ F479 CD E8 F3             call rot_yaw
1875+ F47C 22 35 FB             ld (t_x),hl
1876+ F47F 3E 0C                ld a,2*DOT_R+2
1877+ F481 32 6F FB             ld (dot_cols),a
1878+ F484 3A 6C FB     .col:   ld a,(dot_i)
1879+ F487 FE 41                cp GRID+1
1880+ F489 DC B7 F4             call c,plot_dot
1881+ F48C 2A 35 FB             ld hl,(t_x)
1882+ F48F ED 5B B3 BA          ld de,(cos_y)
1883+ F493 19                   add hl,de
1884+ F494 22 35 FB             ld (t_x),hl
1885+ F497 2A 33 FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
1886+ F49A ED 5B B1 BA          ld de,(sin_y)
1887+ F49E 19                   add hl,de
1888+ F49F 22 33 FB             ld (t_z1),hl
1889+ F4A2 21 6C FB             ld hl,dot_i
1890+ F4A5 34                   inc (hl)
1891+ F4A6 21 6F FB             ld hl,dot_cols
1892+ F4A9 35                   dec (hl)
1893+ F4AA 20 D8                jr nz,.col
1894+ F4AC 21 6D FB     .nrow:  ld hl,dot_j
1895+ F4AF 34                   inc (hl)
1896+ F4B0 21 6E FB             ld hl,dot_rows
1897+ F4B3 35                   dec (hl)
1898+ F4B4 20 97                jr nz,.row
1899+ F4B6 C9                   ret
1900+ F4B7
1901+ F4B7              ; Plot the ground point (t_x, t_z1)
1902+ F4B7              plot_dot:
1903+ F4B7 2A 35 FB             ld hl,(t_x)
1904+ F4BA 22 70 FB             ld (tmpv+0),hl
1905+ F4BD CD F9 B5             call abs_hl
1906+ F4C0 ED 5B CD BA          ld de,(cull_c)
1907+ F4C4 CD FE B5             call fmul
1908+ F4C7 ED 5B 33 FB          ld de,(t_z1)
1909+ F4CB B7                   or a
1910+ F4CC ED 52                sbc hl,de
1911+ F4CE F0                   ret p                           ; outside horizontal frustum / behind
1912+ F4CF CD 1C F4             call rot_pitch
1913+ F4D2 22 72 FB             ld (tmpv+2),hl
1914+ F4D5 ED 53 74 FB          ld (tmpv+4),de
1915+ F4D9 21 70 FB             ld hl,tmpv
1916+ F4DC CD 95 E8             call calc_oc
1917+ F4DF B7                   or a
1918+ F4E0 C0                   ret nz
1919+ F4E1 21 70 FB             ld hl,tmpv
1920+ F4E4 CD B0 E7             call project
1921+ F4E7 6F                   ld l,a
1922+ F4E8 79                   ld a,c
1923+ F4E9 CD 20 E7             call seg_map
1924+ F4EC 67                   ld h,a
1925+ F4ED 36 17                ld (hl),DOT_COL
1926+ F4EF C9                   ret
1927+ F4F0
1928+ F4F0              ;==============================================================================
1929+ F4F0              ; Rounds and the laser beam (`bullets`, laser_rec; moved by weapons.asm): a
1930+ F4F0              ; line along the path each covered this frame, from where it started to
1931+ F4F0              ; where it is.  A fresh round's path starts at the muzzle, below and behind
1932+ F4F0              ; the view, so its start is moved along the line to where it enters the
1933+ F4F0              ; view and pinned to that screen edge: the shot rises from the bottom row.
1934+ F4F0              ; Drawn over the city; a line whose end is outside the view is skipped.
1935+ F4F0              ;==============================================================================
1936+ F4F0              draw_bullets:
1937+ F4F0 DD 21 7D B6          ld ix,bullets                   ; the rounds, then the laser beam
1938+ F4F4 06 19                ld b,NBULLETS+1
1939+ F4F6 C5           .lp:    push bc
1940+ F4F7 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
1941+ F4FA B7                   or a
1942+ F4FB 28 4F                jr z,.nx
1943+ F4FD DD 7E 1A             ld a,(ix+B_KIND)
1944+ F500 FE 01                cp BK_ROCKET
1945+ F502 20 05                jr nz,.rnd
1946+ F504 CD 55 F5             call draw_rocket
1947+ F507 18 43                jr .nx
1948+ F509 DD 7E 00     .rnd:   ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
1949+ F50C FE 03                cp 3                            ; 3: laser beam
1950+ F50E 3E 1F                ld a,BULLET_COL
1951+ F510 20 02                jr nz,.col
1952+ F512 3E 23                ld a,LASER_COL
1953+ F514 32 66 FC     .col:   ld (line_col),a
1954+ F517 DD E5                push ix                         ; end: where it is now
1955+ F519 E1                   pop hl
1956+ F51A 23                   inc hl                          ; -> B_X, B_Y, B_Z
1957+ F51B CD 2D F6             call bul_cam
1958+ F51E 20 2C                jr nz,.nx                       ; not in view
1959+ F520 32 60 FB             ld (bl_x),a
1960+ F523 79                   ld a,c
1961+ F524 32 61 FB             ld (bl_y),a
1962+ F527 21 70 FB             ld hl,tmpv
1963+ F52A 11 62 FB             ld de,bh_cam
1964+ F52D 01 06 00             ld bc,6
1965+ F530 ED B0                ldir
1966+ F532 DD E5                push ix                         ; start of this frame's path
1967+ F534 E1                   pop hl
1968+ F535 11 0F 00             ld de,B_PX
1969+ F538 19                   add hl,de
1970+ F539 CD 2D F6             call bul_cam
1971+ F53C C4 93 F6             call nz,bul_clip                ; outside: where the line enters
1972+ F53F 5F                   ld e,a
1973+ F540 51                   ld d,c
1974+ F541 3A 60 FB             ld a,(bl_x)
1975+ F544 6F                   ld l,a
1976+ F545 3A 61 FB             ld a,(bl_y)
1977+ F548 67                   ld h,a
1978+ F549 CD D3 ED             call draw_line
1979+ F54C 11 1B 00     .nx:    ld de,BREC
1980+ F54F DD 19                add ix,de
1981+ F551 C1                   pop bc
1982+ F552 10 A2                djnz .lp
1983+ F554 C9                   ret
1984+ F555
1985+ F555              ; Rocket IX: a white streak ROCKET_LEN (1 m) long from its head back along
1986+ F555              ; its path, then a yellow trail to ROCKET_TRAIL (1.5 m) behind the head.
1987+ F555              ; Drawn only when every point drawn is in view (it's tiny: no clipping).
1988+ F555              draw_rocket:
1989+ F555 DD E5                push ix
1990+ F557 E1                   pop hl
1991+ F558 23                   inc hl                          ; head: B_X, B_Y, B_Z
1992+ F559 CD 2D F6             call bul_cam
1993+ F55C C0                   ret nz                          ; not in view
1994+ F55D 32 60 FB             ld (bl_x),a
1995+ F560 79                   ld a,c
1996+ F561 32 61 FB             ld (bl_y),a
1997+ F564 3E 0D                ld a,ROCKET_LEN                 ; the tail of the streak
1998+ F566 CD B3 F5             call rk_back
1999+ F569 CD 2D F6             call bul_cam
2000+ F56C 20 2D                jr nz,.head                     ; out of view: the head pixel only
2001+ F56E 32 58 FB             ld (rk_sx),a
2002+ F571 79                   ld a,c
2003+ F572 32 59 FB             ld (rk_sy),a
2004+ F575 3E 13                ld a,ROCKET_TRAIL               ; the end of the trail
2005+ F577 CD B3 F5             call rk_back
2006+ F57A CD 2D F6             call bul_cam
2007+ F57D 20 12                jr nz,.white
2008+ F57F 5F                   ld e,a                          ; yellow trail first, so the white
2009+ F580 51                   ld d,c                          ; streak stays on top
2010+ F581 3A 58 FB             ld a,(rk_sx)
2011+ F584 6F                   ld l,a
2012+ F585 3A 59 FB             ld a,(rk_sy)
2013+ F588 67                   ld h,a
2014+ F589 3E 1F                ld a,TRAIL_COL
2015+ F58B 32 66 FC             ld (line_col),a
2016+ F58E CD D3 ED             call draw_line
2017+ F591 3A 58 FB     .white: ld a,(rk_sx)
2018+ F594 5F                   ld e,a
2019+ F595 3A 59 FB             ld a,(rk_sy)
2020+ F598 57                   ld d,a
2021+ F599 18 08                jr .line
2022+ F59B 3A 60 FB     .head:  ld a,(bl_x)
2023+ F59E 5F                   ld e,a
2024+ F59F 3A 61 FB             ld a,(bl_y)
2025+ F5A2 57                   ld d,a
2026+ F5A3 3A 60 FB     .line:  ld a,(bl_x)
2027+ F5A6 6F                   ld l,a
2028+ F5A7 3A 61 FB             ld a,(bl_y)
2029+ F5AA 67                   ld h,a
2030+ F5AB 3E 0B                ld a,ROCKET_COL
2031+ F5AD 32 66 FC             ld (line_col),a
2032+ F5B0 C3 D3 ED             jp draw_line
2033+ F5B3
2034+ F5B3              ; A = distance d (units) -> HL -> rk_pt = rocket IX's head - d * (sin, slope,
2035+ F5B3              ; cos): d back along its path
2036+ F5B3              rk_back:
2037+ F5B3 32 57 FB             ld (rk_d),a
2038+ F5B6 DD 5E 07             ld e,(ix+B_SIN)
2039+ F5B9 DD 56 08             ld d,(ix+B_SIN+1)
2040+ F5BC 01 01 00             ld bc,B_X
2041+ F5BF CD E7 F5             call .ax
2042+ F5C2 22 51 FB             ld (rk_pt),hl
2043+ F5C5 DD 5E 0B             ld e,(ix+B_SLOPE)
2044+ F5C8 DD 56 0C             ld d,(ix+B_SLOPE+1)
2045+ F5CB 01 03 00             ld bc,B_Y
2046+ F5CE CD E7 F5             call .ax
2047+ F5D1 22 53 FB             ld (rk_pt+2),hl
2048+ F5D4 DD 5E 09             ld e,(ix+B_COS)
2049+ F5D7 DD 56 0A             ld d,(ix+B_COS+1)
2050+ F5DA 01 05 00             ld bc,B_Z
2051+ F5DD CD E7 F5             call .ax
2052+ F5E0 22 55 FB             ld (rk_pt+4),hl
2053+ F5E3 21 51 FB             ld hl,rk_pt
2054+ F5E6 C9                   ret
2055+ F5E7              ; DE = S7.8 component, BC = offset of the coordinate -> HL = coord - d * DE
2056+ F5E7 C5           .ax:    push bc
2057+ F5E8 3A 57 FB             ld a,(rk_d)
2058+ F5EB 6F                   ld l,a
2059+ F5EC 26 00                ld h,0
2060+ F5EE CD FE B5             call fmul
2061+ F5F1 EB                   ex de,hl
2062+ F5F2 DD E5                push ix
2063+ F5F4 E1                   pop hl
2064+ F5F5 C1                   pop bc
2065+ F5F6 09                   add hl,bc
2066+ F5F7 7E                   ld a,(hl)
2067+ F5F8 23                   inc hl
2068+ F5F9 66                   ld h,(hl)
2069+ F5FA 6F                   ld l,a
2070+ F5FB B7                   or a
2071+ F5FC ED 52                sbc hl,de
2072+ F5FE C9                   ret
2073+ F5FF
2074+ F5FF              ;==============================================================================
2075+ F5FF              ; Zombie burst particles: one pixel each, coloured by age (PART_COL0 + 4 per
2076+ F5FF              ; frame of age, bright to dark green), drawn over the city
2077+ F5FF              ;==============================================================================
2078+ F5FF              draw_particles:
2079+ F5FF DD 21 20 B9          ld ix,particles
2080+ F603 06 28                ld b,NPART
2081+ F605 C5           .lp:    push bc
2082+ F606 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)
2083+ F609 B7                   or a
2084+ F60A 28 18                jr z,.nx
2085+ F60C DD E5                push ix
2086+ F60E E1                   pop hl
2087+ F60F 23                   inc hl                          ; -> x, y, z
2088+ F610 CD 2D F6             call bul_cam
2089+ F613 20 0F                jr nz,.nx                       ; not in view
2090+ F615 6F                   ld l,a                          ; x

2092+ F617 CD 20 E7             call seg_map                    ; row's section -> A = y & 31
2093+ F61A 67                   ld h,a
2094+ F61B DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)                     ; colour: PART_COL0 + (age-1)*4
2095+ F61E 3D                   dec a
2096+ F61F 87                   add a,a
2097+ F620 87                   add a,a
2098+ F621 C6 3B                add a,PART_COL0
2099+ F623 77                   ld (hl),a
2100+ F624 11 0A 00     .nx:    ld de,PREC
2101+ F627 DD 19                add ix,de
2102+ F629 C1                   pop bc
2103+ F62A 10 D9                djnz .lp
2104+ F62C C9                   ret
2105+ F62D
2106+ F62D              ; HL -> world x, y, z: camera space into tmpv; A = outcode, Z set if in view
2107+ F62D              ; (then A = screen x, C = screen y)
2108+ F62D              bul_cam:
2109+ F62D 11 5A FB             ld de,bp_x
2110+ F630 01 06 00             ld bc,6
2111+ F633 ED B0                ldir
2112+ F635 2A 5A FB             ld hl,(bp_x)
2113+ F638 ED 5B 38 B6          ld de,(cam_x+1)
2114+ F63C B7                   or a
2115+ F63D ED 52                sbc hl,de
2116+ F63F 22 2F FB             ld (t_dx),hl
2117+ F642 2A 5E FB             ld hl,(bp_z)
2118+ F645 ED 5B 3B B6          ld de,(cam_z+1)
2119+ F649 B7                   or a
2120+ F64A ED 52                sbc hl,de
2121+ F64C 22 31 FB             ld (t_dz),hl
2122+ F64F CD E8 F3             call rot_yaw
2123+ F652 22 70 FB             ld (tmpv+0),hl
2124+ F655 CD 1C F4             call rot_pitch                  ; at ground level
2125+ F658 22 72 FB             ld (tmpv+2),hl
2126+ F65B ED 53 74 FB          ld (tmpv+4),de
2127+ F65F 2A 5C FB             ld hl,(bp_y)                    ; + height * (cosP, sinP)
2128+ F662 ED 5B B7 BA          ld de,(cos_p)
2129+ F666 CD FE B5             call fmul
2130+ F669 ED 5B 72 FB          ld de,(tmpv+2)
2131+ F66D 19                   add hl,de
2132+ F66E 22 72 FB             ld (tmpv+2),hl
2133+ F671 2A 5C FB             ld hl,(bp_y)
2134+ F674 ED 5B B5 BA          ld de,(sin_p)
2135+ F678 CD FE B5             call fmul
2136+ F67B ED 5B 74 FB          ld de,(tmpv+4)
2137+ F67F 19                   add hl,de
2138+ F680 22 74 FB             ld (tmpv+4),hl
2139+ F683 21 70 FB             ld hl,tmpv
2140+ F686 CD 95 E8             call calc_oc
2141+ F689 B7                   or a
2142+ F68A C0                   ret nz
2143+ F68B 21 70 FB             ld hl,tmpv
2144+ F68E CD B0 E7             call project
2145+ F691 BF                   cp a                            ; Z set
2146+ F692 C9                   ret
2147+ F693
2148+ F693              ; tmpv = the start, outside the planes in A; bh_cam = the end, in view.
2149+ F693              ; -> A = screen x, C = screen y where the line enters the view, pinned to
2150+ F693              ; the edge it crosses.
2151+ F693              bul_clip:
2152+ F693 DD E5                push ix
2153+ F695 32 68 FB             ld (bc_oc),a
2154+ F698 21 00 00             ld hl,0
2155+ F69B 22 69 FB             ld (bc_t),hl
2156+ F69E 0E 01                ld c,1                          ; each plane the start is outside
2157+ F6A0 3A 68 FB     .pl:    ld a,(bc_oc)
2158+ F6A3 A1                   and c
2159+ F6A4 28 34                jr z,.np
2160+ F6A6 79                   ld a,c
2161+ F6A7 32 90 FB             ld (clip_plane),a
2162+ F6AA C5                   push bc
2163+ F6AB DD 21 70 FB          ld ix,tmpv
2164+ F6AF CD DE E8             call plane_dist                 ; start: < 0
2165+ F6B2 E5                   push hl
2166+ F6B3 DD 21 62 FB          ld ix,bh_cam
2167+ F6B7 CD DE E8             call plane_dist                 ; end: >= 0
2168+ F6BA D1                   pop de
2169+ F6BB B7                   or a
2170+ F6BC ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; fe - fs
2171+ F6BE EB                   ex de,hl
2172+ F6BF CD F2 B5             call neg_hl                     ; -fs
2173+ F6C2 CD 83 E7             call div_frac                   ; t = -fs / (fe - fs)
2174+ F6C5 DD E5                push ix
2175+ F6C7 E1                   pop hl
2176+ F6C8 ED 5B 69 FB          ld de,(bc_t)                    ; the latest crossing counts
2177+ F6CC B7                   or a
2178+ F6CD ED 52                sbc hl,de
2179+ F6CF C1                   pop bc
2180+ F6D0 38 08                jr c,.np
2181+ F6D2 19                   add hl,de
2182+ F6D3 22 69 FB             ld (bc_t),hl
2183+ F6D6 79                   ld a,c
2184+ F6D7 32 6B FB             ld (bc_pl),a
2185+ F6DA CB 21        .np:    sla c
2186+ F6DC 79                   ld a,c
2187+ F6DD FE 20                cp 32
2188+ F6DF 20 BF                jr nz,.pl
2189+ F6E1 DD 21 70 FB          ld ix,tmpv                      ; start += t * (end - start)
2190+ F6E5 06 03                ld b,3
2191+ F6E7 C5           .lc:    push bc
2192+ F6E8 DD 6E F2             ld l,(ix+bh_cam-tmpv)           ; end - start
2193+ F6EB DD 66 F3             ld h,(ix+bh_cam-tmpv+1)
2194+ F6EE DD 5E 00             ld e,(ix+0)
2195+ F6F1 DD 56 01             ld d,(ix+1)
2196+ F6F4 B7                   or a
2197+ F6F5 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2198+ F6F7 D5                   push de
2199+ F6F8 ED 5B 69 FB          ld de,(bc_t)
2200+ F6FC CD 50 E7             call mul_frac
2201+ F6FF D1                   pop de
2202+ F700 19                   add hl,de
2203+ F701 DD 75 00             ld (ix+0),l
2204+ F704 DD 74 01             ld (ix+1),h
2205+ F707 DD 23                inc ix
2206+ F709 DD 23                inc ix
2207+ F70B C1                   pop bc
2208+ F70C 10 D9                djnz .lc
2209+ F70E DD E1                pop ix
2210+ F710 21 70 FB             ld hl,tmpv
2211+ F713 CD B0 E7             call project
2212+ F716 5F                   ld e,a
2213+ F717 3A 6B FB             ld a,(bc_pl)                    ; pin to the edge it crossed
2214+ F71A FE 10                cp 16
2215+ F71C 20 02                jr nz,.t
2216+ F71E 0E 7F                ld c,VIEW_H-1                   ; bottom
2217+ F720 FE 08        .t:     cp 8
2218+ F722 20 02                jr nz,.l
2219+ F724 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; top
2220+ F726 FE 02        .l:     cp 2
2221+ F728 20 02                jr nz,.r
2222+ F72A 1E 00                ld e,0                          ; left
2223+ F72C FE 04        .r:     cp 4
2224+ F72E 20 02                jr nz,.d
2225+ F730 1E FF                ld e,255                        ; right
2226+ F732 7B           .d:     ld a,e
2227+ F733 C9                   ret
2228+ F734
2229+ F734              ;==============================================================================
2230+ F734              ; Gunsight in the centre of the Layer 2 viewport
2231+ F734              ;==============================================================================
2232+ F734              draw_gunsight:
2233+ F734 3E 0F                ld a,HUD_COL
2234+ F736 32 66 FC             ld (line_col),a
2235+ F739 21 7A 40             ld hl,VIEW_CY*256+122
2236+ F73C 11 7D 40             ld de,VIEW_CY*256+125
2237+ F73F CD D3 ED             call draw_line
2238+ F742 21 83 40             ld hl,VIEW_CY*256+131
2239+ F745 11 86 40             ld de,VIEW_CY*256+134
2240+ F748 CD D3 ED             call draw_line
2241+ F74B 21 80 3A             ld hl,(VIEW_CY-6)*256+128
2242+ F74E 11 80 3D             ld de,(VIEW_CY-3)*256+128
2243+ F751 CD D3 ED             call draw_line
2244+ F754 21 80 43             ld hl,(VIEW_CY+3)*256+128
2245+ F757 11 80 46             ld de,(VIEW_CY+6)*256+128
2246+ F75A C3 D3 ED             jp draw_line
2247+ F75D
2248+ F75D                  IFDEF OCCLUSION_CULL
2249+ F75D              ;==============================================================================
2250+ F75D              ; Occlusion culling (OCCLUSION_CULL, level flight only)
2251+ F75D              ;
2252+ F75D              ; In level flight every screen column is a vertical slice of the world through
2253+ F75D              ; the camera.  The cells are walked in exactly the reverse of the painter's
2254+ F75D              ; order, i.e. front to back.  Each building is transformed as usual, then
2255+ F75D              ; tested against OCC_YBUF, which holds per column the row from which the
2256+ F75D              ; screen is known to be covered down to the ground:
2257+ F75D              ;   * hidden - in every column it spans, its highest point is no higher than
2258+ F75D              ;     that row - it is dropped;
2259+ F75D              ;   * otherwise it lowers the row across its columns to its lowest top
2260+ F75D              ;     corner, and its corners and face settings are kept in a record.
2261+ F75D              ; The kept buildings and the zombie cells are then drawn back to front.
2262+ F75D              ; This is safe because buildings that share a column meet the walk front to
2263+ F75D              ; back (the grid order guarantees it), the camera is above the ground (what
2264+ F75D              ; is behind sits higher on screen), the cover row is the lowest top corner
2265+ F75D              ; (an underestimate) and bounds are clamped inwards to the screen.  A
2266+ F75D              ; building with a corner behind the camera is never dropped and never covers.
2267+ F75D              ;==============================================================================
2268+ F75D              OCC_YBUF        equ $4000       ; per column: covered from this row down
2269+ F75D              OCC_LIST        equ $4100       ; kept buildings and zombie cells, 2 bytes each
2270+ F75D              OCC_REC0        equ $42         ; first record (high byte); 256 bytes each,
2271+ F75D              OCC_RECN        equ $60         ; up to $5F: 30 records ($6000 on: city_col)
2272+ F75D              ; Record: copy of vtx (bytes 0-8 of each 16), then
2273+ F75D              OR_VIS          equ 128         ; bld_vis, bld_mx, bld_mz, col_x, col_z, col_top
2274+ F75D              ; A list entry is a record's high byte (< $60), a cell with humans as its
2275+ F75D              ; city_col address ($6000-$6FFF) or a cell with zombies as its city_map
2276+ F75D              ; address (>= $8000).
2277+ F75D
2278+ F75D              ; Start of the city: occlusion on only in level flight
2279+ F75D              occ_begin:
2280+ F75D AF                   xor a
2281+ F75E 32 3E FB             ld (occ_on),a
2282+ F761 32 3F FB             ld (occ_full),a
2283+ F764 3A 42 B6             ld a,(pitch)
2284+ F767 B7                   or a
2285+ F768 C0                   ret nz
2286+ F769 3C                   inc a
2287+ F76A 32 3E FB             ld (occ_on),a
2288+ F76D 21 00 41             ld hl,OCC_LIST
2289+ F770 22 40 FB             ld (occ_lp),hl
2290+ F773 3E 42                ld a,OCC_REC0
2291+ F775 32 42 FB             ld (occ_rec),a
2292+ F778 21 00 40             ld hl,OCC_YBUF                  ; nothing covered yet
2293+ F77B 36 80                ld (hl),VIEW_H
2294+ F77D 11 01 40             ld de,OCC_YBUF+1
2295+ F780 01 FF 00             ld bc,255
2296+ F783 ED B0                ldir
2297+ F785 C9                   ret
2298+ F786
2299+ F786              ; The cells of draw_cells in exactly the reverse order.  Each half row
2300+ F786              ; starts from the same cell as draw_row and steps the same way, so every
2301+ F786              ; cell gets bit-identical yaw-space coordinates.
2302+ F786              occ_cells:
2303+ F786 3A D6 BA             ld a,(cz)
2304+ F789 32 1F FB             ld (cell_j),a
2305+ F78C CD B0 F7             call occ_row                    ; camera row first
2306+ F78F 06 06                ld b,VIEW_R
2307+ F791 C5           .f:     push bc
2308+ F792 21 1F FB             ld hl,cell_j
2309+ F795 34                   inc (hl)
2310+ F796 CD B0 F7             call occ_row                    ; then cz+1 .. cz+R
2311+ F799 C1                   pop bc
2312+ F79A 10 F5                djnz .f
2313+ F79C 3A D6 BA             ld a,(cz)
2314+ F79F 32 1F FB             ld (cell_j),a
2315+ F7A2 06 06                ld b,VIEW_R
2316+ F7A4 C5           .n:     push bc
2317+ F7A5 21 1F FB             ld hl,cell_j
2318+ F7A8 35                   dec (hl)
2319+ F7A9 CD B0 F7             call occ_row                    ; then cz-1 .. cz-R
2320+ F7AC C1                   pop bc
2321+ F7AD 10 F5                djnz .n
2322+ F7AF C9                   ret
2323+ F7B0
2324+ F7B0              occ_row:
2325+ F7B0 3A 1F FB             ld a,(cell_j)
2326+ F7B3 FE 40                cp GRID
2327+ F7B5 D0                   ret nc
2328+ F7B6                      ; right half, as draw_row: start at cx+R, step down to cx
2329+ F7B6 3A D5 BA             ld a,(cx)
2330+ F7B9 C6 06                add a,VIEW_R
2331+ F7BB 32 1E FB             ld (cell_i),a
2332+ F7BE CD 4D EF             call row_start
2333+ F7C1 06 06                ld b,VIEW_R
2334+ F7C3 CD 25 F8     .d:     call occ_stepm
2335+ F7C6 10 FB                djnz .d
2336+ F7C8 3A D5 BA             ld a,(cx)
2337+ F7CB 32 1E FB             ld (cell_i),a
2338+ F7CE CD 7A EF             call try_cell                   ; camera column first
2339+ F7D1 06 06                ld b,VIEW_R
2340+ F7D3 C5           .r:     push bc
2341+ F7D4 21 1E FB             ld hl,cell_i
2342+ F7D7 34                   inc (hl)
2343+ F7D8 CD 0E F8             call occ_stepp
2344+ F7DB CD 7A EF             call try_cell                   ; then cx+1 .. cx+R
2345+ F7DE C1                   pop bc
2346+ F7DF 10 F2                djnz .r
2347+ F7E1                      ; left half, as draw_row: start at cx-R, step up to cx-1
2348+ F7E1 3A D5 BA             ld a,(cx)
2349+ F7E4 D6 06                sub VIEW_R
2350+ F7E6 32 1E FB             ld (cell_i),a
2351+ F7E9 CD 4D EF             call row_start
2352+ F7EC 06 05                ld b,VIEW_R-1
2353+ F7EE CD 0E F8     .u:     call occ_stepp
2354+ F7F1 10 FB                djnz .u
2355+ F7F3 3A D5 BA             ld a,(cx)
2356+ F7F6 3D                   dec a
2357+ F7F7 32 1E FB             ld (cell_i),a
2358+ F7FA CD 7A EF             call try_cell                   ; then cx-1 .. cx-R
2359+ F7FD 06 05                ld b,VIEW_R-1
2360+ F7FF C5           .l:     push bc
2361+ F800 21 1E FB             ld hl,cell_i
2362+ F803 35                   dec (hl)
2363+ F804 CD 25 F8             call occ_stepm
2364+ F807 CD 7A EF             call try_cell
2365+ F80A C1                   pop bc
2366+ F80B 10 F2                djnz .l
2367+ F80D C9                   ret
2368+ F80E
2369+ F80E              ; One cell +x / -x in yaw space: (X, Z1) +/-= (cosY, sinY).  Keeps B.
2370+ F80E              occ_stepp:
2371+ F80E 2A 37 FB             ld hl,(row_x)
2372+ F811 ED 5B B3 BA          ld de,(cos_y)
2373+ F815 19                   add hl,de
2374+ F816 22 37 FB             ld (row_x),hl
2375+ F819 2A 39 FB             ld hl,(row_z1)
2376+ F81C ED 5B B1 BA          ld de,(sin_y)
2377+ F820 19                   add hl,de
2378+ F821 22 39 FB             ld (row_z1),hl
2379+ F824 C9                   ret
2380+ F825              occ_stepm:
2381+ F825 2A 37 FB             ld hl,(row_x)
2382+ F828 ED 5B B3 BA          ld de,(cos_y)
2383+ F82C B7                   or a
2384+ F82D ED 52                sbc hl,de
2385+ F82F 22 37 FB             ld (row_x),hl
2386+ F832 2A 39 FB             ld hl,(row_z1)
2387+ F835 ED 5B B1 BA          ld de,(sin_y)
2388+ F839 B7                   or a
2389+ F83A ED 52                sbc hl,de
2390+ F83C 22 39 FB             ld (row_z1),hl
2391+ F83F C9                   ret
2392+ F840
2393+ F840              ; HL -> a city_map byte: list the cell (as its city_col address) if humans
2394+ F840              ; stand there
2395+ F840              occ_human:
2396+ F840 CD 74 74             call hum_here
2397+ F843 C8                   ret z
2398+ F844 11 AB BF             ld de,city_col-city_map
2399+ F847 19                   add hl,de
2400+ F848 EB                   ex de,hl
2401+ F849 2A 40 FB             ld hl,(occ_lp)
2402+ F84C 73                   ld (hl),e
2403+ F84D 2C                   inc l
2404+ F84E 72                   ld (hl),d
2405+ F84F 2C                   inc l
2406+ F850 22 40 FB             ld (occ_lp),hl
2407+ F853 C9                   ret
2408+ F854
2409+ F854              ; Empty cell, HL -> its city_map byte: list it if zombies stand there
2410+ F854              occ_zombie:
2411+ F854 E5                   push hl
2412+ F855 11 AB 7F             ld de,zcell-city_map
2413+ F858 19                   add hl,de
2414+ F859 7E                   ld a,(hl)
2415+ F85A E1                   pop hl
2416+ F85B B7                   or a
2417+ F85C C8                   ret z
2418+ F85D EB                   ex de,hl
2419+ F85E 2A 40 FB             ld hl,(occ_lp)
2420+ F861 73                   ld (hl),e
2421+ F862 2C                   inc l
2422+ F863 72                   ld (hl),d
2423+ F864 2C                   inc l
2424+ F865 22 40 FB             ld (occ_lp),hl
2425+ F868 C9                   ret
2426+ F869
2427+ F869              ; draw_cell tail in level flight: drop the building if it is hidden,
2428+ F869              ; otherwise make it a cover and keep it (corners in vtx, face settings)
2429+ F869              occ_record:
2430+ F869 3A 3F FB             ld a,(occ_full)
2431+ F86C B7                   or a
2432+ F86D C0                   ret nz
2433+ F86E 3A 42 FB             ld a,(occ_rec)
2434+ F871 FE 60                cp OCC_RECN
2435+ F873 38 06                jr c,.room
2436+ F875 3E 01                ld a,1                          ; out of records: give up this frame
2437+ F877 32 3F FB             ld (occ_full),a
2438+ F87A C9                   ret
2439+ F87B 32 43 FB     .room:  ld (occ_cur),a
2440+ F87E                      ; screen bounds of the corners of the drawn faces
2441+ F87E AF                   xor a
2442+ F87F 32 46 FB             ld (o_xmax),a
2443+ F882 32 48 FB             ld (o_tcov),a
2444+ F885 3D                   dec a
2445+ F886 32 45 FB             ld (o_xmin),a
2446+ F889 32 47 FB             ld (o_ymin),a
2447+ F88C 3A 3D FB             ld a,(bld_used0)
2448+ F88F 32 44 FB             ld (o_used),a
2449+ F892 DD 21 00 E0          ld ix,vtx
2450+ F896 06 08                ld b,8
2451+ F898 C5           .cl:    push bc
2452+ F899 3A 44 FB             ld a,(o_used)
2453+ F89C 0F                   rrca
2454+ F89D 32 44 FB             ld (o_used),a
2455+ F8A0 30 40                jr nc,.nx                       ; corner not on a drawn face
2456+ F8A2 DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
2457+ F8A5 CB 47                bit 0,a
2458+ F8A7 20 47                jr nz,.keep                     ; behind the near plane: no bounds
2459+ F8A9 B7                   or a
2460+ F8AA C4 B6 F3             call nz,vtx_proj                ; off screen: clamped projection
2461+ F8AD DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)                     ; sx
2462+ F8B0 21 45 FB             ld hl,o_xmin
2463+ F8B3 BE                   cp (hl)
2464+ F8B4 30 01                jr nc,.x1
2465+ F8B6 77                   ld (hl),a
2466+ F8B7 21 46 FB     .x1:    ld hl,o_xmax
2467+ F8BA BE                   cp (hl)
2468+ F8BB 38 01                jr c,.x2
2469+ F8BD 77                   ld (hl),a
2470+ F8BE DD 7E 08     .x2:    ld a,(ix+8)                     ; sy
2471+ F8C1 21 47 FB             ld hl,o_ymin
2472+ F8C4 BE                   cp (hl)
2473+ F8C5 30 01                jr nc,.y1
2474+ F8C7 77                   ld (hl),a
2475+ F8C8 C1           .y1:    pop bc
2476+ F8C9 C5                   push bc
2477+ F8CA 78                   ld a,b
2478+ F8CB FE 05                cp 5
2479+ F8CD 30 13                jr nc,.nx                       ; corners 0-3 are the bottom ones
2480+ F8CF DD 7E 06             ld a,(ix+6)
2481+ F8D2 E6 10                and 16
2482+ F8D4 3E 80                ld a,VIEW_H                     ; top corner below the screen:
2483+ F8D6 20 03                jr nz,.tc                       ; covers nothing
2484+ F8D8 DD 7E 08             ld a,(ix+8)
2485+ F8DB 21 48 FB     .tc:    ld hl,o_tcov
2486+ F8DE BE                   cp (hl)
2487+ F8DF 38 01                jr c,.nx
2488+ F8E1 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; lowest top corner
2489+ F8E2 11 10 00     .nx:    ld de,16
2490+ F8E5 DD 19                add ix,de
2491+ F8E7 C1                   pop bc
2492+ F8E8 10 AE                djnz .cl
2493+ F8EA CD 84 FA             call occ_test
2494+ F8ED D8                   ret c                           ; hidden: dropped
2495+ F8EE 18 01                jr .store
2496+ F8F0 C1           .keep:  pop bc                          ; no bounds: keep, but no cover
2497+ F8F1 3A 43 FB     .store: ld a,(occ_cur)
2498+ F8F4 57                   ld d,a
2499+ F8F5 1E 00                ld e,0
2500+ F8F7 21 00 E0             ld hl,vtx
2501+ F8FA                      ; corners: only bytes 0-8 of each 16 are used (X,Y,Z,oc,sx,sy);
2502+ F8FA                      ; vtx and the record are both page aligned, so L = E throughout
2503+ F8FA                      REPT 8
2504+ F8FA ED A0       >        ldi
2505+ F8FC ED A0       >        ldi

2530+ F9CD 34                   inc (hl)

2532+ F9CF
2533+ F9CF              ; Before the transform: rough screen bounds of the building from its cell
2534+ F9CF              ; centre (t_x, t_z1; at pitch 0 camera Z = Z1), a circle of radius OCC_R
2535+ F9CF              ; around it and its height, each widened by a pixel for rounding.
2536+ F9CF              ; Carry set if those bounds are already hidden.
2537+ F9CF              OCC_R           equ 188             ; half the cell diagonal (181) + rounding
2538+ F9CF              occ_pretest:
2539+ F9CF 3A 3F FB             ld a,(occ_full)
2540+ F9D2 B7                   or a
2541+ F9D3 C0                   ret nz                          ; not keeping any more: no point
2542+ F9D4 2A 33 FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
2543+ F9D7 11 34 FF             ld de,-OCC_R-NEAR
2544+ F9DA 19                   add hl,de
2545+ F9DB CB 7C                bit 7,h
2546+ F9DD C2 65 FA             jp nz,.no                       ; may reach the near plane
2547+ F9E0 2A 33 FB             ld hl,(t_z1)
2548+ F9E3 11 44 FF             ld de,-OCC_R
2549+ F9E6 19                   add hl,de
2550+ F9E7 22 49 FB             ld (o_zmin),hl
2551+ F9EA 11 78 01             ld de,2*OCC_R
2552+ F9ED 19                   add hl,de
2553+ F9EE 22 4B FB             ld (o_zmax),hl
2554+ F9F1                      ; left edge: X-R, over the far Z if positive, the near Z if negative
2555+ F9F1 2A 35 FB             ld hl,(t_x)
2556+ F9F4 11 44 FF             ld de,-OCC_R
2557+ F9F7 19                   add hl,de
2558+ F9F8 ED 5B 4B FB          ld de,(o_zmax)
2559+ F9FC CB 7C                bit 7,h
2560+ F9FE 28 04                jr z,.l1
2561+ FA00 ED 5B 49 FB          ld de,(o_zmin)
2562+ FA04 CD 67 FA     .l1:    call occ_sx
2563+ FA07 B7                   or a
2564+ FA08 28 01                jr z,.l2
2565+ FA0A 3D                   dec a
2566+ FA0B 32 45 FB     .l2:    ld (o_xmin),a
2567+ FA0E                      ; right edge: X+R, over the near Z if positive, the far Z if negative
2568+ FA0E 2A 35 FB             ld hl,(t_x)
2569+ FA11 11 BC 00             ld de,OCC_R
2570+ FA14 19                   add hl,de
2571+ FA15 ED 5B 49 FB          ld de,(o_zmin)
2572+ FA19 CB 7C                bit 7,h
2573+ FA1B 28 04                jr z,.r1
2574+ FA1D ED 5B 4B FB          ld de,(o_zmax)
2575+ FA21 CD 67 FA     .r1:    call occ_sx
2576+ FA24 3C                   inc a
2577+ FA25 20 01                jr nz,.r2
2578+ FA27 3D                   dec a
2579+ FA28 32 46 FB     .r2:    ld (o_xmax),a
2580+ FA2B                      ; top: height above the eye over the near Z, or below it over the far Z
2581+ FA2B 2A 20 FB             ld hl,(bld_h)
2582+ FA2E ED 5B 3D B6          ld de,(cam_y)
2583+ FA32 B7                   or a
2584+ FA33 ED 52                sbc hl,de
2585+ FA35 38 14                jr c,.below
2586+ FA37 E5                   push hl                         ; (zpatch uses DE)
2587+ FA38 2A 49 FB             ld hl,(o_zmin)
2588+ FA3B CD 01 E8             call zpatch
2589+ FA3E E1                   pop hl
2590+ FA3F CD 45 E8             call scale_comp                 ; A = 128 * (h - camY) / Zmin
2591+ FA42 4F                   ld c,a
2592+ FA43 3E 3F                ld a,VIEW_CY-1
2593+ FA45 91                   sub c
2594+ FA46 30 17                jr nc,.ty
2595+ FA48 AF                   xor a
2596+ FA49 18 14                jr .ty
2597+ FA4B CD F2 B5     .below: call neg_hl                     ; camY - h
2598+ FA4E E5                   push hl
2599+ FA4F 2A 4B FB             ld hl,(o_zmax)
2600+ FA52 CD 01 E8             call zpatch
2601+ FA55 E1                   pop hl
2602+ FA56 CD 45 E8             call scale_comp                 ; A = 128 * (camY - h) / Zmax
2603+ FA59 C6 3F                add a,VIEW_CY-1
2604+ FA5B 30 02                jr nc,.ty
2605+ FA5D 3E FF                ld a,255
2606+ FA5F 32 47 FB     .ty:    ld (o_ymin),a
2607+ FA62 C3 9B FA             jp occ_hidden
2608+ FA65 B7           .no:    or a
2609+ FA66 C9                   ret
2610+ FA67
2611+ FA67              ; HL = X, DE = Z -> A = screen x, clamped to 0..255
2612+ FA67              occ_sx:
2613+ FA67 7C                   ld a,h
2614+ FA68 F5                   push af
2615+ FA69 E5                   push hl
2616+ FA6A EB                   ex de,hl
2617+ FA6B CD 01 E8             call zpatch
2618+ FA6E E1                   pop hl
2619+ FA6F CD 45 E8             call scale_comp
2620+ FA72 4F                   ld c,a
2621+ FA73 F1                   pop af
2622+ FA74 17                   rla
2623+ FA75 38 07                jr c,.n
2624+ FA77 3E 80                ld a,128
2625+ FA79 81                   add a,c
2626+ FA7A D0                   ret nc
2627+ FA7B 3E FF                ld a,255
2628+ FA7D C9                   ret
2629+ FA7E 3E 80        .n:     ld a,128
2630+ FA80 91                   sub c
2631+ FA81 D0                   ret nc
2632+ FA82 AF                   xor a
2633+ FA83 C9                   ret
2634+ FA84
2635+ FA84              ; Bounds o_xmin/o_xmax/o_ymin against the cover buffer: carry set if hidden
2636+ FA84              ; in every column; otherwise lower the cover to o_tcov over those columns.
2637+ FA84              occ_test:

2639+ FA87 D8                   ret c
2640+ FA88 3A 48 FB             ld a,(o_tcov)
2641+ FA8B FE 80                cp VIEW_H

2643+ FA8F 4F                   ld c,a
2644+ FA90 6B                   ld l,e
2645+ FA91 7E           .u:     ld a,(hl)
2646+ FA92 B9                   cp c
2647+ FA93 38 01                jr c,.un                        ; already covered higher up
2648+ FA95 71                   ld (hl),c
2649+ FA96 2C           .un:    inc l
2650+ FA97 10 F8                djnz .u
2651+ FA99 B7           .none:  or a                            ; visible (carry clear)
2652+ FA9A C9                   ret
2653+ FA9B
2654+ FA9B              ; Carry set if o_xmin..o_xmax are all covered from o_ymin or higher.
2655+ FA9B              ; Leaves E = o_xmin, B = column count, H = high OCC_YBUF.
2656+ FA9B              occ_hidden:

2659+ FA9F 3A 46 FB             ld a,(o_xmax)

2662+ FAA4 47                   ld b,a                          ; columns (0 = all 256)

2665+ FAA9 4F                   ld c,a                          ; hidden where cover <= ymin

2668+ FAAD C5                   push bc
2669+ FAAE 7E           .t:     ld a,(hl)
2670+ FAAF B9                   cp c

2673+ FAB3 10 F9                djnz .t
2674+ FAB5 C1                   pop bc
2675+ FAB6 37                   scf
2676+ FAB7 C9                   ret
2677+ FAB8 C1           .vis:   pop bc
2678+ FAB9 B7                   or a
2679+ FABA C9                   ret

 116+ E096 36 0C        .dv:    ld (hl),P_PANEL<<3 | C_OK
 117+ E098 23                   inc hl
 118+ E099 10 FB                djnz .dv
 119+ E09B 21 00 90             ld hl,ULA_BMP+$1000             ; char row 16, pixel line 0
 120+ E09E 06 20                ld b,32
 121+ E0A0 36 FF        .dl:    ld (hl),$FF
 122+ E0A2 23                   inc hl
 123+ E0A3 10 FB                djnz .dl
 124+ E0A5
 125+ E0A5 3E FF                ld a,$FF                        ; the HUD's "last drawn" values:
 126+ E0A7 32 1B E3             ld (pax_shown),a                ; none, so everything is drawn
 127+ E0AA 32 1C E3             ld (wsel_shown),a
 128+ E0AD 3E 0C                ld a,GLYPH_S                    ; gauge labels: S (speed) and
 129+ E0AF 11 E1 90             ld de,ULA_ROW_LBL+SPD_GCOL      ; A (altitude | ground)
 130+ E0B2 CD 02 E0             call print_glyph_at
 131+ E0B5 3E 0F                ld a,GLYPH_A
 132+ E0B7 11 E3 90             ld de,ULA_ROW_LBL+ALT_GCOL
 133+ E0BA CD 02 E0             call print_glyph_at
 134+ E0BD 21 21 9A             ld hl,ULA_ATTR + 17*32 + SPD_GCOL  ; the gauges' cells: transparent,
 135+ E0C0 06 06                ld b,GAUGE_H/8                  ; showing the Layer 2 bars
 136+ E0C2 36 00        .ga:    ld (hl),0
 137+ E0C4 3E 02                ld a,ALT_GCOL-SPD_GCOL
 138+ E0C6 ED 31                add hl,a
 139+ E0C8 36 00                ld (hl),0
 140+ E0CA 3E 1E                ld a,32-(ALT_GCOL-SPD_GCOL)
 141+ E0CC ED 31                add hl,a
 142+ E0CE 10 F2                djnz .ga
 143+ E0D0 21 23 E3             ld hl,lbl_wpn                   ; "WPN:" and the slot digits
 144+ E0D3 11 2B 90             ld de,ULA_ROW_PAX+WPN_LBL
 145+ E0D6 CD 3A E2             call print_label
 146+ E0D9 3E 18                ld a,GLYPH_COLON
 147+ E0DB 11 2E 90             ld de,ULA_ROW_PAX+WPN_LBL+3
 148+ E0DE CD 02 E0             call print_glyph_at
 149+ E0E1 0E 00                ld c,0
 150+ E0E3 79           .wd:    ld a,c
 151+ E0E4 C5                   push bc
 152+ E0E5 06 00                ld b,0
 153+ E0E7 CD DF E1             call wpn_digit
 154+ E0EA C1                   pop bc
 155+ E0EB 0C                   inc c
 156+ E0EC 79                   ld a,c
 157+ E0ED FE 05                cp WPN_SLOTS
 158+ E0EF 20 F2                jr nz,.wd
 159+ E0F1 CD 59 77             call map_init
 160+ E0F4 CD F8 E0             call compass_init
 161+ E0F7                  IFDEF DRAW_FPS
 162+ E0F7 ~                    jp fps_draw                     ; initial " 0 FPS"
 163+ E0F7                  ELSE
 164+ E0F7 C9                   ret
 165+ E0F8                  ENDIF
 166+ E0F8
 167+ E0F8              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 168+ E0F8              ; Compass: a horizontal strip, 32x8 pixels in a 2-pixel frame, centred on the
 169+ E0F8              ; current heading.  Layer 2 (per-pixel colour: red N/E/S/W, yellow NE/SE/
 170+ E0F8              ; SW/NW) under transparent ULA cells.  compass_tape (gentables.py) covers a
 171+ E0F8              ; full turn at 4 yaw units a pixel; each frame the 32 columns centred on yaw
 172+ E0F8              ; are copied into the back buffer.
 173+ E0F8              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 174+ E0F8              ; New game: the ULA cells over the compass transparent (paper 0, no ink),
 175+ E0F8              ; and the frame and background drawn into both Layer 2 buffers
 176+ E0F8              compass_init:
 177+ E0F8 21 6F 9A             ld hl,ULA_ATTR + 19*32 + 15
 178+ E0FB 06 06                ld b,6
 179+ E0FD 36 00        .a1:    ld (hl),0
 180+ E0FF 23                   inc hl
 181+ E100 10 FB                djnz .a1
 182+ E102 21 8F 9A             ld hl,ULA_ATTR + 20*32 + 15
 183+ E105 06 06                ld b,6
 184+ E107 36 00        .a2:    ld (hl),0
 185+ E109 23                   inc hl
 186+ E10A 10 FB                djnz .a2
 187+ E10C 3E 12                ld a,PAGE_L2_A
 188+ E10E CD 13 E1             call .buf
 189+ E111 3E 18                ld a,PAGE_L2_B
 190+ E113 32 22 E3     .buf:   ld (cp_base),a                  ; A = first page of a buffer
 191+ E116 21 26 E6             ld hl,compass_bg
 192+ E119 0E 98                ld c,COMP_BGY                   ; row
 193+ E11B 79           .row:   ld a,c
 194+ E11C 07                   rlca
 195+ E11D 07                   rlca
 196+ E11E 07                   rlca
 197+ E11F E6 07                and 7                           ; its 32-row section
 198+ E121 47                   ld b,a
 199+ E122 3A 22 E3             ld a,(cp_base)
 200+ E125 80                   add a,b
 201+ E126 ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a
 202+ E129 79                   ld a,c
 203+ E12A E6 1F                and 31
 204+ E12C 57                   ld d,a
 205+ E12D 1E 78                ld e,COMP_BGX
 206+ E12F C5                   push bc
 207+ E130 01 30 00             ld bc,48
 208+ E133 ED B0                ldir
 209+ E135 C1                   pop bc
 210+ E136 0C                   inc c
 211+ E137 79                   ld a,c
 212+ E138 FE A8                cp COMP_BGY+16
 213+ E13A 20 DF                jr nz,.row
 214+ E13C C9                   ret
 215+ E13D
 216+ E13D              ; Each frame: the 32 columns of the tape centred on the heading
 217+ E13D              draw_compass:
 218+ E13D 3A 40 B6             ld a,(yaw)
 219+ E140 0F                   rrca
 220+ E141 0F                   rrca
 221+ E142 E6 3F                and 63                          ; centre column (4 yaw units a pixel)
 222+ E144 D6 10                sub 16
 223+ E146 E6 3F                and 63                          ; left edge
 224+ E148 32 21 E3             ld (cp_col),a
 225+ E14B 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; tape row
 226+ E14D 79           .row:   ld a,c                          ; screen row COMP_Y + c
 227+ E14E C6 9C                add a,COMP_Y
 228+ E150 47                   ld b,a
 229+ E151 07                   rlca
 230+ E152 07                   rlca
 231+ E153 07                   rlca
 232+ E154 E6 07                and 7
 233+ E156 5F                   ld e,a
 234+ E157 3A D7 BA             ld a,(seg_base)                 ; the back buffer's section
 235+ E15A 83                   add a,e
 236+ E15B ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a
 237+ E15E 78                   ld a,b
 238+ E15F E6 1F                and 31
 239+ E161 C5                   push bc
 240+ E162 47                   ld b,a                          ; DE -> the row in the section
 241+ E163 51                   ld d,c
 242+ E164 1E 60                ld e,96
 243+ E166 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; tape row * 96
 244+ E168 21 26 E3             ld hl,compass_tape
 245+ E16B 19                   add hl,de
 246+ E16C 3A 21 E3             ld a,(cp_col)
 247+ E16F ED 31                add hl,a
 248+ E171 50                   ld d,b
 249+ E172 1E 80                ld e,COMP_X
 250+ E174 01 20 00             ld bc,32
 251+ E177 ED B0                ldir
 252+ E179 C1                   pop bc
 253+ E17A 0C                   inc c
 254+ E17B 79                   ld a,c
 255+ E17C FE 08                cp 8
 256+ E17E 20 CD                jr nz,.row
 257+ E180 C9                   ret
 258+ E181
 259+ E181              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 260+ E181              ; Weapon slots, right of the passengers: "WPN:" and the digits 1-5.  A digit
 261+ E181              ; is green if its slot holds a weapon, light blue if it is empty; the
 262+ E181              ; selected slot's digit has a box round it.  An empty slot that was asked
 263+ E181              ; for flashes yellow / blue (WFLASH frames each, 3 times, ending blue).
 264+ E181              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 265+ E181              draw_wpn:
 266+ E181 3A 4E B6             ld a,(wpn_sel)                  ; move the box when the selection changes
 267+ E184 21 1C E3             ld hl,wsel_shown
 268+ E187 BE                   cp (hl)
 269+ E188 28 14                jr z,.col
 270+ E18A 4E                   ld c,(hl)
 271+ E18B 77                   ld (hl),a
 272+ E18C 79                   ld a,c
 273+ E18D FE 05                cp WPN_SLOTS
 274+ E18F 30 05                jr nc,.new                      ; nothing boxed yet
 275+ E191 06 00                ld b,0
 276+ E193 CD DF E1             call wpn_digit                  ; the old one: plain
 277+ E196 3A 4E B6     .new:   ld a,(wpn_sel)
 278+ E199 06 01                ld b,1
 279+ E19B CD DF E1             call wpn_digit                  ; the new one: boxed
 280+ E19E 21 2F 9A     .col:   ld hl,ULA_ATTR + PAX_ROW*32 + WPN_COL
 281+ E1A1 11 44 B6             ld de,wpn_slots
 282+ E1A4 06 00                ld b,0
 283+ E1A6 0E 0C        .lp:    ld c,P_PANEL<<3 | C_OK          ; fitted: green
 284+ E1A8 1A                   ld a,(de)
 285+ E1A9 B7                   or a
 286+ E1AA 20 02                jr nz,.fl
 287+ E1AC 0E 0D                ld c,P_PANEL<<3 | C_BLUE        ; empty: blue
 288+ E1AE 3A 50 B6     .fl:    ld a,(wflash_t)                 ; flashing this one?
 289+ E1B1 B7                   or a
 290+ E1B2 28 19                jr z,.st
 291+ E1B4 3A 4F B6             ld a,(wflash_slot)
 292+ E1B7 B8                   cp b
 293+ E1B8 20 13                jr nz,.st
 294+ E1BA 3A 50 B6             ld a,(wflash_t)                 ; phase (t-1)/WFLASH: odd = yellow
 295+ E1BD 3D                   dec a
 296+ E1BE C5                   push bc
 297+ E1BF 06 FF                ld b,-1
 298+ E1C1 04           .dv:    inc b
 299+ E1C2 D6 05                sub WFLASH
 300+ E1C4 30 FB                jr nc,.dv
 301+ E1C6 CB 40                bit 0,b
 302+ E1C8 C1                   pop bc
 303+ E1C9 28 02                jr z,.st
 304+ E1CB 0E 0A                ld c,P_PANEL<<3 | C_YEL
 305+ E1CD 71           .st:    ld (hl),c
 306+ E1CE 23                   inc hl
 307+ E1CF 13                   inc de
 308+ E1D0 13                   inc de
 309+ E1D1 04                   inc b
 310+ E1D2 78                   ld a,b
 311+ E1D3 FE 05                cp WPN_SLOTS
 312+ E1D5 20 CF                jr nz,.lp
 313+ E1D7 21 50 B6             ld hl,wflash_t                  ; count the flash down
 314+ E1DA 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 315+ E1DB B7                   or a
 316+ E1DC C8                   ret z
 317+ E1DD 35                   dec (hl)
 318+ E1DE C9                   ret
 319+ E1DF
 320+ E1DF              ; A = slot: draw its digit (slot + 1); B = 1: with a box round it
 321+ E1DF              wpn_digit:
 322+ E1DF 5F                   ld e,a
 323+ E1E0 C6 2F                add a,low (ULA_ROW_PAX + WPN_COL)
 324+ E1E2 6F                   ld l,a
 325+ E1E3 26 90                ld h,high (ULA_ROW_PAX + WPN_COL)
 326+ E1E5 E5                   push hl
 327+ E1E6 7B                   ld a,e
 328+ E1E7 3C                   inc a                           ; glyph = digit
 329+ E1E8 EB                   ex de,hl
 330+ E1E9 C5                   push bc
 331+ E1EA CD 02 E0             call print_glyph_at
 332+ E1ED C1                   pop bc
 333+ E1EE E1                   pop hl
 334+ E1EF CB 40                bit 0,b
 335+ E1F1 C8                   ret z
 336+ E1F2 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; box: top and bottom rows, and
 337+ E1F3 F6 FF                or $FF                          ; both sides between (the digits
 338+ E1F5 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; keep clear of the cell's edge)
 339+ E1F6 06 06                ld b,6
 340+ E1F8 24           .sd:    inc h
 341+ E1F9 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 342+ E1FA F6 81                or $81
 343+ E1FC 77                   ld (hl),a
 344+ E1FD 10 F9                djnz .sd
 345+ E1FF 24                   inc h
 346+ E200 36 FF                ld (hl),$FF
 347+ E202 C9                   ret
 348+ E203
 349+ E203              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 350+ E203              ; Passengers: PASSENGER_MAX 8x8 cells on the panel's top row.  From the left:
 351+ E203              ; a green stick man for each human saved (delivered to the base), a white
 352+ E203              ; one for each human aboard, and an empty seat (a hollow square) for the rest.
 353+ E203              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 354+ E203              draw_pax:
 355+ E203 11 25 90             ld de,ULA_ROW_PAX + PAX_COL
 356+ E206 21 25 9A             ld hl,ULA_ATTR + PAX_ROW*32 + PAX_COL
 357+ E209 06 00                ld b,0                          ; cell
 358+ E20B C5           .lp:    push bc
 359+ E20C D5                   push de
 360+ E20D E5                   push hl
 361+ E20E 3A 6B 76             ld a,(saved)
 362+ E211 4F                   ld c,a
 363+ E212 78                   ld a,b
 364+ E213 B9                   cp c
 365+ E214 30 06                jr nc,.ab
 366+ E216 36 0C                ld (hl),P_PANEL<<3 | C_OK       ; saved: green man
 367+ E218 3E 15                ld a,GLYPH_MAN
 368+ E21A 18 0F                jr .g
 369+ E21C 36 0F        .ab:    ld (hl),ATTR_PANEL              ; white
 370+ E21E 3A 6A 76             ld a,(passengers)
 371+ E221 81                   add a,c
 372+ E222 4F                   ld c,a                          ; saved + aboard
 373+ E223 78                   ld a,b
 374+ E224 B9                   cp c
 375+ E225 3E 16                ld a,GLYPH_SEAT
 376+ E227 30 02                jr nc,.g                        ; empty seat
 377+ E229 3E 15                ld a,GLYPH_MAN                  ; aboard
 378+ E22B CD 02 E0     .g:     call print_glyph_at
 379+ E22E E1                   pop hl
 380+ E22F 23                   inc hl
 381+ E230 D1                   pop de
 382+ E231 1C                   inc e
 383+ E232 C1                   pop bc
 384+ E233 04                   inc b
 385+ E234 78                   ld a,b
 386+ E235 FE 05                cp PASSENGER_MAX
 387+ E237 20 D2                jr nz,.lp
 388+ E239 C9                   ret
 389+ E23A
 390+ E23A              ; HL -> 3 glyph codes, DE = first ULA cell
 391+ E23A              print_label:
 392+ E23A 06 03                ld b,3
 393+ E23C C5           .l:     push bc
 394+ E23D D5                   push de
 395+ E23E 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 396+ E23F 23                   inc hl
 397+ E240 E5                   push hl
 398+ E241 CD 02 E0             call print_glyph_at
 399+ E244 E1                   pop hl
 400+ E245 D1                   pop de
 401+ E246 1C                   inc e
 402+ E247 C1                   pop bc
 403+ E248 10 F2                djnz .l
 404+ E24A C9                   ret
 405+ E24B
 406+ E24B              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 407+ E24B              ; Layer 2 double buffering with the hardware shadow register.
 408+ E24B              ;   NextReg $12 = active bank (on screen), NextReg $13 = shadow bank.
 409+ E24B              ; The game always draws into the shadow bank; l2_flip swaps the two during
 410+ E24B              ; vertical blank, so the registers themselves record which buffer is which.
 411+ E24B              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 412+ E24B
 413+ E24B              ;==============================================================================
 414+ E24B              ; HUD: weapons, compass, passengers, and the speed, altitude and ground
 415+ E24B              ; height gauges.  The gauges are vertical bars on Layer 2 (in the back
 416+ E24B              ; buffer, every frame) behind transparent ULA cells, as the compass is: the
 417+ E24B              ; altitude and ground bars share one 8-pixel cell column, which ULA
 418+ E24B              ; attributes couldn't colour (green, amber and black in one cell).
 419+ E24B              ;==============================================================================
 420+ E24B              draw_hud:
 421+ E24B CD 81 E1             call draw_wpn
 422+ E24E CD 3D E1             call draw_compass
 423+ E251                      ; passengers: redrawn when the number aboard or saved changes
 424+ E251 3A 6B 76             ld a,(saved)                    ; (human.asm, page 7)
 425+ E254 87                   add a,a
 426+ E255 87                   add a,a
 427+ E256 87                   add a,a
 428+ E257 21 6A 76             ld hl,passengers
 429+ E25A 86                   add a,(hl)
 430+ E25B 21 1B E3             ld hl,pax_shown
 431+ E25E BE                   cp (hl)
 432+ E25F 28 04                jr z,.pxok
 433+ E261 77                   ld (hl),a
 434+ E262 CD 03 E2             call draw_pax
 435+ E265              .pxok:
 436+ E265
 437+ E265 CD 6B E2             call draw_gauges
 438+ E268 C3 CB 77             jp map_player
 439+ E26B
 440+ E26B              ; The gauges, GAUGE_H (48) pixels tall:
 441+ E26B              ;   S  speed, 8 wide: (speed * 55) / 32 pixels, so SPEED_MAX 28 fills it;
 442+ E26B              ;      green, red when reversing
 443+ E26B              ;   A  left 4 pixels: altitude, green; right 4: the height of the city tile
 444+ E26B              ;      under the craft (0 on a road), amber.  Both 1 pixel per 4 units, so
 445+ E26B              ;      the ceiling (MAX_Y 1.5 height units, 192) fills the bar; the ground
 446+ E26B              ;      under the craft is always lower, so the gap between them is the
 447+ E26B              ;      clearance.
 448+ E26B              draw_gauges:
 449+ E26B 3A 43 B6             ld a,(speed)
 450+ E26E 0E 0F                ld c,HUD_COL
 451+ E270 CB 7F                bit 7,a
 452+ E272 28 04                jr z,.sp
 453+ E274 ED 44                neg
 454+ E276 0E 13                ld c,HUD_WARN
 455+ E278 57           .sp:    ld d,a
 456+ E279 1E 37                ld e,55
 457+ E27B ED 30                mul d,e
 458+ E27D 06 05                ld b,5
 459+ E27F ED 2A                bsrl de,b
 460+ E281 79                   ld a,c
 461+ E282 32 1E E3             ld (g_spdc),a
 462+ E285 7B                   ld a,e
 463+ E286 CD 15 E3             call g_clamp
 464+ E289 32 1D E3             ld (g_spd),a
 465+ E28C 2A 3D B6             ld hl,(cam_y)
 466+ E28F CD 07 E3             call g_px
 467+ E292 32 1F E3             ld (g_alt),a
 468+ E295 CD CF B3             call cam_cell_ptr
 469+ E298 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 470+ E299 CD BE B4             call bld_height                 ; HL = height of this tile
 471+ E29C CD 07 E3             call g_px
 472+ E29F 32 20 E3             ld (g_gnd),a
 473+ E2A2 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; row from the top
 474+ E2A4 79           .row:   ld a,c
 475+ E2A5 C6 88                add a,GAUGE_TOP
 476+ E2A7 47                   ld b,a
 477+ E2A8 07                   rlca
 478+ E2A9 07                   rlca
 479+ E2AA 07                   rlca
 480+ E2AB E6 07                and 7                           ; its 32-row section
 481+ E2AD 5F                   ld e,a
 482+ E2AE 3A D7 BA             ld a,(seg_base)                 ; (the back buffer)
 483+ E2B1 83                   add a,e
 484+ E2B2 ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a
 485+ E2B5 78                   ld a,b
 486+ E2B6 E6 1F                and 31
 487+ E2B8 67                   ld h,a
 488+ E2B9 2E 08                ld l,SPD_GCOL*8
 489+ E2BB 3E 30                ld a,GAUGE_H
 490+ E2BD 91                   sub c
 491+ E2BE 47                   ld b,a                          ; this row's level: GAUGE_H..1
 492+ E2BF 3A 1D E3             ld a,(g_spd)                    ; speed: filled up to its level?
 493+ E2C2 B8                   cp b
 494+ E2C3 3A 1E E3             ld a,(g_spdc)
 495+ E2C6 30 02                jr nc,.s
 496+ E2C8 3E 27                ld a,MAP_BG                     ; empty: black
 497+ E2CA              .s:     REPT 8
 498+ E2CA 77          >        ld (hl),a
 499+ E2CB 2C          >        inc l
 498+ E2CC 77          >        ld (hl),a
 499+ E2CD 2C          >        inc l
 498+ E2CE 77          >        ld (hl),a
 499+ E2CF 2C          >        inc l
 498+ E2D0 77          >        ld (hl),a
 499+ E2D1 2C          >        inc l
 498+ E2D2 77          >        ld (hl),a
 499+ E2D3 2C          >        inc l
 498+ E2D4 77          >        ld (hl),a
 499+ E2D5 2C          >        inc l
 498+ E2D6 77          >        ld (hl),a
 499+ E2D7 2C          >        inc l
 498+ E2D8 77          >        ld (hl),a
 499+ E2D9 2C          >        inc l
 500+ E2DA                      ENDR
 501+ E2DA 2E 18                ld l,ALT_GCOL*8
 502+ E2DC 3A 1F E3             ld a,(g_alt)
 503+ E2DF B8                   cp b
 504+ E2E0 3E 0F                ld a,HUD_COL
 505+ E2E2 30 02                jr nc,.a
 506+ E2E4 3E 27                ld a,MAP_BG
 507+ E2E6              .a:     REPT 4
 508+ E2E6 77          >        ld (hl),a
 509+ E2E7 2C          >        inc l
 508+ E2E8 77          >        ld (hl),a
 509+ E2E9 2C          >        inc l
 508+ E2EA 77          >        ld (hl),a
 509+ E2EB 2C          >        inc l
 508+ E2EC 77          >        ld (hl),a
 509+ E2ED 2C          >        inc l
 510+ E2EE                      ENDR
 511+ E2EE 3A 20 E3             ld a,(g_gnd)
 512+ E2F1 B8                   cp b
 513+ E2F2 3E 5F                ld a,GAUGE_AMBER
 514+ E2F4 30 02                jr nc,.g
 515+ E2F6 3E 27                ld a,MAP_BG
 516+ E2F8              .g:     REPT 4
 517+ E2F8 77          >        ld (hl),a
 518+ E2F9 2C          >        inc l
 517+ E2FA 77          >        ld (hl),a
 518+ E2FB 2C          >        inc l
 517+ E2FC 77          >        ld (hl),a
 518+ E2FD 2C          >        inc l
 517+ E2FE 77          >        ld (hl),a
 518+ E2FF 2C          >        inc l
 519+ E300                      ENDR
 520+ E300 0C                   inc c
 521+ E301 79                   ld a,c
 522+ E302 FE 30                cp GAUGE_H
 523+ E304 20 9E                jr nz,.row
 524+ E306 C9                   ret
 525+ E307
 526+ E307              ; HL = height (world units) -> A = HL / 4, at most GAUGE_H
 527+ E307              g_px:
 528+ E307 CB 3C                srl h
 529+ E309 CB 1D                rr l
 530+ E30B CB 3C                srl h
 531+ E30D CB 1D                rr l
 532+ E30F 7C                   ld a,h
 533+ E310 B7                   or a
 534+ E311 3E 30                ld a,GAUGE_H
 535+ E313 C0                   ret nz
 536+ E314 7D                   ld a,l
 537+ E315              g_clamp:
 538+ E315 FE 31                cp GAUGE_H+1
 539+ E317 D8                   ret c
 540+ E318 3E 30                ld a,GAUGE_H
 541+ E31A C9                   ret
 542+ E31B
 543+ E31B              ;==============================================================================
 544+ E31B              ; HUD page data
 545+ E31B              ;==============================================================================
 546+ E31B FF           pax_shown:  db $FF                      ; passengers last drawn ($FF: not yet)
 547+ E31C FF           wsel_shown: db $FF                      ; weapon slot boxed ($FF: none yet)
 548+ E31D 00           g_spd:      db 0                        ; draw_gauges: bar heights (pixels)
 549+ E31E 00           g_spdc:     db 0                        ;   and the speed bar's colour
 550+ E31F 00           g_alt:      db 0
 551+ E320 00           g_gnd:      db 0
 552+ E321 00           cp_col:     db 0                        ; compass: tape column at the left edge
 553+ E322 00           cp_base:    db 0                        ; compass_init: buffer being drawn
 554+ E323
 555+ E323              ; 8x8 glyphs: digits 0-9, then F, P, S, space
 556+ E323              GLYPH_F     equ 10
 557+ E323              GLYPH_P     equ 11
 558+ E323              GLYPH_S     equ 12
 559+ E323              GLYPH_SPACE equ 13
 560+ E323              GLYPH_D     equ 14
 561+ E323              GLYPH_A     equ 15
 562+ E323              GLYPH_L     equ 16
 563+ E323              GLYPH_T     equ 17
 564+ E323              GLYPH_G     equ 18
 565+ E323              GLYPH_R     equ 19
 566+ E323              GLYPH_N     equ 20
 567+ E323              GLYPH_MAN   equ 21
 568+ E323              GLYPH_SEAT  equ 22
 569+ E323              GLYPH_W     equ 23
