# file opened: keys.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - keyboard (8K page PAGE_KEYS, runs at $E000 in slot 7: main_loop
   3+ E000              ; and the menu page it in, and the game logic pages in slot 6 call it)
   4+ E000              ;
   5+ E000              ; Redefinable keys.  A key is a 16-bit code: high byte = key bank, low byte
   6+ E000              ; = bit mask within that bank.
   7+ E000              ;   banks 0-7: the ZX Spectrum keyboard half-rows (port $xxFE, address line
   8+ E000              ;              A8+bank low): read 0 = pressed
   9+ E000              ;   bank 8:    NextReg $B0, Extended Keys 0: read 1 = pressed
  10+ E000              ;   bank 9:    NextReg $B1, Extended Keys 1: read 1 = pressed
  11+ E000              ; kbd_scan, once per frame at the top of the main loop, reads all ten banks
  12+ E000              ; into key_now, inverting the ZX rows so that every bank holds 1 = pressed,
  13+ E000              ; and keeps the previous scan in key_prev.  The game asks about actions
  14+ E000              ; (K_FIRE, K_LEFT, ...), not keys: key_defs holds the key code of each
  15+ E000              ; action, so keys can be redefined (key_define) at any time.
  16+ E000              ;   key_held    A = action -> NZ if its key is down
  17+ E000              ;   key_pressed A = action -> NZ if its key went down since the last scan
  18+ E000              ;   key_find    -> NZ and HL = code of a key that is down (for a redefine
  19+ E000              ;               screen); Z if none
  20+ E000              ;   key_code_pressed  HL = key code -> NZ if that key went down
  21+ E000              ; The Next normally also enters the extended keys into the 8x5 matrix (e.g.
  22+ E000              ; cursor LEFT as CAPS SHIFT + 5).  NextReg $68 bit 4 stops that: set it with
  23+ E000              ; kbd_double_on (KBD_STOP_DOUBLING = TRUE), clear it with kbd_double_off
  24+ E000              ; (FALSE, the default).
  25+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  26+ E000
  27+ E000              ; --- key codes: bank << 8 | mask ---------------------------------------------
  28+ E000              KB_SHIFT    equ $0001                   ; bank 0: $FEFE
  29+ E000              KB_Z        equ $0002
  30+ E000              KB_X        equ $0004
  31+ E000              KB_C        equ $0008
  32+ E000              KB_V        equ $0010
  33+ E000              KB_A        equ $0101                   ; bank 1: $FDFE
  34+ E000              KB_S        equ $0102
  35+ E000              KB_D        equ $0104
  36+ E000              KB_F        equ $0108
  37+ E000              KB_G        equ $0110
  38+ E000              KB_Q        equ $0201                   ; bank 2: $FBFE
  39+ E000              KB_W        equ $0202
  40+ E000              KB_E        equ $0204
  41+ E000              KB_R        equ $0208
  42+ E000              KB_T        equ $0210
  43+ E000              KB_1        equ $0301                   ; bank 3: $F7FE
  44+ E000              KB_2        equ $0302
  45+ E000              KB_3        equ $0304
  46+ E000              KB_4        equ $0308
  47+ E000              KB_5        equ $0310
  48+ E000              KB_0        equ $0401                   ; bank 4: $EFFE
  49+ E000              KB_9        equ $0402
  50+ E000              KB_8        equ $0404
  51+ E000              KB_7        equ $0408
  52+ E000              KB_6        equ $0410
  53+ E000              KB_P        equ $0501                   ; bank 5: $DFFE
  54+ E000              KB_O        equ $0502
  55+ E000              KB_I        equ $0504
  56+ E000              KB_U        equ $0508
  57+ E000              KB_Y        equ $0510
  58+ E000              KB_ENTER    equ $0601                   ; bank 6: $BFFE
  59+ E000              KB_L        equ $0602
  60+ E000              KB_K        equ $0604
  61+ E000              KB_J        equ $0608
  62+ E000              KB_H        equ $0610
  63+ E000              KB_SPACE    equ $0701                   ; bank 7: $7FFE
  64+ E000              KB_SYM      equ $0702
  65+ E000              KB_M        equ $0704
  66+ E000              KB_N        equ $0708
  67+ E000              KB_B        equ $0710
  68+ E000              KB_RIGHT    equ $0801                   ; bank 8: NextReg $B0
  69+ E000              KB_LEFT     equ $0802
  70+ E000              KB_DOWN     equ $0804
  71+ E000              KB_UP       equ $0808
  72+ E000              KB_PERIOD   equ $0810
  73+ E000              KB_COMMA    equ $0820
  74+ E000              KB_QUOTE    equ $0840
  75+ E000              KB_SEMI     equ $0880
  76+ E000              KB_EXTEND   equ $0901                   ; bank 9: NextReg $B1
  77+ E000              KB_CAPSLOCK equ $0902
  78+ E000              KB_GRAPH    equ $0904
  79+ E000              KB_TRUEVID  equ $0908
  80+ E000              KB_INVVID   equ $0910
  81+ E000              KB_BREAK    equ $0920
  82+ E000              KB_EDIT     equ $0940
  83+ E000              KB_DELETE   equ $0980
  84+ E000              KEY_BANKS   equ 10
  85+ E000              FALSE       equ 0
  86+ E000              TRUE        equ 1
  87+ E000
  88+ E000              ; --- actions: index into key_defs --------------------------------------------
  89+ E000              K_SPEED_UP  equ 0
  90+ E000              K_SPEED_DN  equ 1
  91+ E000              K_STOP      equ 2
  92+ E000              K_LEFT      equ 3
  93+ E000              K_RIGHT     equ 4
  94+ E000              K_CLIMB     equ 5
  95+ E000              K_DESCEND   equ 6
  96+ E000              K_NOSE_UP   equ 7
  97+ E000              K_NOSE_DN   equ 8
  98+ E000              K_FIRE      equ 9
  99+ E000              K_WEAPON    equ 10                      ; cycle the centre slot's weapon
 100+ E000              K_SLOT1     equ 11                      ; select weapon slots 1-5
 101+ E000              K_ACTIONS   equ 16
 102+ E000
 103+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 104+ E000              ; Read all ten key banks: key_prev = last scan, key_now = this one, every bit
 105+ E000              ; 1 = pressed
 106+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 107+ E000              kbd_scan:
 108+ E000 21 C4 E0             ld hl,key_now
 109+ E003 11 CE E0             ld de,key_prev
 110+ E006 01 0A 00             ld bc,KEY_BANKS
 111+ E009 ED B0                ldir
 112+ E00B 21 C4 E0             ld hl,key_now
 113+ E00E 01 FE FE             ld bc,$FEFE                     ; banks 0-7: half-rows $FE..$7F
 114+ E011 ED 78        .row:   in a,(c)
 115+ E013 2F                   cpl                             ; 0 = pressed -> 1 = pressed
 116+ E014 E6 1F                and %00011111
 117+ E016 77                   ld (hl),a
 118+ E017 23                   inc hl
 119+ E018 CB 00                rlc b                           ; next half-row
 120+ E01A 38 F5                jr c,.row                       ; (stops after $7F)
 121+ E01C 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B                     ; banks 8, 9: NextRegs $B0, $B1
 122+ E01F 3E B0                ld a,$B0
 123+ E021 ED 79                out (c),a
 124+ E023 04                   inc b
 125+ E024 ED 78                in a,(c)                        ; already 1 = pressed
 126+ E026 77                   ld (hl),a
 127+ E027 23                   inc hl
 128+ E028 05                   dec b
 129+ E029 3E B1                ld a,$B1
 130+ E02B ED 79                out (c),a
 131+ E02D 04                   inc b
 132+ E02E ED 78                in a,(c)
 133+ E030 77                   ld (hl),a
 134+ E031 C9                   ret
 135+ E032
 136+ E032              ; Stop the extended keys also appearing in the 8x5 matrix:
 137+ E032              ; KBD_STOP_DOUBLING = TRUE, NextReg $68 bit 4 = 1
 138+ E032              kbd_double_on:
 139+ E032 3E 01                ld a,TRUE
 140+ E034 18 01                jr kbd_double
 141+ E036
 142+ E036              ; Let the extended keys also appear in the 8x5 matrix (the default):
 143+ E036              ; KBD_STOP_DOUBLING = FALSE, NextReg $68 bit 4 = 0
 144+ E036              kbd_double_off:
 145+ E036              kdb_double_off:                         ; (alias: the name as first asked for)
 146+ E036 AF                   xor a                           ; FALSE
 147+ E037
 148+ E037              ; A = TRUE / FALSE: store it, and set NextReg $68 bit 4 to match, keeping
 149+ E037              ; the register's other bits
 150+ E037              kbd_double:
 151+ E037 32 C3 E0             ld (KBD_STOP_DOUBLING),a
 152+ E03A 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 153+ E03D 3E 68                ld a,$68
 154+ E03F ED 79                out (c),a
 155+ E041 04                   inc b
 156+ E042 ED 78                in a,(c)                        ; current NextReg $68
 157+ E044 CB A7                res 4,a
 158+ E046 21 C3 E0             ld hl,KBD_STOP_DOUBLING
 159+ E049 CB 46                bit 0,(hl)
 160+ E04B 28 02                jr z,.w
 161+ E04D CB E7                set 4,a
 162+ E04F ED 79        .w:     out (c),a                       ; ($253B: still selects $68)
 163+ E051 C9                   ret
 164+ E052
 165+ E052              ; A = action -> NZ if its key is down.  Preserves BC, DE, HL.
 166+ E052              key_held:
 167+ E052 E5                   push hl
 168+ E053 CD 86 E0             call key_def
 169+ E056 A6                   and (hl)
 170+ E057 E1                   pop hl
 171+ E058 C9                   ret
 172+ E059
 173+ E059              ; A = action -> NZ if its key is down now but wasn't at the last scan.
 174+ E059              ; Preserves BC, DE, HL.
 175+ E059              key_pressed:
 176+ E059 E5                   push hl
 177+ E05A C5                   push bc
 178+ E05B CD 86 E0             call key_def
 179+ E05E 4F                   ld c,a
 180+ E05F A6                   and (hl)
 181+ E060 28 09                jr z,.x                         ; not down
 182+ E062 79                   ld a,c
 183+ E063 D5                   push de
 184+ E064 11 0A 00             ld de,KEY_BANKS
 185+ E067 19                   add hl,de                       ; key_prev, same bank
 186+ E068 A6                   and (hl)
 187+ E069 D1                   pop de
 188+ E06A A9                   xor c                           ; down before: Z; new: NZ
 189+ E06B C1           .x:     pop bc
 190+ E06C E1                   pop hl
 191+ E06D C9                   ret
 192+ E06E
 193+ E06E              ; HL = a key code (KB_...) -> NZ if that key went down since the last scan
 194+ E06E              ; (for fixed keys, such as the menu's).  Preserves BC, DE, HL.
 195+ E06E              key_code_pressed:
 196+ E06E E5                   push hl
 197+ E06F C5                   push bc
 198+ E070 4D                   ld c,l                          ; mask
 199+ E071 7C                   ld a,h                          ; bank
 200+ E072 21 C4 E0             ld hl,key_now
 201+ E075 ED 31                add hl,a
 202+ E077 79                   ld a,c
 203+ E078 A6                   and (hl)
 204+ E079 28 08                jr z,.x                         ; not down
 205+ E07B D5                   push de
 206+ E07C 11 0A 00             ld de,KEY_BANKS
 207+ E07F 19                   add hl,de                       ; key_prev, same bank
 208+ E080 A6                   and (hl)
 209+ E081 D1                   pop de
 210+ E082 A9                   xor c                           ; down before: Z; new: NZ
 211+ E083 C1           .x:     pop bc
 212+ E084 E1                   pop hl
 213+ E085 C9                   ret
 214+ E086
 215+ E086              ; A = action -> HL -> key_now[its bank], A = its mask
 216+ E086              key_def:
 217+ E086 87                   add a,a
 218+ E087 21 D8 E0             ld hl,key_defs
 219+ E08A ED 31                add hl,a
 220+ E08C 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; mask
 221+ E08D 23                   inc hl
 222+ E08E F5                   push af
 223+ E08F 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; bank
 224+ E090 21 C4 E0             ld hl,key_now
 225+ E093 ED 31                add hl,a
 226+ E095 F1                   pop af
 227+ E096 C9                   ret
 228+ E097
 229+ E097              ; A = action, HL = key code: redefine the action's key
 230+ E097              key_define:
 231+ E097 EB                   ex de,hl
 232+ E098 87                   add a,a
 233+ E099 21 D8 E0             ld hl,key_defs
 234+ E09C ED 31                add hl,a
 235+ E09E 73                   ld (hl),e
 236+ E09F 23                   inc hl
 237+ E0A0 72                   ld (hl),d
 238+ E0A1 EB                   ex de,hl
 239+ E0A2 C9                   ret
 240+ E0A3
 241+ E0A3              ; -> NZ and HL = code of a key that is down (lowest bank, lowest bit); Z if
 242+ E0A3              ; none.  For a redefine screen.
 243+ E0A3              key_find:
 244+ E0A3 21 C4 E0             ld hl,key_now
 245+ E0A6 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; bank
 246+ E0A8 7E           .b:     ld a,(hl)
 247+ E0A9 B7                   or a
 248+ E0AA 20 09                jr nz,.got
 249+ E0AC 23                   inc hl
 250+ E0AD 0C                   inc c
 251+ E0AE 79                   ld a,c
 252+ E0AF FE 0A                cp KEY_BANKS
 253+ E0B1 20 F5                jr nz,.b
 254+ E0B3 AF                   xor a                           ; Z: nothing down
 255+ E0B4 C9                   ret
 256+ E0B5 06 01        .got:   ld b,1                          ; lowest bit set
 257+ E0B7 1F           .m:     rra
 258+ E0B8 38 04                jr c,.f
 259+ E0BA CB 20                sla b
 260+ E0BC 18 F9                jr .m
 261+ E0BE 61           .f:     ld h,c
 262+ E0BF 68                   ld l,b
 263+ E0C0 F6 01                or 1                            ; NZ
 264+ E0C2 C9                   ret
 265+ E0C3
 266+ E0C3              ;==============================================================================
 267+ E0C3              ; Keyboard data
 268+ E0C3              ;==============================================================================
 269+ E0C3              KBD_STOP_DOUBLING:
 270+ E0C3 00                       db FALSE                    ; TRUE: NextReg $68 bit 4 set, extended
 271+ E0C4                                                      ; keys kept out of the 8x5 matrix
 272+ E0C4 00 00 00...  key_now:    ds KEY_BANKS                ; this scan, 1 = pressed
 273+ E0CE 00 00 00...  key_prev:   ds KEY_BANKS                ; the scan before (must follow key_now)
 274+ E0D8              key_defs:                               ; the key of each action (redefinable)
 275+ E0D8 01 02                    dw KB_Q                     ; K_SPEED_UP
 276+ E0DA 01 01                    dw KB_A                     ; K_SPEED_DN
 277+ E0DC 02 00                    dw KB_Z                     ; K_STOP
 278+ E0DE 02 05                    dw KB_O                     ; K_LEFT
 279+ E0E0 01 05                    dw KB_P                     ; K_RIGHT
 280+ E0E2 02 02                    dw KB_W                     ; K_CLIMB
 281+ E0E4 02 01                    dw KB_S                     ; K_DESCEND
 282+ E0E6 04 02                    dw KB_E                     ; K_NOSE_UP
 283+ E0E8 04 01                    dw KB_D                     ; K_NOSE_DN
 284+ E0EA 01 07                    dw KB_SPACE                 ; K_FIRE
 285+ E0EC 01 04                    dw KB_0                     ; K_WEAPON
 286+ E0EE 01 03 02 03              dw KB_1, KB_2, KB_3, KB_4, KB_5     ; K_SLOT1..5
 286+ E0F2 04 03 08 03
 286+ E0F6 10 03
 287+ E0F8                          ASSERT $ - key_defs == K_ACTIONS*2
 288+ E0F8
# file closed: keys.asm
1093  E0F8                      DISPLAY "Keys end:  ",/A,$

1095  E0F8                      MMU CODE_SLOT e, PAGE_SPRITES, CODE_ADDR
1096  E000                      ORG CODE_ADDR
1097  E000                      INCLUDE "sprites.asm"
# file opened: sprites.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - hardware sprites, Layer 3 (8K page PAGE_SPRITES, runs at $E000
   3+ E000              ; in slot 7: page it in with nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPRITES before calling)
   4+ E000              ;
   5+ E000              ; The Next has 128 hardware sprites, each 16x16 pixels from a pattern in the
   6+ E000              ; sprite pattern memory (16K, separate from the Z80's: 64 8-bit patterns of
   7+ E000              ; 256 bytes, one byte per pixel, row by row).  A pattern byte is an index
   8+ E000              ; into the sprite palette; SPR_TRANS ($E3, NextReg $4B) is transparent.  The
   9+ E000              ; sprite palette starts as the identity, so until spr_palette changes it a
  10+ E000              ; pattern byte is simply its RRRGGGBB colour.
  11+ E000              ;
  12+ E000              ; Sprites are drawn above Layer 2 and the ULA (priority S U L) and, with
  13+ E000              ; NextReg $15 bit 1 set, over the border as well, so the sprite screen is
  14+ E000              ; 320 x 256 pixels:
  15+ E000              ;
  16+ E000              ;     x 0..319, y 0..255;  (SPR_SCREEN_X, SPR_SCREEN_Y) = (32, 32) is the top
  17+ E000              ;     left of the 256x192 Layer 2 / ULA screen, (288, 224) its bottom right
  18+ E000              ;
  19+ E000              ; A sprite's position is its top left pixel.  Coordinates are 9 bits and
  20+ E000              ; wrap round at 512, so they may be negative (two's complement): a sprite at
  21+ E000              ; x = -8 shows its right half at the left edge.  With overlapping sprites,
  22+ E000              ; the higher numbered one is drawn on top.
  23+ E000              ;
  24+ E000              ; Use: sprites_init once (start-up), load patterns with spr_patterns or
  25+ E000              ; from a sprite bank (sprite_data.asm: page the bank into slot 7 and call
  26+ E000              ; its spr_load_bank, from outside slot 7), and a palette with spr_palette
  27+ E000              ; if wanted, then set up sprites in the shadow
  28+ E000              ; table with spr_set / spr_pos / spr_pattern / spr_flags / spr_scale /
  29+ E000              ; spr_hide / spr_show / spr_hide_all, and copy the table to the hardware
  30+ E000              ; once a frame with spr_update.  The table holds the first SPR_COUNT sprites;
  31+ E000              ; the rest stay hidden.
  32+ E000              ;
  33+ E000              ; Attributes, SPR_ATTR (5) bytes a sprite, kept in the table as they are
  34+ E000              ; sent (the 5-byte form):
  35+ E000              ;   +0  x bits 0-7
  36+ E000              ;   +1  y bits 0-7
  37+ E000              ;   +2  bits 7-4 palette offset (added to the pattern byte's top nibble),
  38+ E000              ;       bit 3 x mirror, 2 y mirror, 1 rotate 90 degrees clockwise, 0 x bit 8
  39+ E000              ;   +3  bit 7 visible, bit 6 = 1 (byte +4 follows), bits 5-0 pattern
  40+ E000              ;   +4  bits 4-3 x scale, bits 2-1 y scale (0..3 = x1, x2, x4, x8), 0 y bit 8
  41+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  42+ E000
  43+ E000              SPR_COUNT       equ 32          ; sprites in the shadow table (max 128)
  44+ E000              SPR_HW          equ 128         ; hardware sprites
  45+ E000              SPR_ATTR        equ 5           ; attribute bytes per sprite
  46+ E000              SPR_PATTERNS    equ 64          ; 8-bit 16x16 patterns in pattern memory
  47+ E000              SPR_SIZE        equ 16          ; pixels, each way (at scale x1)
  48+ E000              SPR_SCREEN_X    equ 32          ; sprite coordinates of the 256x192
  49+ E000              SPR_SCREEN_Y    equ 32          ; screen's top left pixel
  50+ E000              SPR_TRANS       equ $E3         ; transparent pattern byte (NextReg $4B)
  51+ E000
  52+ E000              SPRF_PAL        equ $10         ; flags (attribute +2): palette offset x 16,
  53+ E000              SPRF_XMIRROR    equ $08         ; mirror left-right,
  54+ E000              SPRF_YMIRROR    equ $04         ; mirror top-bottom,
  55+ E000              SPRF_ROT        equ $02         ; rotate 90 degrees clockwise
  56+ E000
  57+ E000              SPR_SLOT_PORT   equ $303B       ; write: select sprite / pattern slot
  58+ E000              SPR_ATTR_PORT   equ $57         ; write: attributes, from the selected sprite
  59+ E000              SPR_PAT_PORT    equ $5B         ; write: pattern bytes, from the selected slot
  60+ E000
  61+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  62+ E000              ; Start-up: sprites on, shown over the border too (the full 320 x 256),
  63+ E000              ; priority S U L; the transparent index; every hardware sprite hidden and the shadow table cleared (all hidden,
  64+ E000              ; pattern 0, at 0,0).
  65+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  66+ E000              sprites_init:
  67+ E000 ED 91 15 0B          nextreg $15,%00001011           ; priority S U L, sprites over the
  68+ E004                                                      ; border (bit 1), visible (bit 0)
  69+ E004 ED 91 4B E3          nextreg $4B,SPR_TRANS
  70+ E008 01 3B 30             ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT             ; from sprite 0, the 4-byte form
  71+ E00B AF                   xor a                           ; (+3 bit 6 clear): all hidden
  72+ E00C ED 79                out (c),a
  73+ E00E 0E 57                ld c,SPR_ATTR_PORT
  74+ E010 11 00 02             ld de,SPR_HW*4
  75+ E013 ED 79        .hw:    out (c),a
  76+ E015 1B                   dec de
  77+ E016 7A                   ld a,d
  78+ E017 B3                   or e
  79+ E018 3E 00                ld a,0
  80+ E01A 20 F7                jr nz,.hw
  81+ E01C 21 00 E2             ld hl,spr_shadow
  82+ E01F 06 20                ld b,SPR_COUNT
  83+ E021 77           .sh:    ld (hl),a                       ; x, y, flags 0
  84+ E022 23                   inc hl
  85+ E023 77                   ld (hl),a
  86+ E024 23                   inc hl
  87+ E025 77                   ld (hl),a
  88+ E026 23                   inc hl
  89+ E027 36 40                ld (hl),$40                     ; hidden, 5-byte form, pattern 0
  90+ E029 23                   inc hl
  91+ E02A 77                   ld (hl),a                       ; scale x1
  92+ E02B 23                   inc hl
  93+ E02C 10 F3                djnz .sh
  94+ E02E                      ; fall through: send it
  95+ E02E
  96+ E02E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  97+ E02E              ; Copy the shadow table to sprites 0..SPR_COUNT-1.  Once a frame, after the
  98+ E02E              ; changes (best just after the frame flip, so a move doesn't show half done).
  99+ E02E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 100+ E02E              spr_update:
 101+ E02E 01 3B 30             ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT
 102+ E031 AF                   xor a
 103+ E032 ED 79                out (c),a                       ; from sprite 0
 104+ E034 21 00 E2             ld hl,spr_shadow
 105+ E037 0E 57                ld c,SPR_ATTR_PORT
 106+ E039 16 20                ld d,SPR_COUNT
 107+ E03B 06 05        .lp:    ld b,SPR_ATTR
 108+ E03D ED B3                otir
 109+ E03F 15                   dec d
 110+ E040 20 F9                jr nz,.lp
 111+ E042 C9                   ret
 112+ E043
 113+ E043              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 114+ E043              ; Load B 8-bit patterns (256 bytes each) from HL into pattern memory, from
 115+ E043              ; pattern A (0..63).  HL ends past them.
 116+ E043              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 117+ E043              spr_patterns:
 118+ E043 50                   ld d,b
 119+ E044 01 3B 30             ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT
 120+ E047 ED 79                out (c),a
 121+ E049 0E 5B                ld c,SPR_PAT_PORT
 122+ E04B 06 00        .lp:    ld b,0                          ; 256 bytes
 123+ E04D ED B3                otir
 124+ E04F 15                   dec d
 125+ E050 20 F9                jr nz,.lp
 126+ E052 C9                   ret
 127+ E053
 128+ E053              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 129+ E053              ; Load B colours (8-bit RRRGGGBB) from HL into the sprite palette, from
 130+ E053              ; index A.  (It starts as the identity: only needed for other colours.)
 131+ E053              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 132+ E053              spr_palette:
 133+ E053 ED 91 43 20          nextreg $43,%00100000           ; edit the first sprite palette
 134+ E057 ED 92 40             nextreg $40,a
 135+ E05A 7E           .lp:    ld a,(hl)
 136+ E05B ED 92 41             nextreg $41,a
 137+ E05E 23                   inc hl
 138+ E05F 10 F9                djnz .lp
 139+ E061 C9                   ret
 140+ E062
 141+ E062              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 142+ E062              ; Sprite A (0..SPR_COUNT-1) in the shadow table.  BC = x, DE = y (sprite
 143+ E062              ; screen, signed 9 bits), H = pattern (0..63), L = flags (SPRF_*).  Shows it.
 144+ E062              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 145+ E062              spr_set:
 146+ E062 22 04 E1             ld (spr_pf),hl
 147+ E065 F5                   push af
 148+ E066 CD 84 E0             call spr_pos
 149+ E069 F1                   pop af
 150+ E06A CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 151+ E06D 23                   inc hl
 152+ E06E 23                   inc hl                          ; +2: the flags, keeping x bit 8
 153+ E06F 3A 04 E1             ld a,(spr_pf)
 154+ E072 E6 FE                and $FE
 155+ E074 5F                   ld e,a
 156+ E075 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 157+ E076 E6 01                and 1
 158+ E078 B3                   or e
 159+ E079 77                   ld (hl),a
 160+ E07A 23                   inc hl                          ; +3: visible, 5-byte form, pattern
 161+ E07B 3A 05 E1             ld a,(spr_pf+1)
 162+ E07E E6 3F                and $3F
 163+ E080 F6 C0                or $C0
 164+ E082 77                   ld (hl),a
 165+ E083 C9                   ret
 166+ E084
 167+ E084              ; Sprite A: move to BC = x, DE = y (signed 9 bits)
 168+ E084              spr_pos:
 169+ E084 D5                   push de
 170+ E085 CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 171+ E088 D1                   pop de
 172+ E089 71                   ld (hl),c                       ; +0 x bits 0-7
 173+ E08A 23                   inc hl
 174+ E08B 73                   ld (hl),e                       ; +1 y bits 0-7
 175+ E08C 23                   inc hl
 176+ E08D 78                   ld a,b                          ; +2 bit 0: x bit 8
 177+ E08E E6 01                and 1
 178+ E090 47                   ld b,a
 179+ E091 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 180+ E092 E6 FE                and $FE
 181+ E094 B0                   or b
 182+ E095 77                   ld (hl),a
 183+ E096 23                   inc hl
 184+ E097 23                   inc hl
 185+ E098 7A                   ld a,d                          ; +4 bit 0: y bit 8
 186+ E099 E6 01                and 1
 187+ E09B 57                   ld d,a
 188+ E09C 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 189+ E09D E6 FE                and $FE
 190+ E09F B2                   or d
 191+ E0A0 77                   ld (hl),a
 192+ E0A1 C9                   ret
 193+ E0A2
 194+ E0A2              ; Sprite A: pattern E (0..63), keeping whether it is shown
 195+ E0A2              spr_pattern:
 196+ E0A2 CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 197+ E0A5 7D                   ld a,l
 198+ E0A6 C6 03                add a,3
 199+ E0A8 6F                   ld l,a
 200+ E0A9 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 201+ E0AA E6 C0                and $C0
 202+ E0AC B3                   or e
 203+ E0AD 77                   ld (hl),a
 204+ E0AE C9                   ret
 205+ E0AF
 206+ E0AF              ; Sprite A: flags E (SPRF_*: palette offset, mirrors, rotation)
 207+ E0AF              spr_flags:
 208+ E0AF CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 209+ E0B2 23                   inc hl
 210+ E0B3 23                   inc hl
 211+ E0B4 7B                   ld a,e
 212+ E0B5 E6 FE                and $FE
 213+ E0B7 5F                   ld e,a
 214+ E0B8 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 215+ E0B9 E6 01                and 1
 216+ E0BB B3                   or e
 217+ E0BC 77                   ld (hl),a
 218+ E0BD C9                   ret
 219+ E0BE
 220+ E0BE              ; Sprite A: scale E across, D down (0..3 = x1, x2, x4, x8)
 221+ E0BE              spr_scale:
 222+ E0BE D5                   push de
 223+ E0BF CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 224+ E0C2 D1                   pop de
 225+ E0C3 7D                   ld a,l
 226+ E0C4 C6 04                add a,4
 227+ E0C6 6F                   ld l,a
 228+ E0C7 7B                   ld a,e
 229+ E0C8 E6 03                and 3
 230+ E0CA 07                   rlca
 231+ E0CB 07                   rlca
 232+ E0CC 5F                   ld e,a
 233+ E0CD 7A                   ld a,d
 234+ E0CE E6 03                and 3
 235+ E0D0 B3                   or e
 236+ E0D1 07                   rlca                            ; x scale bits 4-3, y scale 2-1
 237+ E0D2 5F                   ld e,a
 238+ E0D3 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 239+ E0D4 E6 01                and 1                           ; (keep y bit 8)
 240+ E0D6 B3                   or e
 241+ E0D7 77                   ld (hl),a
 242+ E0D8 C9                   ret
 243+ E0D9
 244+ E0D9              ; Sprite A: hide / show
 245+ E0D9              spr_hide:
 246+ E0D9 CD F3 E0             call spr_addr3
 247+ E0DC CB BE                res 7,(hl)
 248+ E0DE C9                   ret
 249+ E0DF              spr_show:
 250+ E0DF CD F3 E0             call spr_addr3
 251+ E0E2 CB FE                set 7,(hl)
 252+ E0E4 C9                   ret
 253+ E0E5
 254+ E0E5              ; Hide every sprite in the table
 255+ E0E5              spr_hide_all:
 256+ E0E5 21 03 E2             ld hl,spr_shadow+3
 257+ E0E8 11 05 00             ld de,SPR_ATTR
 258+ E0EB 06 20                ld b,SPR_COUNT
 259+ E0ED CB BE        .lp:    res 7,(hl)
 260+ E0EF 19                   add hl,de
 261+ E0F0 10 FB                djnz .lp
 262+ E0F2 C9                   ret
 263+ E0F3
 264+ E0F3              ; A = sprite -> HL -> its attributes in the table, +3 for spr_addr3.
 265+ E0F3              ; (The table doesn't cross a 256-byte boundary, so +n is just L + n.)
 266+ E0F3              spr_addr3:
 267+ E0F3 CD FA E0             call spr_addr
 268+ E0F6 23                   inc hl
 269+ E0F7 23                   inc hl
 270+ E0F8 23                   inc hl

 286+ E104              ;   HSPR_FIELD   0  waiting in the field, not yet rescued: an empty square

# file opened: initialise.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - start-up initialisation (8K page PAGE_INIT, runs at $E000 in
   3+ E000              ; slot 7)
   4+ E000              ;
   5+ E000              ; Code needed only once, at start-up, kept out of the resident page: start
   6+ E000              ; (zpolice.asm) pages this in with nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_INIT and calls
   7+ E000              ; initgame, then slot 7 is free for the other core pages.
   8+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   9+ E000
  10+ E000              ; Hardware set-up: 28MHz, Layer 2 mode, transparency, layer priority, Layer
  11+ E000              ; 2 scroll and clip window, the memory map of the always-mapped slots, and
  12+ E000              ; a black border.
  13+ E000              initgame:
  14+ E000 ED 91 07 03          nextreg $07,3                   ; 28MHz
  15+ E004 ED 91 70 00          nextreg $70,0                   ; Layer 2 256x192 mode
  16+ E008 ED 91 14 E3          nextreg $14,$E3                 ; global transparency colour
  17+ E00C ED 91 15 08          nextreg $15,%00001000           ; priority S U L (ULA over L2); sprites
  18+ E010                                                      ; off until sprites_init
  19+ E010 ED 91 16 00          nextreg $16,0                   ; Layer 2 scroll X
  20+ E014 ED 91 17 00          nextreg $17,0                   ; Layer 2 scroll Y
  21+ E018 ED 91 1C 01          nextreg $1C,%00000001           ; reset L2 clip index
  22+ E01C ED 91 18 00          nextreg $18,0                   ; Layer 2 clip: x 0..255,
  23+ E020 ED 91 18 FF          nextreg $18,255
  24+ E024 ED 91 18 00          nextreg $18,0                   ; y 0..191: the viewport, and the
  25+ E028 ED 91 18 BF          nextreg $18,191                 ; city map through the ULA panel
  26+ E02C ED 91 54 0A          nextreg ULA_MMU,PAGE_ULA0       ; ULA screen (bank 5) at $8000
  27+ E030 ED 91 51 05          nextreg ZOMBIE_MMU,PAGE_ZOMBIE  ; zombie code/data stays at $2000
  28+ E034 ED 91 52 06          nextreg OCC_MMU,PAGE_OCC0       ; occlusion data at $4000-$5FFF,
  29+ E038 ED 91 53 07          nextreg TABLES_MMU,PAGE_OCC1    ; city_col at $6000-$6FFF
  30+ E03C AF                   xor a
  31+ E03D D3 FE                out ($FE),a                     ; black border
  32+ E03F C9                   ret

   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - player page (8K page PAGE_PLAYER, runs at $C000 in slot 6)
   3+ C000              ;
   4+ C000              ; Keyboard input, movement along the heading, world-edge clamping and
   5+ C000              ; building collision.  Paged in by main_loop with nextreg LOGIC_MMU,
   6+ C000              ; PAGE_PLAYER, with the keys page (key_held) in slot 7.
   7+ C000              ; Uses the resident helpers get_sin, fmul and bld_height; camera state (cam_x/z/y, yaw, pitch, speed) is shared and
   8+ C000              ; stays in zpolice.asm.

  19+ C006 11 CE C1             ld de,old_pos                   ; the VTOL's nose and tail)

  87+ C08F CD 52 E0             call key_held
  88+ C092 20 14                jr nz,.held
  89+ C094 3E 08                ld a,K_NOSE_DN

 111+ C0C4 3E 06        .ne:    ld a,K_DESCEND
 112+ C0C6 CD 52 E0             call key_held

 115+ C0CE 11 FC FF             ld de,-CLIMB_RATE

 149+ C11A              .moved:
 150+ C11A CD 89 C1             call check_collision

 152+ C11E                      ; crashed - restore and stop

 155+ C124 01 0A 00             ld bc,10

 159+ C12D 3E 03                ld a,C_WARN

 161+ C131 C9                   ret

 163+ C132              ; IX -> 24-bit position (frac, lo, hi). HL = signed delta in 1/256 units.
 164+ C132              ; Clamps the integer part to WORLD_MIN..WORLD_MAX.
 165+ C132              add_pos:

 167+ C133 17                   rla
 168+ C134 9F                   sbc a,a
 169+ C135 4F                   ld c,a                          ; sign extension
 170+ C136 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)
 171+ C139 85                   add a,l
 172+ C13A DD 77 00             ld (ix+0),a
 173+ C13D DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)
 174+ C140 8C                   adc a,h
 175+ C141 DD 77 01             ld (ix+1),a
 176+ C144 DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+2)
 177+ C147 89                   adc a,c
 178+ C148 DD 77 02             ld (ix+2),a
 179+ C14B DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+1)
 180+ C14E DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+2)
 181+ C151 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 182+ C153 20 1B                jr nz,.lo
 183+ C155 11 40 00             ld de,WORLD_MIN
 184+ C158 B7                   or a
 185+ C159 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 186+ C15B 38 13                jr c,.lo
 187+ C15D DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+1)
 188+ C160 DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+2)
 189+ C163 11 C0 3F             ld de,WORLD_MAX
 190+ C166 CD 79 C1             call min_hl_de
 191+ C169 DD 75 01             ld (ix+1),l
 192+ C16C DD 74 02             ld (ix+2),h
 193+ C16F C9                   ret
 194+ C170 DD 36 01 40  .lo:    ld (ix+1),WORLD_MIN & $FF
 195+ C174 DD 36 02 00          ld (ix+2),WORLD_MIN >> 8
 196+ C178 C9                   ret
 197+ C179
 198+ C179              ; Unsigned 16-bit clamps
 199+ C179              min_hl_de:
 200+ C179 E5                   push hl
 201+ C17A B7                   or a
 202+ C17B ED 52                sbc hl,de
 203+ C17D E1                   pop hl
 204+ C17E D8                   ret c
 205+ C17F EB                   ex de,hl
 206+ C180 C9                   ret
 207+ C181
 208+ C181              max_hl_de:
 209+ C181 E5                   push hl
 210+ C182 B7                   or a
 211+ C183 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 212+ C185 E1                   pop hl
 213+ C186 D0                   ret nc
 214+ C187 EB                   ex de,hl
 215+ C188 C9                   ret
 216+ C189
 217+ C189              ; Returns NZ if the VTOL hits a building: its footprint (10 m x 3 m, turned
 218+ C189              ; to the heading; vtol_ground) is over a roof less than COLL_H below it.
 219+ C189              check_collision:
 220+ C189 CD F4 B3             call vtol_ground                ; HL = highest ground under it
 221+ C18C C8                   ret z                           ; all road
 222+ C18D 11 0C 00             ld de,COLL_H
 223+ C190 19                   add hl,de
 224+ C191 ED 5B 3D B6          ld de,(cam_y)
 225+ C195 B7                   or a
 226+ C196 ED 52                sbc hl,de                       ; height+M - camY
 227+ C198 38 05                jr c,.no
 228+ C19A 28 03                jr z,.no
 229+ C19C F6 01                or 1                            ; NZ: collision
 230+ C19E C9                   ret
 231+ C19F AF           .no:    xor a
 232+ C1A0 C9                   ret
 233+ C1A1
 234+ C1A1              ; One damping step: move the signed 8.8 pitch (pitch_frac/pitch) towards 0
 235+ C1A1              ; by max(PDAMP_MIN, |whole pitch| * PDAMP_K), stopping exactly at 0.
 236+ C1A1              ; Calculated rather than looked up: the Z80N MUL makes it as quick as a
 237+ C1A1              ; table, and the curve stays editable through the two equates.
 238+ C1A1              pitch_damp:
 239+ C1A1 2A 41 B6             ld hl,(pitch_frac)
 240+ C1A4 7C                   ld a,h
 241+ C1A5 B5                   or l
 242+ C1A6 C8                   ret z                           ; already level
 243+ C1A7 7C                   ld a,h
 244+ C1A8 F5                   push af                         ; sign
 245+ C1A9 CD F9 B5             call abs_hl
 246+ C1AC 54                   ld d,h                          ; whole part of |pitch|
 247+ C1AD 1E 2B                ld e,PDAMP_K
 248+ C1AF ED 30                mul d,e                         ; DE = step
 249+ C1B1 7A                   ld a,d
 250+ C1B2 B7                   or a
 251+ C1B3 20 08                jr nz,.sub
 252+ C1B5 7B                   ld a,e
 253+ C1B6 FE 20                cp PDAMP_MIN
 254+ C1B8 30 03                jr nc,.sub
 255+ C1BA 11 20 00             ld de,PDAMP_MIN
 256+ C1BD B7           .sub:   or a
 257+ C1BE ED 52                sbc hl,de
 258+ C1C0 30 03                jr nc,.sgn
 259+ C1C2 21 00 00             ld hl,0                         ; would pass level: stop at 0
 260+ C1C5 F1           .sgn:   pop af
 261+ C1C6 17                   rla                             ; carry = was nose down
 262+ C1C7 DC F2 B5             call c,neg_hl
 263+ C1CA 22 41 B6             ld (pitch_frac),hl
 264+ C1CD C9                   ret

# file opened: weapons.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - weapons page (8K page PAGE_WEAPONS, runs at $C000 in slot 6)
   3+ C000              ;
   4+ C000              ; Player weapons.  Paged in by main_loop with nextreg LOGIC_MMU,PAGE_WEAPONS
   5+ C000              ; (the keys page in slot 7) after
   6+ C000              ; the frame is set up (it uses sin_y/cos_y/sin_p/cos_p) and the zombies have
   7+ C000              ; moved.  Bullet state lives in the resident `bullets` table so that the city
   8+ C000              ; page can draw the tracers (draw_bullets).
   9+ C000              ;
  10+ C000              ; Keys 1-5 select the weapon slot that SPACE fires (wpn_sel: 1 = the centre
  11+ C000              ; slot, 2-5 = the wing pylons).  An empty slot isn't selected: the HUD
  12+ C000              ; flashes it instead (wflash_slot / wflash_t).  Key 0 cycles the centre slot: machine gun,
  13+ C000              ; pulse laser, chain gun.
  14+ C000              ;
  15+ C000              ; Machine gun (WPN_GUN, the centre slot): hold SPACE to fire one round every
  16+ C000              ; GUN_INTERVAL frames.  Rounds leave the ship's centre line 0.5 m below the
  17+ C000              ; camera and fly straight along the view direction at 500 m/s until they hit
  18+ C000              ; something.
  19+ C000              ;
  20+ C000              ; Collision, every frame for every round in flight: the path from its last
  21+ C000              ; position to its new one (BULLET_STEP long) is walked cell by cell through
  22+ C000              ; the city grid, in order (a 2D DDA on the horizontal distance s along the
  23+ C000              ; path).  The first thing met ends the path:
  24+ C000              ;   * a building cell: hit where the path enters the cell if it is below the
  25+ C000              ;     roof there (a wall), otherwise where it drops below the roof (the roof);
  26+ C000              ;   * a street cell: the zombies standing in it (zcell lists), each a box
  27+ C000              ;     0.5 m wide and 2 m tall, nearest along the path first;
  28+ C000              ;   * the ground, where the path reaches height 0;
  29+ C000              ;   * leaving the GRID x GRID map: the round is gone.
  30+ C000              ; A round that hits is drawn once at the impact point, then freed.  A zombie
  31+ C000              ; that is hit loses B_DMG hit points; at 0 it is killed (its map blip
  32+ C000              ; removed, its record made inactive).  A rocket also bursts (rocket_blast).
  33+ C000              ;
  34+ C000              ; Rocket pod (WPN_ROCKETS, a wing pylon, ROCKET_SHOTS rockets): rockets are
  35+ C000              ; rounds too (B_KIND = BK_ROCKET), at 150 m/s and drawn as a short white
  36+ C000              ; streak with a yellow trail.  Wherever one stops (a building, the ground,
  37+ C000              ; a zombie) rocket_blast does BLAST_DMG to every zombie, human and base tile
  38+ C000              ; within BLAST_R on each axis.
  39+ C000              ;
  40+ C000              ; Damage: each round carries its damage (B_DMG): machine gun GUN_DMG, chain
  41+ C000              ; gun CHAIN_DMG, the laser LASER_DMG a beam frame, a rocket none directly
  42+ C000              ; (only its blast).  A zombie dies when its hit points (+18) run out.
  43+ C000              ;
  44+ C000              ; Positions are world units (1 tile = 20 m = 256 units, 1 m = 12.8).  Along the path,
  45+ C000              ; x = x0 + s*sinY, z = z0 + s*cosY and y = y0 + s*slope, with sinY/cosY the
  46+ C000              ; S7.8 heading at firing and slope = tan(pitch) in S7.8.
  47+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  48+ C000
  49+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  50+ C000              ; Per frame: fire, then move every round in flight
  51+ C000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  52+ C000              update_weapons:
  53+ C000 CD DC C2             call part_update                ; age and move the burst particles
  54+ C003 CD C3 C1             call wpn_select
  55+ C006 CD 2F C0             call gun_fire
  56+ C009 CD 2E C2             call laser_update
  57+ C00C DD 21 7D B6          ld ix,bullets
  58+ C010 06 18                ld b,NBULLETS
  59+ C012 C5           .lp:    push bc
  60+ C013 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
  61+ C016 B7                   or a
  62+ C017 28 0D                jr z,.nx
  63+ C019 FE 02                cp 2
  64+ C01B 20 06                jr nz,.fly
  65+ C01D DD 36 00 00          ld (ix+B_STATE),0               ; its impact was shown last frame
  66+ C021 18 03                jr .nx
  67+ C023 CD 47 C3     .fly:   call bullet_step
  68+ C026 11 1B 00     .nx:    ld de,BREC
  69+ C029 DD 19                add ix,de
  70+ C02B C1                   pop bc
  71+ C02C 10 E4                djnz .lp
  72+ C02E C9                   ret
  73+ C02F
  74+ C02F              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  75+ C02F              ; Machine gun / chain gun: SPACE held with one of them in the centre slot,
  76+ C02F              ; ammo, and a free round
  77+ C02F              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  78+ C02F              gun_fire:
  79+ C02F 21 BD C8             ld hl,gun_wait
  80+ C032 7E                   ld a,(hl)
  81+ C033 B7                   or a
  82+ C034 28 02                jr z,.ready
  83+ C036 35                   dec (hl)                        ; still cycling
  84+ C037 C9                   ret
  85+ C038 3E 09        .ready: ld a,K_FIRE
  86+ C03A CD 52 E0             call key_held
  87+ C03D C8                   ret z                           ; fire not pressed
  88+ C03E CD 24 C2             call sel_slot
  89+ C041 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; the selected slot's weapon
  90+ C042 FE 03                cp WPN_CHAIN
  91+ C044 28 37                jr z,.chain
  92+ C046 FE 04                cp WPN_ROCKETS
  93+ C048 28 1B                jr z,.rocket
  94+ C04A FE 01                cp WPN_GUN
  95+ C04C C0                   ret nz
  96+ C04D CD 1D C1             call free_round                 ; machine gun: one round
  97+ C050 D8                   ret c
  98+ C051 CD 0F C1             call take_ammo
  99+ C054 D8                   ret c
 100+ C055 3E 03                ld a,GUN_INTERVAL-1
 101+ C057 32 BD C8             ld (gun_wait),a
 102+ C05A 21 C9 01             ld hl,BULLET_STEP
 103+ C05D CD 31 C1             call spawn_round
 104+ C060 DD 36 19 04          ld (ix+B_DMG),GUN_DMG
 105+ C064 C9                   ret
 106+ C065              .rocket:
 107+ C065 CD 1D C1             call free_round                 ; rocket pod: one rocket
 108+ C068 D8                   ret c
 109+ C069 CD 0F C1             call take_ammo
 110+ C06C D8                   ret c
 111+ C06D 3E 06                ld a,ROCKET_INTERVAL-1
 112+ C06F 32 BD C8             ld (gun_wait),a
 113+ C072 21 89 00             ld hl,ROCKET_STEP
 114+ C075 CD 31 C1             call spawn_round
 115+ C078 DD 36 1A 01          ld (ix+B_KIND),BK_ROCKET        ; no direct damage: its blast
 116+ C07C C9                   ret
 117+ C07D              .chain: ; chain gun: one round every CHAIN_INTERVAL frames
 118+ C07D CD 1D C1             call free_round
 119+ C080 D8                   ret c
 120+ C081 CD 0F C1             call take_ammo
 121+ C084 D8                   ret c
 122+ C085 3E 00                ld a,CHAIN_INTERVAL-1
 123+ C087 32 BD C8             ld (gun_wait),a
 124+ C08A                      ; fall through
 125+ C08A
 126+ C08A              ; A chain gun round from IX: the view direction turned by a random angle
 127+ C08A              ; of -CHAIN_DEV..+CHAIN_DEV steps (1/256 rad) sideways and up/down
 128+ C08A              chain_round:
 129+ C08A 21 C9 01             ld hl,BULLET_STEP
 130+ C08D CD 31 C1             call spawn_round
 131+ C090 DD 36 19 02          ld (ix+B_DMG),CHAIN_DMG
 132+ C094 CD F6 39             call zrand                      ; (zombie page)
 133+ C097 E5                   push hl
 134+ C098 7D                   ld a,l
 135+ C099 CD 00 C1             call dev_n                      ; sideways: rotate (sin, cos) by d/256
 136+ C09C B7                   or a
 137+ C09D 28 3F                jr z,.v
 138+ C09F 67                   ld h,a
 139+ C0A0 2E 00                ld l,0
 140+ C0A2 22 C2 C8             ld (cg_d),hl                    ; d * 256
 141+ C0A5 DD 5E 09             ld e,(ix+B_COS)                 ; sin += round(d * cos / 256)
 142+ C0A8 DD 56 0A             ld d,(ix+B_COS+1)
 143+ C0AB CD F3 C0             call dmul
 144+ C0AE 22 C4 C8             ld (cg_s),hl
 145+ C0B1 2A C2 C8             ld hl,(cg_d)                    ; cos -= round(d * sin / 256)
 146+ C0B4 DD 5E 07             ld e,(ix+B_SIN)
 147+ C0B7 DD 56 08             ld d,(ix+B_SIN+1)
 148+ C0BA CD F3 C0             call dmul
 149+ C0BD EB                   ex de,hl
 150+ C0BE DD 6E 09             ld l,(ix+B_COS)
 151+ C0C1 DD 66 0A             ld h,(ix+B_COS+1)
 152+ C0C4 B7                   or a
 153+ C0C5 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 154+ C0C7 DD 75 09             ld (ix+B_COS),l
 155+ C0CA DD 74 0A             ld (ix+B_COS+1),h
 156+ C0CD DD 6E 07             ld l,(ix+B_SIN)
 157+ C0D0 DD 66 08             ld h,(ix+B_SIN+1)
 158+ C0D3 ED 5B C4 C8          ld de,(cg_s)
 159+ C0D7 19                   add hl,de
 160+ C0D8 DD 75 07             ld (ix+B_SIN),l
 161+ C0DB DD 74 08             ld (ix+B_SIN+1),h
 162+ C0DE E1           .v:     pop hl                          ; up/down: slope (tan) +/- d/256
 163+ C0DF 7C                   ld a,h
 164+ C0E0 CD 00 C1             call dev_n
 165+ C0E3 DD 6E 0B             ld l,(ix+B_SLOPE)
 166+ C0E6 DD 66 0C             ld h,(ix+B_SLOPE+1)
 167+ C0E9 CD 09 C1             call add_sa
 168+ C0EC DD 75 0B             ld (ix+B_SLOPE),l
 169+ C0EF DD 74 0C             ld (ix+B_SLOPE+1),h
 170+ C0F2 C9                   ret
 171+ C0F3
 172+ C0F3              ; HL = d * 256, DE = S7.8 value -> HL = d * value / 256, rounded
 173+ C0F3              dmul:
 174+ C0F3 CD FE B5             call fmul                       ; HL = d * value (exact)
 175+ C0F6 11 80 00             ld de,128
 176+ C0F9 19                   add hl,de
 177+ C0FA 6C                   ld l,h                          ; >> 8, keeping the sign
 178+ C0FB 7C                   ld a,h
 179+ C0FC 17                   rla
 180+ C0FD 9F                   sbc a,a
 181+ C0FE 67                   ld h,a
 182+ C0FF C9                   ret
 183+ C100
 184+ C100              ; A = random byte -> A = -CHAIN_DEV..+CHAIN_DEV, evenly spread
 185+ C100              dev_n:
 186+ C100 57                   ld d,a
 187+ C101 1E 13                ld e,2*CHAIN_DEV+1
 188+ C103 ED 30                mul d,e
 189+ C105 7A                   ld a,d
 190+ C106 D6 09                sub CHAIN_DEV
 191+ C108 C9                   ret
 192+ C109
 193+ C109              ; HL = HL + A (signed)
 194+ C109              add_sa:
 195+ C109 5F                   ld e,a
 196+ C10A 17                   rla
 197+ C10B 9F                   sbc a,a
 198+ C10C 57                   ld d,a
 199+ C10D 19                   add hl,de
 200+ C10E C9                   ret
 201+ C10F
 202+ C10F              ; One round from the selected slot's ammo: carry set if it is empty.
 203+ C10F              ; AMMO_INF (255) is never counted down.
 204+ C10F              take_ammo:
 205+ C10F CD 24 C2             call sel_slot
 206+ C112 23                   inc hl                          ; its ammo counter
 207+ C113 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 208+ C114 FE FF                cp AMMO_INF
 209+ C116 C8                   ret z                           ; infinite (carry clear)
 210+ C117 B7                   or a
 211+ C118 37                   scf
 212+ C119 C8                   ret z                           ; empty
 213+ C11A 35                   dec (hl)
 214+ C11B B7                   or a
 215+ C11C C9                   ret
 216+ C11D
 217+ C11D              ; IX = a free round, carry clear; carry set if all are in flight
 218+ C11D              free_round:
 219+ C11D DD 21 7D B6          ld ix,bullets
 220+ C121 06 18                ld b,NBULLETS
 221+ C123 11 1B 00             ld de,BREC
 222+ C126 DD 7E 00     .f:     ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
 223+ C129 B7                   or a
 224+ C12A C8                   ret z                           ; (carry clear)
 225+ C12B DD 19                add ix,de
 226+ C12D 10 F7                djnz .f
 227+ C12F 37                   scf
 228+ C130 C9                   ret
 229+ C131              ; Start round IX at the muzzle along the view direction, travelling HL
 230+ C131              ; units (along its path) per frame
 231+ C131              spawn_round:
 232+ C131 22 C0 C8             ld (sr_len),hl
 233+ C134 DD 36 00 81          ld (ix+B_STATE),$81             ; in flight, fresh: its first path
 234+ C138                                                      ; starts at the muzzle (B_PX..)
 235+ C138 AF                   xor a                           ; cell intervals: on its first step
 236+ C139 DD 77 15             ld (ix+B_TDX),a
 237+ C13C DD 77 16             ld (ix+B_TDX+1),a
 238+ C13F DD 77 1A             ld (ix+B_KIND),a                ; a round, no damage (the caller
 239+ C142 DD 77 19             ld (ix+B_DMG),a                 ; sets them)
 240+ C145                      ; muzzle: the ship's centre line, GUN_DY below the camera
 241+ C145 2A 38 B6             ld hl,(cam_x+1)
 242+ C148 DD 75 01             ld (ix+B_X),l
 243+ C14B DD 74 02             ld (ix+B_X+1),h
 244+ C14E 2A 3D B6             ld hl,(cam_y)
 245+ C151 11 FA FF             ld de,GUN_DY
 246+ C154 19                   add hl,de
 247+ C155 DD 75 03             ld (ix+B_Y),l
 248+ C158 DD 74 04             ld (ix+B_Y+1),h
 249+ C15B 2A 3B B6             ld hl,(cam_z+1)
 250+ C15E DD 75 05             ld (ix+B_Z),l
 251+ C161 DD 74 06             ld (ix+B_Z+1),h
 252+ C164 DD 75 13             ld (ix+B_PZ),l
 253+ C167 DD 74 14             ld (ix+B_PZ+1),h
 254+ C16A DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+B_X)
 255+ C16D DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+B_X+1)
 256+ C170 DD 75 0F             ld (ix+B_PX),l
 257+ C173 DD 74 10             ld (ix+B_PX+1),h
 258+ C176 DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+B_Y)
 259+ C179 DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+B_Y+1)
 260+ C17C DD 75 11             ld (ix+B_PY),l
 261+ C17F DD 74 12             ld (ix+B_PY+1),h
 262+ C182                      ; direction: the view direction this frame
 263+ C182 2A B1 BA             ld hl,(sin_y)
 264+ C185 DD 75 07             ld (ix+B_SIN),l
 265+ C188 DD 74 08             ld (ix+B_SIN+1),h
 266+ C18B 2A B3 BA             ld hl,(cos_y)
 267+ C18E DD 75 09             ld (ix+B_COS),l
 268+ C191 DD 74 0A             ld (ix+B_COS+1),h
 269+ C194 2A C0 C8             ld hl,(sr_len)                  ; horizontal distance per frame
 270+ C197 ED 5B B7 BA          ld de,(cos_p)
 271+ C19B CD FE B5             call fmul
 272+ C19E DD 75 0D             ld (ix+B_S),l
 273+ C1A1 DD 74 0E             ld (ix+B_S+1),h
 274+ C1A4 2A B5 BA             ld hl,(sin_p)                   ; slope = sinP / cosP  (S7.8)
 275+ C1A7 7C                   ld a,h
 276+ C1A8 F5                   push af
 277+ C1A9 CD F9 B5             call abs_hl
 278+ C1AC 7C                   ld a,h                          ; A:HL = |sinP| << 8
 279+ C1AD 65                   ld h,l
 280+ C1AE 2E 00                ld l,0
 281+ C1B0 ED 5B B7 BA          ld de,(cos_p)
 282+ C1B4 CD 86 C8             call udiv24
 283+ C1B7 F1                   pop af
 284+ C1B8 17                   rla
 285+ C1B9 DC F2 B5             call c,neg_hl
 286+ C1BC DD 75 0B             ld (ix+B_SLOPE),l
 287+ C1BF DD 74 0C             ld (ix+B_SLOPE+1),h
 288+ C1C2 C9                   ret
 289+ C1C3
 290+ C1C3              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 291+ C1C3              ; Key 0: cycle the centre slot through machine gun, pulse laser and chain
 292+ C1C3              ; gun (once per press)
 293+ C1C3              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 294+ C1C3              wpn_select:
 295+ C1C3 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; slot keys, newly pressed
 296+ C1C5 79           .k:     ld a,c
 297+ C1C6 C6 0B                add a,K_SLOT1
 298+ C1C8 CD 59 E0             call key_pressed
 299+ C1CB 20 08                jr nz,.sel
 300+ C1CD 0C                   inc c
 301+ C1CE 79                   ld a,c
 302+ C1CF FE 05                cp WPN_SLOTS
 303+ C1D1 20 F2                jr nz,.k
 304+ C1D3 18 23                jr .cyc
 305+ C1D5 79           .sel:   ld a,c
 306+ C1D6 87                   add a,a
 307+ C1D7 21 44 B6             ld hl,wpn_slots
 308+ C1DA ED 31                add hl,a
 309+ C1DC 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 310+ C1DD B7                   or a
 311+ C1DE 20 0B                jr nz,.fitted
 312+ C1E0 79                   ld a,c                          ; empty: keep the current slot and
 313+ C1E1 32 4F B6             ld (wflash_slot),a              ; have the HUD flash the asked-for one
 314+ C1E4 3E 1E                ld a,6*WFLASH
 315+ C1E6 32 50 B6             ld (wflash_t),a
 316+ C1E9 18 0D                jr .cyc
 317+ C1EB              .fitted:
 318+ C1EB 3A 4E B6             ld a,(wpn_sel)
 319+ C1EE B9                   cp c
 320+ C1EF 28 07                jr z,.cyc                       ; already selected
 321+ C1F1 79                   ld a,c
 322+ C1F2 32 4E B6             ld (wpn_sel),a
 323+ C1F5 CD 19 C2             call las_reset                  ; no beam carried to the new slot
 324+ C1F8 3E 0A        .cyc:   ld a,K_WEAPON                   ; cycle the centre slot's weapon,
 325+ C1FA CD 59 E0             call key_pressed                ; once per press
 326+ C1FD C8                   ret z
 327+ C1FE 21 44 B6             ld hl,wpn_slots+WPN_CENTRE*2+WPN_TYPE
 328+ C201 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; gun -> laser -> chain gun -> gun
 329+ C202 FE 01                cp WPN_GUN
 330+ C204 3E 02                ld a,WPN_LASER
 331+ C206 28 09                jr z,.set
 332+ C208 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 333+ C209 FE 02                cp WPN_LASER
 334+ C20B 3E 03                ld a,WPN_CHAIN
 335+ C20D 28 02                jr z,.set
 336+ C20F 3E 01                ld a,WPN_GUN
 337+ C211 77           .set:   ld (hl),a
 338+ C212 23                   inc hl
 339+ C213 36 FF                ld (hl),AMMO_INF                ; all have infinite ammo
 340+ C215                      ; fall through: no beam or cool-down carried over
 341+ C215
 342+ C215              ; New game: no gun cycling, no beam or cool-down
 343+ C215              weapons_reset:
 344+ C215 AF                   xor a
 345+ C216 32 BD C8             ld (gun_wait),a
 346+ C219                      ; fall through
 347+ C219
 348+ C219              ; Stop any laser beam or cool-down
 349+ C219              las_reset:
 350+ C219 AF                   xor a
 351+ C21A 32 BE C8             ld (las_on),a
 352+ C21D 32 BF C8             ld (las_cool),a
 353+ C220 32 05 B9             ld (laser_rec+B_STATE),a
 354+ C223 C9                   ret
 355+ C224
 356+ C224              ; HL -> the selected weapon slot (type, then ammo)
 357+ C224              sel_slot:
 358+ C224 3A 4E B6             ld a,(wpn_sel)
 359+ C227 87                   add a,a
 360+ C228 21 44 B6             ld hl,wpn_slots
 361+ C22B ED 31                add hl,a
 362+ C22D C9                   ret
 363+ C22E
 364+ C22E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 365+ C22E              ; Pulse laser: SPACE held with the laser in the centre slot fires a beam for
 366+ C22E              ; LASER_ON frames, then it cools for LASER_COOL frames.  Each beam frame the
 367+ C22E              ; beam is traced from the muzzle along the current view direction (the round
 368+ C22E              ; walk, LASER_RANGE long) to the first building, ground or zombie, killing a
 369+ C22E              ; zombie it reaches.  It is kept in laser_rec as a round (state 3) whose
 370+ C22E              ; path runs from the muzzle to where the beam stops; draw_bullets clips it
 371+ C22E              ; to the view, so it rises from the bottom edge of the screen.
 372+ C22E              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 373+ C22E              laser_update:
 374+ C22E 3A BE C8             ld a,(las_on)
 375+ C231 B7                   or a
 376+ C232 20 20                jr nz,.beam
 377+ C234 32 05 B9             ld (laser_rec+B_STATE),a        ; no beam this frame
 378+ C237 3A BF C8             ld a,(las_cool)
 379+ C23A B7                   or a
 380+ C23B 28 05                jr z,.ready
 381+ C23D 3D                   dec a                           ; cooling down
 382+ C23E 32 BF C8             ld (las_cool),a
 383+ C241 C9                   ret
 384+ C242 CD 24 C2     .ready: call sel_slot
 385+ C245 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 386+ C246 FE 02                cp WPN_LASER
 387+ C248 C0                   ret nz
 388+ C249 3E 09                ld a,K_FIRE
 389+ C24B CD 52 E0             call key_held
 390+ C24E C8                   ret z
 391+ C24F 3E 03                ld a,LASER_ON
 392+ C251 32 BE C8             ld (las_on),a
 393+ C254 21 BE C8     .beam:  ld hl,las_on
 394+ C257 35                   dec (hl)
 395+ C258 20 05                jr nz,.on
 396+ C25A 3E 08                ld a,LASER_COOL                 ; last beam frame: cool down next
 397+ C25C 32 BF C8             ld (las_cool),a
 398+ C25F DD 21 05 B9  .on:    ld ix,laser_rec
 399+ C263 21 00 06             ld hl,LASER_RANGE
 400+ C266 CD 31 C1             call spawn_round                ; (start of path = the muzzle)
 401+ C269 DD 36 19 01          ld (ix+B_DMG),LASER_DMG         ; per beam frame on a target
 402+ C26D CD 47 C3             call bullet_step                ; trace it to the first thing it meets
 403+ C270 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+B_STATE)
 404+ C273 B7                   or a
 405+ C274 C8                   ret z                           ; off the map (can't, inside the wall)
 406+ C275 DD 36 00 03          ld (ix+B_STATE),3               ; beam
 407+ C279 C9                   ret
 408+ C27A
 409+ C27A              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 410+ C27A              ; Zombie burst particles (pool `particles`, drawn by draw_particles)
 411+ C27A              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 412+ C27A              ; IX = a zombie just killed: spray NPART_BURST particles from its body, each
 413+ C27A              ; with a random velocity (sideways -4..3 units a frame in x and z, upwards
 414+ C27A              ; 2..5).  Uses free particles only; if the pool runs out, fewer.
 415+ C27A              part_burst:
 416+ C27A FD E5                push iy
 417+ C27C FD 21 20 B9          ld iy,particles
 418+ C280 06 28                ld b,NPART
 419+ C282 0E 0A                ld c,NPART_BURST
 420+ C284 FD 7E 00     .lp:    ld a,(iy+0)
 421+ C287 B7                   or a
 422+ C288 20 48                jr nz,.nx                       ; in use
 423+ C28A FD 36 00 01          ld (iy+0),1                     ; age 1: brightest
 424+ C28E DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)                     ; at the zombie, PART_Y up
 425+ C291 FD 77 01             ld (iy+1),a
 426+ C294 DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)
 427+ C297 FD 77 02             ld (iy+2),a
 428+ C29A FD 36 03 0D          ld (iy+3),PART_Y
 429+ C29E FD 36 04 00          ld (iy+4),0
 430+ C2A2 DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+2)
 431+ C2A5 FD 77 05             ld (iy+5),a
 432+ C2A8 DD 7E 03             ld a,(ix+3)
 433+ C2AB FD 77 06             ld (iy+6),a
 434+ C2AE C5                   push bc
 435+ C2AF CD F6 39             call zrand                      ; (zombie page)
 436+ C2B2 7D                   ld a,l
 437+ C2B3 E6 07                and 7
 438+ C2B5 D6 04                sub 4
 439+ C2B7 FD 77 07             ld (iy+7),a                     ; vx -4..3
 440+ C2BA 7C                   ld a,h
 441+ C2BB E6 07                and 7
 442+ C2BD D6 04                sub 4
 443+ C2BF FD 77 09             ld (iy+9),a                     ; vz -4..3
 444+ C2C2 7D                   ld a,l
 445+ C2C3 0F                   rrca
 446+ C2C4 0F                   rrca
 447+ C2C5 0F                   rrca
 448+ C2C6 0F                   rrca
 449+ C2C7 E6 03                and 3
 450+ C2C9 C6 02                add a,2
 451+ C2CB FD 77 08             ld (iy+8),a                     ; vy 2..5 (up)
 452+ C2CE C1                   pop bc
 453+ C2CF 0D                   dec c
 454+ C2D0 28 07                jr z,.done
 455+ C2D2 11 0A 00     .nx:    ld de,PREC
 456+ C2D5 FD 19                add iy,de
 457+ C2D7 10 AB                djnz .lp
 458+ C2D9 FD E1        .done:  pop iy
 459+ C2DB C9                   ret
 460+ C2DC
 461+ C2DC              ; Age every particle; past PART_LIFE it is freed.  Move the rest by their
 462+ C2DC              ; velocity, with gravity, stopping on the ground.
 463+ C2DC              part_update:
 464+ C2DC DD 21 20 B9          ld ix,particles
 465+ C2E0 06 28                ld b,NPART
 466+ C2E2 DD 7E 00     .lp:    ld a,(ix+0)
 467+ C2E5 B7                   or a
 468+ C2E6 28 57                jr z,.nx
 469+ C2E8 3C                   inc a
 470+ C2E9 FE 09                cp PART_LIFE+1
 471+ C2EB 38 01                jr c,.live
 472+ C2ED AF                   xor a                           ; gone
 473+ C2EE DD 77 00     .live:  ld (ix+0),a
 474+ C2F1 B7                   or a
 475+ C2F2 28 4B                jr z,.nx
 476+ C2F4 DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)                     ; x += vx
 477+ C2F7 DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+1)
 478+ C2FA DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+2)
 479+ C2FD CD 09 C1             call add_sa
 480+ C300 DD 75 01             ld (ix+1),l
 481+ C303 DD 74 02             ld (ix+2),h
 482+ C306 DD 7E 09             ld a,(ix+9)                     ; z += vz
 483+ C309 DD 6E 05             ld l,(ix+5)
 484+ C30C DD 66 06             ld h,(ix+6)
 485+ C30F CD 09 C1             call add_sa
 486+ C312 DD 75 05             ld (ix+5),l
 487+ C315 DD 74 06             ld (ix+6),h
 488+ C318 DD 7E 08             ld a,(ix+8)                     ; y += vy, vy -= gravity
 489+ C31B DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+3)
 490+ C31E DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+4)
 491+ C321 CD 09 C1             call add_sa
 492+ C324 CB 7C                bit 7,h
 493+ C326 28 09                jr z,.up
 494+ C328 21 00 00             ld hl,0                         ; on the ground: stays there
 495+ C32B DD 36 08 00          ld (ix+8),0
 496+ C32F 18 08                jr .y
 497+ C331 DD 7E 08     .up:    ld a,(ix+8)
 498+ C334 D6 01                sub PART_GRAV
 499+ C336 DD 77 08             ld (ix+8),a
 500+ C339 DD 75 03     .y:     ld (ix+3),l
 501+ C33C DD 74 04             ld (ix+4),h
 502+ C33F 11 0A 00     .nx:    ld de,PREC
 503+ C342 DD 19                add ix,de
 504+ C344 10 9C                djnz .lp
 505+ C346 C9                   ret
 506+ C347
 507+ C347              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 508+ C347              ; Move round IX one frame along its path, stopping at the first thing it hits
 509+ C347              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 510+ C347              bullet_step:
 511+ C347                      ; start of this frame's path; kept for drawing (B_PX..), except on a
 512+ C347                      ; fresh round's first step, which is drawn from the muzzle
 513+ C347 DD CB 00 7E          bit 7,(ix+B_STATE)
 514+ C34B DD CB 00 BE          res 7,(ix+B_STATE)
 515+ C34F 20 24                jr nz,.fresh
 516+ C351 DD 6E 01             ld l,(ix+B_X)
 517+ C354 DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+B_X+1)
 518+ C357 DD 75 0F             ld (ix+B_PX),l
 519+ C35A DD 74 10             ld (ix+B_PX+1),h
 520+ C35D DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+B_Y)
 521+ C360 DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+B_Y+1)
 522+ C363 DD 75 11             ld (ix+B_PY),l
 523+ C366 DD 74 12             ld (ix+B_PY+1),h
 524+ C369 DD 6E 05             ld l,(ix+B_Z)
 525+ C36C DD 66 06             ld h,(ix+B_Z+1)
 526+ C36F DD 75 13             ld (ix+B_PZ),l
 527+ C372 DD 74 14             ld (ix+B_PZ+1),h
 528+ C375 DD 6E 01     .fresh: ld l,(ix+B_X)
 529+ C378 DD 66 02             ld h,(ix+B_X+1)
 530+ C37B 22 C6 C8             ld (b_x0),hl
 531+ C37E DD 6E 03             ld l,(ix+B_Y)
 532+ C381 DD 66 04             ld h,(ix+B_Y+1)
 533+ C384 22 C8 C8             ld (b_y0),hl
 534+ C387 DD 6E 05             ld l,(ix+B_Z)
 535+ C38A DD 66 06             ld h,(ix+B_Z+1)
 536+ C38D 22 CA C8             ld (b_z0),hl
 537+ C390 DD 6E 07             ld l,(ix+B_SIN)
 538+ C393 DD 66 08             ld h,(ix+B_SIN+1)
 539+ C396 22 CC C8             ld (b_sin),hl
 540+ C399 DD 6E 09             ld l,(ix+B_COS)
 541+ C39C DD 66 0A             ld h,(ix+B_COS+1)
 542+ C39F 22 CE C8             ld (b_cos),hl
 543+ C3A2 DD 6E 0B             ld l,(ix+B_SLOPE)
 544+ C3A5 DD 66 0C             ld h,(ix+B_SLOPE+1)

 553+ C3BE                      ; does it reach the ground this frame?  s = y0 / -slope

 570+ C3E3 3E 01                ld a,1
 571+ C3E5 32 D4 C8             ld (b_gnd),a
 572+ C3E8              .nognd:
 573+ C3E8                      ; grid walk set-up.  The distance between cell boundaries depends only
 574+ C3E8                      ; on the direction: worked out (two divides) on the round's first step.
 575+ C3E8 DD 7E 15             ld a,(ix+B_TDX)
 576+ C3EB DD B6 16             or (ix+B_TDX+1)
 577+ C3EE 20 18                jr nz,.tdok

 583+ C3FF CD 00 C5             call cell_span

 585+ C405 DD 74 18             ld (ix+B_TDZ+1),h
 586+ C408 DD 6E 15     .tdok:  ld l,(ix+B_TDX)
 587+ C40B DD 66 16             ld h,(ix+B_TDX+1)

 589+ C411 DD 6E 17             ld l,(ix+B_TDZ)
 590+ C414 DD 66 18             ld h,(ix+B_TDZ+1)
 591+ C417 22 DB C8             ld (b_tdz),hl

 607+ C449 32 E0 C8             ld (b_cj),a
 608+ C44C 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 609+ C44F 22 E1 C8             ld (b_sin_),hl                  ; s where the path enters this cell
 610+ C452              .cell:  ; s_out = min(tmx, tmz, send)

 612+ C455 ED 5B D7 C8          ld de,(b_tmz)
 613+ C459 CD 72 C8             call min_u
 614+ C45C ED 5B D2 C8          ld de,(b_send)
 615+ C460 CD 72 C8             call min_u
 616+ C463 22 E3 C8             ld (b_sout),hl
 617+ C466 CD 46 C5             call cell_hit
 618+ C469 DA ED C4             jp c,.hit
 619+ C46C 2A E3 C8             ld hl,(b_sout)                  ; path ends in this cell?

 622+ C474 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 623+ C476 28 51                jr z,.end
 624+ C478 2A D5 C8             ld hl,(b_tmx)                   ; step into the next cell
 625+ C47B ED 5B D7 C8          ld de,(b_tmz)
 626+ C47F B7                   or a
 627+ C480 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 628+ C482 30 1A                jr nc,.stz

 630+ C487 22 E1 C8             ld (b_sin_),hl

 637+ C49B 77                   ld (hl),a
 638+ C49C 18 18                jr .chk
 639+ C49E 2A D7 C8     .stz:   ld hl,(b_tmz)                   ; z boundary first
 640+ C4A1 22 E1 C8             ld (b_sin_),hl
 641+ C4A4 ED 5B DB C8          ld de,(b_tdz)
 642+ C4A8 CD 80 C8             call add_sat
 643+ C4AB 22 D7 C8             ld (b_tmz),hl
 644+ C4AE 3A DE C8             ld a,(b_stz)

 647+ C4B5 77                   ld (hl),a
 648+ C4B6 3A DF C8     .chk:   ld a,(b_ci)
 649+ C4B9 FE 40                cp GRID
 650+ C4BB 30 07                jr nc,.gone                     ; left the map
 651+ C4BD 3A E0 C8             ld a,(b_cj)
 652+ C4C0 FE 40                cp GRID
 653+ C4C2 38 8E                jr c,.cell
 654+ C4C4 DD 36 00 00  .gone:  ld (ix+B_STATE),0
 655+ C4C8 C9                   ret
 656+ C4C9 2A D2 C8     .end:   ld hl,(b_send)

 658+ C4CF B7                   or a
 659+ C4D0 28 09                jr z,.fly
 660+ C4D2 CD 40 C8             call b_move                     ; reached the ground
 661+ C4D5 DD 36 00 02          ld (ix+B_STATE),2
 662+ C4D9 18 1C                jr .burst
 663+ C4DB CD 40 C8     .fly:   call b_move                     ; whole step, nothing hit
 664+ C4DE DD 7E 02             ld a,(ix+B_X+1)                 ; still over the map?

 666+ C4E3 30 DF                jr nc,.gone

 669+ C4EA 30 D8                jr nc,.gone
 670+ C4EC C9                   ret

 676+ C4FC C0                   ret nz

 678+ C500
 679+ C500              ; HL = S7.8 direction component -> HL = path distance s between cell
 680+ C500              ; boundaries along that axis, 256 * 256 / |c|  ($FFFF = never)
 681+ C500              cell_span:
 682+ C500 7C                   ld a,h
 683+ C501 B5                   or l
 684+ C502 28 0C                jr z,.never
 685+ C504 CD F9 B5             call abs_hl
 686+ C507 EB                   ex de,hl
 687+ C508 3E 01                ld a,1
 688+ C50A 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 689+ C50D C3 86 C8             jp udiv24
 690+ C510 2B           .never: dec hl                          ; $FFFF
 691+ C511 C9                   ret
 692+ C512
 693+ C512              ; HL = S7.8 direction component, A = low byte of the start coordinate,
 694+ C512              ; DE = cell_span for it.  -> HL = s to the first cell boundary ($FFFF =
 695+ C512              ; never), A = cell step (+1, -1, 0).  s = distance * span / 256, by MUL.
 696+ C512              dda_axis:
 697+ C512 47                   ld b,a
 698+ C513 7C                   ld a,h
 699+ C514 B5                   or l
 700+ C515 20 05                jr nz,.mv
 701+ C517 21 FF FF             ld hl,$FFFF                     ; not moving along this axis
 702+ C51A AF                   xor a
 703+ C51B C9                   ret
 704+ C51C 7C           .mv:    ld a,h
 705+ C51D 32 F3 C8             ld (dd_sg),a
 706+ C520 17                   rla
 707+ C521 38 07                jr c,.neg
 708+ C523 AF                   xor a                           ; moving up: 256 - (x & 255)
 709+ C524 90                   sub b
 710+ C525 20 04                jr nz,.part
 711+ C527 EB                   ex de,hl                        ; (x & 255) = 0: a whole cell
 712+ C528 18 12                jr .sgn
 713+ C52A 78           .neg:   ld a,b                          ; moving down: x & 255
 714+ C52B 4F           .part:  ld c,a                          ; HL = (dist * span) >> 8
 715+ C52C D5                   push de
 716+ C52D 57                   ld d,a
 717+ C52E ED 30                mul d,e                         ; dist * span low
 718+ C530 7A                   ld a,d
 719+ C531 D1                   pop de
 720+ C532 5A                   ld e,d
 721+ C533 51                   ld d,c
 722+ C534 ED 30                mul d,e                         ; dist * span high
 723+ C536 83                   add a,e
 724+ C537 6F                   ld l,a
 725+ C538 7A                   ld a,d
 726+ C539 CE 00                adc a,0
 727+ C53B 67                   ld h,a
 728+ C53C 3A F3 C8     .sgn:   ld a,(dd_sg)
 729+ C53F 17                   rla
 730+ C540 3E 01                ld a,1
 731+ C542 D0                   ret nc
 732+ C543 3E FF                ld a,-1
 733+ C545 C9                   ret
 734+ C546
 735+ C546              ; Carry set if the path hits something in cell (b_ci, b_cj) between
 736+ C546              ; s = b_sin_ and b_sout; b_shit = where.
 737+ C546              cell_hit:
 738+ C546 3A E0 C8             ld a,(b_cj)
 739+ C549 6F                   ld l,a
 740+ C54A 26 00                ld h,0

 741+ C54D 29          >        add hl,hl
 741+ C54E 29          >        add hl,hl
 741+ C54F 29          >        add hl,hl
 741+ C550 29          >        add hl,hl
 741+ C551 29          >        add hl,hl

 748+ C55D E1                   pop hl

 750+ C55F 28 57                jr z,cell_zombies

 752+ C564 22 E5 C8             ld (b_h),hl
 753+ C567 2A E1 C8             ld hl,(b_sin_)                  ; below the roof where it enters?
 754+ C56A CD 33 C8             call b_y_at
 755+ C56D ED 5B E5 C8          ld de,(b_h)
 756+ C571 B7                   or a
 757+ C572 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 758+ C574 FA B0 C5             jp m,.wall

 760+ C57A CD 33 C8             call b_y_at
 761+ C57D ED 5B E5 C8          ld de,(b_h)
 762+ C581 B7                   or a
 763+ C582 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 764+ C584 F0                   ret p                           ; (carry clear)
 765+ C585                      ; drops through the roof: s = (y0 - h) / -slope
 766+ C585 2A D0 C8             ld hl,(b_slope)
 767+ C588 CD F2 B5             call neg_hl
 768+ C58B EB                   ex de,hl
 769+ C58C 2A C8 C8             ld hl,(b_y0)
 770+ C58F ED 4B E5 C8          ld bc,(b_h)
 771+ C593 B7                   or a
 772+ C594 ED 42                sbc hl,bc
 773+ C596 7C                   ld a,h
 774+ C597 65                   ld h,l
 775+ C598 2E 00                ld l,0

 777+ C59D ED 5B E1 C8          ld de,(b_sin_)                  ; keep it inside this cell

 780+ C5A8 CD 72 C8             call min_u
 781+ C5AB 22 E7 C8             ld (b_shit),hl
 782+ C5AE 37                   scf
 783+ C5AF C9                   ret
 784+ C5B0 2A E1 C8     .wall:  ld hl,(b_sin_)
 785+ C5B3 22 E7 C8             ld (b_shit),hl
 786+ C5B6 37                   scf
 787+ C5B7 C9                   ret
 788+ C5B8
 789+ C5B8              ; Street cell, HL = map index: the nearest zombie standing in it that the
 790+ C5B8              ; path passes through (box ZHIT_R either side, ZHIT_H tall).
 791+ C5B8              cell_zombies:

 844+ C632 ED 5B CE C8          ld de,(b_cos)

 894+ C6A0
 895+ C6A0              ; Zombie IY (index A) takes C damage; when its hit points run out it is
 896+ C6A0              ; killed: map blip off, attack flag cleared, made inactive, particles.
 897+ C6A0              ; Preserves IX, IY.
 898+ C6A0              zdamage:
 899+ C6A0 47                   ld b,a
 900+ C6A1 79                   ld a,c
 901+ C6A2 B7                   or a
 902+ C6A3 C8                   ret z                           ; (a rocket's direct hit: the blast)
 903+ C6A4 FD 7E 12             ld a,(iy+18)

 906+ C6AA 38 04                jr c,.kill

 922+ C6CF
 923+ C6CF              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 924+ C6CF              ; Rocket IX has stopped (at B_X, B_Y, B_Z): BLAST_DMG to every zombie, human
 925+ C6CF              ; and base tile within BLAST_R of it on each axis.  Plain integer box tests:
 926+ C6CF              ; anything more than BLAST_R away on any one axis is skipped.
 927+ C6CF              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
