# file opened: menu.asm
   1+ C000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ C000              ; Z-POLICE - main menu (8K page PAGE_MENU, runs at $C000 in slot 6)
   3+ C000              ;
   4+ C000              ; Shown after start-up.  Layer 2 is hidden and the menu is drawn on the ULA
   5+ C000              ; screen with opaque paper.  Every frame it advances the random number
   6+ C000              ; generator (zrand), so the game doesn't start from the same seed each time
   7+ C000              ; (the wait for a key press varies), then reads the keys:
   8+ C000              ;   1  New game   -> newGame (zpolice.asm): level 1
   9+ C000              ;   2  Save game  (not yet)
  10+ C000              ;   3  Load game  (not yet)
  11+ C000              ;   9  Redefine keys (not yet)
  12+ C000              ; game_over_screen (the landing pad destroyed) shows GAME OVER for 10
  13+ C000              ; seconds, then returns here.
  14+ C000              ;==============================================================================
  15+ C000
  16+ C000              MENU_ATTR       equ P_PANEL<<3 | 7          ; white on the panel colour
  17+ C000              MENU_TITLE      equ P_PANEL<<3 | C_YEL      ; yellow
  18+ C000
  19+ C000              main_menu:
  20+ C000 ED 91 57 0C          nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_KEYS      ; the keys at $E000 (this page: $C000)
  21+ C004 CD 6F C0             call menu_clear
  22+ C007 21 09 C1             ld hl,menu_text                 ; title and options
  23+ C00A 7E           .txt:   ld a,(hl)
  24+ C00B FE FF                cp $FF
  25+ C00D 28 0B                jr z,.loop
  26+ C00F 57                   ld d,a                          ; row
  27+ C010 23                   inc hl
  28+ C011 5E                   ld e,(hl)                       ; column
  29+ C012 23                   inc hl
  30+ C013 4E                   ld c,(hl)                       ; attribute
  31+ C014 23                   inc hl
  32+ C015 CD 90 C0             call menu_print                 ; HL -> the text, past it after
  33+ C018 18 F0                jr .txt
  34+ C01A CD DA C0     .loop:  call menu_frame                 ; one video frame
  35+ C01D CD F6 39             call zrand                      ; stir the random number generator
  36+ C020 CD 00 E0             call kbd_scan
  37+ C023 21 01 03             ld hl,KB_1
  38+ C026 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  39+ C029 C2 A4 B2             jp nz,newGame
  40+ C02C 21 02 03             ld hl,KB_2                      ; save, load, redefine: to come
  41+ C02F CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  42+ C032 21 04 03             ld hl,KB_3
  43+ C035 CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  44+ C038 21 02 04             ld hl,KB_9
  45+ C03B CD 6E E0             call key_code_pressed
  46+ C03E 18 DA                jr .loop
  47+ C040
  48+ C040              ; The landing pad has been destroyed: GAME OVER for GAME_OVER_FR frames
  49+ C040              ; (10 seconds), then the main menu
  50+ C040              game_over_screen:
  51+ C040 31 DD BB             ld sp,stack_top
  52+ C043 CD 6F C0             call menu_clear
  53+ C046 16 0B                ld d,11
  54+ C048 1E 0B                ld e,11
  55+ C04A 0E 0A                ld c,MENU_TITLE
  56+ C04C 21 65 C0             ld hl,txt_game_over
  57+ C04F CD 90 C0             call menu_print
  58+ C052 01 F4 01             ld bc,GAME_OVER_FR
  59+ C055 C5           .w:     push bc
  60+ C056 CD DA C0             call menu_frame
  61+ C059 CD F6 39             call zrand                      ; (keep stirring the seed)
  62+ C05C C1                   pop bc
  63+ C05D 0B                   dec bc
  64+ C05E 78                   ld a,b
  65+ C05F B1                   or c
  66+ C060 20 F3                jr nz,.w
  67+ C062 C3 00 C0             jp main_menu
  68+ C065
  69+ C065              txt_game_over:
  70+ C065 47 41 4D 45          db "GAME OVER", 0
  70+ C069 20 4F 56 45
  70+ C06D 52 00
  71+ C06F
  72+ C06F              ; Hide Layer 2, and blank the ULA screen with opaque paper
  73+ C06F              menu_clear:
  74+ C06F 01 3B 12             ld bc,$123B
  75+ C072 AF                   xor a
  76+ C073 ED 79                out (c),a
  77+ C075 21 00 80             ld hl,ULA_BMP
  78+ C078 11 01 80             ld de,ULA_BMP+1
  79+ C07B 01 FF 17             ld bc,$1800-1
  80+ C07E 36 00                ld (hl),0
  81+ C080 ED B0                ldir
  82+ C082 21 00 98             ld hl,ULA_ATTR
  83+ C085 11 01 98             ld de,ULA_ATTR+1
  84+ C088 01 FF 02             ld bc,$300-1
  85+ C08B 36 0F                ld (hl),MENU_ATTR
  86+ C08D ED B0                ldir
  87+ C08F C9                   ret
  88+ C090
  89+ C090              ; D = character row, E = column, C = attribute, HL -> text ending 0.
  90+ C090              ; Prints it; HL ends past the 0.
  91+ C090              menu_print:
  92+ C090 7E           .ch:    ld a,(hl)
  93+ C091 23                   inc hl
  94+ C092 B7                   or a
  95+ C093 C8                   ret z
  96+ C094 E5                   push hl
  97+ C095 D5                   push de

  99+ C097 CD A0 C0             call menu_char

 103+ C09D 1C                   inc e

 106+ C0A0              ; A = ASCII 32-95, D = row, E = column, C = attribute: draw one character
 107+ C0A0              menu_char:

 109+ C0A2 6F                   ld l,a
 110+ C0A3 26 00                ld h,0
 111+ C0A5 29                   add hl,hl

 116+ C0AC 19                   add hl,de                       ; HL -> its 8 bytes
 117+ C0AD D1                   pop de

 120+ C0B0 0F                   rrca
 121+ C0B1 0F                   rrca
 122+ C0B2 0F                   rrca
 123+ C0B3 47                   ld b,a
 124+ C0B4 E6 E0                and %11100000
 125+ C0B6 B3                   or e
 126+ C0B7 E5                   push hl
 127+ C0B8 6F                   ld l,a
 128+ C0B9 78                   ld a,b
 129+ C0BA E6 03                and %00000011
 130+ C0BC C6 98                add a,high ULA_ATTR

 133+ C0C0 E1                   pop hl

 136+ C0C3 E6 18                and %00011000

 146+ C0D0 50                   ld d,b                          ; DE -> the cell's top line
 147+ C0D1 06 08                ld b,8

 149+ C0D4 12                   ld (de),a

 152+ C0D7 10 FA                djnz .l
 153+ C0D9 C9                   ret
 154+ C0DA
 155+ C0DA              ; Wait for the start of the next video frame (the raster reaching line 192)
 156+ C0DA              menu_frame:
 157+ C0DA CD E5 C0     .in:    call menu_line                  ; let any current blanking end
 158+ C0DD 30 FB                jr nc,.in
 159+ C0DF CD E5 C0     .out:   call menu_line                  ; then wait for the next one
 160+ C0E2 38 FB                jr c,.out
 161+ C0E4 C9                   ret
 162+ C0E5
 163+ C0E5              ; Carry clear if the raster is at line 192 or later (blanking).  The line is
 164+ C0E5              ; read as high byte ($1E), low byte ($1F), high byte again: if the high
 165+ C0E5              ; byte changed in between (line 255 -> 256) the pair is torn, so read again.
 166+ C0E5              menu_line:
 167+ C0E5 01 3B 24             ld bc,$243B
 168+ C0E8 3E 1E                ld a,$1E
 169+ C0EA ED 79                out (c),a
 170+ C0EC 04                   inc b
 171+ C0ED ED 58                in e,(c)                        ; high byte
 172+ C0EF 05                   dec b
 173+ C0F0 3E 1F                ld a,$1F
 174+ C0F2 ED 79                out (c),a
 175+ C0F4 04                   inc b
 176+ C0F5 ED 50                in d,(c)                        ; low byte
 177+ C0F7 05                   dec b
 178+ C0F8 3E 1E                ld a,$1E
 179+ C0FA ED 79                out (c),a
 180+ C0FC 04                   inc b
 181+ C0FD ED 78                in a,(c)
 182+ C0FF BB                   cp e
 183+ C100 20 E3                jr nz,menu_line                 ; torn: again
 184+ C102 E6 01                and 1
 185+ C104 C0                   ret nz                          ; line >= 256 (carry clear)
 186+ C105 7A                   ld a,d
 187+ C106 FE C0                cp 192
 188+ C108 C9                   ret

1128  2000                      ORG ZOMBIE_ADDR
1129  2000                      INCLUDE "zombie.asm"
# file opened: zombie.asm
   1+ 2000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ 2000              ; Z-POLICE - zombie page (8K page PAGE_ZOMBIE, mapped at $2000 in MMU slot 1)
   3+ 2000              ;
   4+ 2000              ; Placeholder zombies: Layer 2 stick-man silhouettes walking the streets.
   5+ 2000              ; The page stays mapped at $2000 the whole time (slot 0 is used for paging
   6+ 2000              ; Layer 2 sections, slot 7 for the other code pages), so the city page can
   7+ 2000              ; call in here while it draws.
   8+ 2000              ;
   9+ 2000              ; Each zombie is a ZREC-byte record:
  10+ 2000              ;   +0,+1  x  8.8 fixed point in cell units (high byte = cell, low = fraction;
  11+ 2000              ;             the same as world units, one cell = 256)
  12+ 2000              ;   +2,+3  z  likewise
  13+ 2000              ;   +4     heading 0 = +z, 1 = +x, 2 = -z, 3 = -x  (yaw angle = heading*64;
  14+ 2000              ;             heading+1 turns right, heading+3 turns left)
  15+ 2000              ;   +5     walk animation phase 0..3
  16+ 2000              ;   +6     step accumulator: fraction of a world unit (4 bits)
  17+ 2000              ;   +7     wait: frames left standing still (0 = walking)
  18+ 2000              ;   +8,+9  cell (x, z) of the last turn decision ($FF = none)
  19+ 2000              ;   +10    active: 1 = in play, 0 = gone (not moved, linked or drawn)
  20+ 2000              ;   +11    frames until this zombie's next decision check
  21+ 2000              ;   +12,+13  cell (x, z) of its blip on the street map
  22+ 2000              ;   +14    1 = blip plotted
  23+ 2000              ;   +15    type: 0 = zombie, 1 = fast zombie (walks at twice the speed)
  24+ 2000              ;   +16    mode: 0 = walking, 1 = going for a base tile, 2 = attacking it
  25+ 2000              ;   +17    frames to its next hit on the tile
  26+ 2000              ;   +18    hit points left (ZHP, ZHP_FAST; weapons.asm zdamage)
  27+ 2000              ; zatk_flags (one byte per zombie, by index): TRUE while it attacks a base
  28+ 2000              ; tile, when its map dot is red; FALSE again once the tile is destroyed.
  29+ 2000              ;
  30+ 2000              ; The base: at junctions a zombie leans towards the road that points at the
  31+ 2000              ; landing pad (zbias_t, from the level).  A base tile (terrace or pad) in its
  32+ 2000              ; way isn't avoided: it walks up to ATTACK_R from it and hits it every
  33+ 2000              ; ZHIT_FRAMES frames.  A destroyed terrace tile becomes road and the zombie
  34+ 2000              ; walks on; the pad destroyed sets game_over.
  35+ 2000              ;
  36+ 2000              ; Street map blips: zblip / zblip_off (map.asm) are called on the zombie's
  37+ 2000              ; decision-check frames.  Call zblip_off for a zombie before making it gone.
  38+ 2000              ;
  39+ 2000              ; Decisions are staggered: each active zombie only checks for junctions and
  40+ 2000              ; obstacles once every zsteps frames, with the zombies' check frames spread
  41+ 2000              ; out so only about NZOMB/zsteps of them check on any one frame.  In between
  42+ 2000              ; they just walk, so the look-ahead (zlook) covers zsteps frames of walking
  43+ 2000              ; plus a margin: ZMARGIN + zsteps*zspeed/16.
  44+ 2000              ;
  45+ 2000              ; Movement (zmove), every frame:
  46+ 2000              ;   * Standing still: count the wait down and do nothing else.
  47+ 2000              ;   * Not this zombie's check frame: step forwards (see the last point).
  48+ 2000              ;   * Junctions: once per cell, when the zombie is 0.25-0.75 of the way
  49+ 2000              ;     through the cell along its heading, look for a road (street cell) to
  50+ 2000              ;     the left and to the right.  Left is tested first: if there is a road,
  51+ 2000              ;     turn left at random (1 in 2); if not taken, the same for the right.
  52+ 2000              ;     The cell is then marked as decided, so it isn't tested again until the
  53+ 2000              ;     zombie has been somewhere else.
  54+ 2000              ;   * Blocked (building or map edge within zlook ahead): turn left if there
  55+ 2000              ;     is a road there, else right; otherwise turn round and stand still for
  56+ 2000              ;     ZWAIT frames before testing anything again.
  57+ 2000              ;   * Otherwise step forwards zspeed/16 world units (the remainder is kept,
  58+ 2000              ;     so slow speeds still move smoothly on average).
  59+ 2000              ;
  60+ 2000              ; Zombies are only ever on street (empty) cells.  They are drawn by the city
  61+ 2000              ; traversal when it reaches their cell (draw_cell jumps to zombie_cell for
  62+ 2000              ; empty cells), so the painter's algorithm hides them behind nearer buildings.
  63+ 2000              ; A 32x32 table (zcell) holds the first zombie in each cell, znext chains the
  64+ 2000              ; rest; it is rebuilt every frame by zombie_update.
  65+ 2000              ;
  66+ 2000              ; Sprites (tables_zombie.inc, generated): 8 scales from 1x4 to 8x16 pixels,
  67+ 2000              ; forward and left facings, 4 walk frames.  Rear and right are the mirrored
  68+ 2000              ; forward and left.  One byte per row, bit 7 = leftmost pixel.
  69+ 2000              ;==============================================================================
  70+ 2000
  71+ 2000              NZOMB           equ 20          ; number of zombies
  72+ 2000              ZREC            equ 19          ; bytes per zombie record
  73+ 2000              ZT_FAST         equ 1           ; +15: fast zombie
  74+ 2000              ZHP             equ 5           ; hit points: a zombie (level 1)
  75+ 2000              ZHP_FAST        equ 4           ; and a fast zombie
  76+ 2000              ZSPEED_INIT     equ 10          ; starting zspeed: 0.63 units (4.9 cm) per
  77+ 2000                                              ; frame, ~0.7 m/s at 14 fps
  78+ 2000              ZSTEPS_INIT     equ 10          ; starting zsteps: decide every 10 frames
  79+ 2000              ZMARGIN         equ 16          ; keep at least this far from walls: 1.25 m
  80+ 2000              ZWAIT           equ 100         ; frames to stand still when stuck
  81+ 2000              ZOMBIE_H        equ 26          ; height: 2 m (the same as the hit box, ZHIT_H)
  82+ 2000
  83+ 2000                      ALIGN 256
  84+ 2000 00 00 00...  zcell:      ds GRID*GRID                ; first zombie in each cell (index+1, 0 none)
  85+ 3000
  86+ 3000                      INCLUDE "tables_zombie.inc"     ; sprites, sizes, bitrev

  91+ 3500              zombie_init:

  93+ 3503 11 01 20             ld de,zcell+1                   ; clear; a new game reuses them)
  94+ 3506 01 FF 0F             ld bc,GRID*GRID-1
  95+ 3509 36 00                ld (hl),0

  97+ 350D                      ; (zseed: seeded at power-on, stirred by the menu every frame)
  98+ 350D 3A 51 B6             ld a,(level)                    ; junction bias towards the pad:
  99+ 3510 87                   add a,a                         ; min(ZBIAS_MAX, ZBIAS_BASE +

 187+ 35D2              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 188+ 35D2              ; Per frame: walk every zombie and rebuild the cell lists.  Call after
 189+ 35D2              ; setup_frame (uses cos_p/sin_p for the zombie height vector).
 190+ 35D2              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 191+ 35D2              zombie_update:
 192+ 35D2 21 DE 3D             ld hl,zanim

 237+ 3637              zlink:

 255+ 365A C9                   ret

 257+ 365B              ; Move zombie IX for one frame (see the header for the rules)
 258+ 365B              zmove:

 267+ 366C CD FA 36     .go:    call zcalc_step

 283+ 369A              .blocked:

 315+ 36E2 28 05                jr z,.rt

 317+ 36E6 C3 75 39             jp zturn
 318+ 36E9 3C           .rt:    inc a
 319+ 36EA C3 75 39             jp zturn
 320+ 36ED DD 7E 04     .dead:  ld a,(ix+4)                     ; dead end: turn round and wait
 321+ 36F0 EE 02                xor 2
 322+ 36F2 DD 77 04             ld (ix+4),a
 323+ 36F5 DD 36 07 64          ld (ix+7),ZWAIT
 324+ 36F9 C9                   ret
 325+ 36FA
 326+ 36FA              ; This frame's distance for zombie IX (zspeed, twice for a fast zombie),
 327+ 36FA              ; 4.4 fixed with the fraction kept -> zstep.  Keeps B.
 328+ 36FA              zcalc_step:
 329+ 36FA 3A 00 3C             ld a,(zspeed)                   ; distance this frame, 4.4 fixed
 330+ 36FD DD CB 0F 46          bit 0,(ix+15)
 331+ 3701 28 01                jr z,.reg
 332+ 3703 87                   add a,a                         ; fast zombie: twice the speed
 333+ 3704 DD 86 06     .reg:   add a,(ix+6)
 334+ 3707 4F                   ld c,a
 335+ 3708 E6 0F                and 15
 336+ 370A DD 77 06             ld (ix+6),a                     ; keep the fraction
 337+ 370D 79                   ld a,c
 338+ 370E 0F                   rrca
 339+ 370F 0F                   rrca
 340+ 3710 0F                   rrca
 341+ 3711 0F                   rrca
 342+ 3712 E6 0F                and 15
 343+ 3714 32 02 3C             ld (zstep),a
 344+ 3717 C9                   ret
 345+ 3718
 346+ 3718              ; Move zombie IX A units along its heading
 347+ 3718              zforward:
 348+ 3718 6F                   ld l,a
 349+ 3719 26 00                ld h,0
 350+ 371B DD 5E 04             ld e,(ix+4)
 351+ 371E CB 4B                bit 1,e
 352+ 3720 C4 F2 B5             call nz,neg_hl
 353+ 3723 CB 43                bit 0,e
 354+ 3725 20 0E                jr nz,.sx
 355+ 3727 DD 4E 02             ld c,(ix+2)
 356+ 372A DD 46 03             ld b,(ix+3)
 357+ 372D 09                   add hl,bc
 358+ 372E DD 75 02             ld (ix+2),l
 359+ 3731 DD 74 03             ld (ix+3),h
 360+ 3734 C9                   ret
 361+ 3735 DD 4E 00     .sx:    ld c,(ix+0)
 362+ 3738 DD 46 01             ld b,(ix+1)
 363+ 373B 09                   add hl,bc
 364+ 373C DD 75 00             ld (ix+0),l
 365+ 373F DD 74 01             ld (ix+1),h
 366+ 3742 C9                   ret
 367+ 3743
 368+ 3743              ; Zombie IX going for (mode 1) or attacking (mode 2) the base tile ahead
 369+ 3743              zattack:
 370+ 3743 DD 7E 04             ld a,(ix+4)
 371+ 3746 CD 87 39             call zside                      ; still a base tile ahead?
 372+ 3749 CD 9E 37             call zbase
 373+ 374C 20 35                jr nz,.gone
 374+ 374E CD AB 37             call zedge                      ; distance to its edge
 375+ 3751 D6 1A                sub ATTACK_R
 376+ 3753 28 10                jr z,.at
 377+ 3755 38 0E                jr c,.at
 378+ 3757 47                   ld b,a                          ; still to go: walk on, no further
 379+ 3758 CD FA 36             call zcalc_step
 380+ 375B 3A 02 3C             ld a,(zstep)
 381+ 375E B8                   cp b
 382+ 375F 38 01                jr c,.mv
 383+ 3761 78                   ld a,b
 384+ 3762 C3 18 37     .mv:    jp zforward
 385+ 3765 DD 7E 10     .at:    ld a,(ix+16)
 386+ 3768 FE 02                cp 2
 387+ 376A 28 0C                jr z,.hit
 388+ 376C DD 36 10 02          ld (ix+16),2                    ; in reach: attack
 389+ 3770 CD 95 37             call zflag                      ; attack flag on, map dot red
 390+ 3773 36 01                ld (hl),TRUE
 391+ 3775 CD 52 78             call zblip_red                  ; (map.asm)
 392+ 3778 DD 35 11     .hit:   dec (ix+17)
 393+ 377B C0                   ret nz
 394+ 377C DD 36 11 05          ld (ix+17),ZHIT_FRAMES
 395+ 3780 C3 C4 37             jp base_hit
 396+ 3783 DD 7E 10     .gone:  ld a,(ix+16)
 397+ 3786 DD 36 10 00          ld (ix+16),0                    ; destroyed: walk on (towards the pad)
 398+ 378A FE 02                cp 2
 399+ 378C C0                   ret nz                          ; (was only on its way)
 400+ 378D CD 95 37             call zflag                      ; attack flag off, map dot back to
 401+ 3790 36 00                ld (hl),FALSE                   ; its usual shade
 402+ 3792 C3 F8 77             jp zblip
 403+ 3795
 404+ 3795              ; HL -> zatk_flags entry of the zombie being moved (zcur_i)
 405+ 3795              zflag:
 406+ 3795 3A 06 3C             ld a,(zcur_i)
 407+ 3798 21 07 3C             ld hl,zatk_flags
 408+ 379B ED 31                add hl,a
 409+ 379D C9                   ret
 410+ 379E
 411+ 379E              ; A = map code: Z if it is a base tile (the pad or the terrace)
 412+ 379E              zbase:
 413+ 379E FE 10                cp PAD_CODE
 414+ 37A0 38 06                jr c,.no
 415+ 37A2 FE 12                cp TERRACE_CODE+1
 416+ 37A4 30 02                jr nc,.no

 418+ 37A7 C9                   ret
 419+ 37A8 F6 01        .no:    or 1                            ; NZ
 420+ 37AA C9                   ret
 421+ 37AB
 422+ 37AB              ; A = distance from zombie IX to the edge of the next cell along its
 423+ 37AB              ; heading (0..255)
 424+ 37AB              zedge:
 425+ 37AB DD 7E 04             ld a,(ix+4)
 426+ 37AE 1F                   rra                             ; carry: along x
 427+ 37AF DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)
 428+ 37B2 38 03                jr c,.ax

 430+ 37B7 4F           .ax:    ld c,a
 431+ 37B8 DD 7E 04             ld a,(ix+4)
 432+ 37BB CB 4F                bit 1,a
 433+ 37BD 79                   ld a,c
 434+ 37BE C0                   ret nz                          ; going down: the low byte
 435+ 37BF ED 44                neg                             ; going up: 256 - the low byte
 436+ 37C1 C0                   ret nz
 437+ 37C2 3D                   dec a                           ; (a whole cell: 255)
 438+ 37C3 C9                   ret
 439+ 37C4
 440+ 37C4              ; Zombie IX hits the base tile ahead of it: 1 damage
 441+ 37C4              base_hit:
 442+ 37C4 DD 7E 04             ld a,(ix+4)

 454+ 37DA                      ; fall through
 455+ 37DA
 456+ 37DA              ; Base tile (E, D) = (x, z) takes C damage (if it is one, and not already
 457+ 37DA              ; gone).  Destroyed, a terrace tile becomes road and the pad sets game_over.
 458+ 37DA              base_damage:

 461+ 37DD FE 05                cp 2*TERRACE_R+1

 465+ 37E2 87                   add a,a

 467+ 37E4 47                   ld b,a
 468+ 37E5 7B                   ld a,e
 469+ 37E6 D6 1E                sub PAD_X-TERRACE_R

 471+ 37EA D0                   ret nc
 472+ 37EB 80                   add a,b
 473+ 37EC 21 2F 3C             ld hl,base_hp
 474+ 37EF ED 31                add hl,a
 475+ 37F1 7E                   ld a,(hl)

 477+ 37F3 C8                   ret z                           ; (already gone)

 482+ 37FA C9                   ret

 484+ 37FD 6A                   ld l,d                          ; destroyed

 486+ 3800                      ROWOFS
 486+ 3800 29          >        add hl,hl
 486+ 3801 29          >        add hl,hl

 486+ 3804 29          >        add hl,hl
 486+ 3805 29          >        add hl,hl

 505+ 3825 C3 30 78             jp map_pix                      ; and on the map (map.asm)
 506+ 3828
 507+ 3828              ; New game: the pad and terrace whole, at full strength
 508+ 3828              base_init:

 529+ 384E 29          >        add hl,hl
 529+ 384F 29          >        add hl,hl
 529+ 3850 29          >        add hl,hl
 529+ 3851 29          >        add hl,hl

 552+ 3870 20 BB                jr nz,.r
 553+ 3872 C9                   ret
 554+ 3873
 555+ 3873              ; A = heading (mod 4): if the cell next to zombie IX that way is a street,
 556+ 3873              ; or a base tile (so the road towards the pad can lead into the base and an
 557+ 3873              ; attack), set the heading's bit in C.  Keeps C otherwise.
 558+ 3873              zroad:
 559+ 3873 E6 03                and 3
 560+ 3875 F5                   push af
 561+ 3876 C5                   push bc
 562+ 3877 CD 87 39             call zside
 563+ 387A C4 9E 37             call nz,zbase                   ; not a street: a base tile?
 564+ 387D C1                   pop bc
 565+ 387E 20 0C                jr nz,.no
 566+ 3880 F1                   pop af
 567+ 3881 E5                   push hl
 568+ 3882 21 23 3C             ld hl,zbits

 572+ 3889 4F                   ld c,a
 573+ 388A E1                   pop hl
 574+ 388B C9                   ret
 575+ 388C F1           .no:    pop af
 576+ 388D C9                   ret
 577+ 388E
 578+ 388E              ; A = heading, C = heading bits: NZ if that heading's bit is set
 579+ 388E              zhas:

 585+ 3896 E1                   pop hl
 586+ 3897 C9                   ret
 587+ 3898
 588+ 3898              ; C = candidate heading bits -> A = the heading pointing most towards the
 589+ 3898              ; landing pad (its dot product with the way to the pad is largest)
 590+ 3898              zbest:
 591+ 3898 79                   ld a,c
 592+ 3899 32 1B 3C             ld (zb_mask),a

 597+ 38A6 ED 52                sbc hl,de

 601+ 38B1 DD 56 03             ld d,(ix+3)

 605+ 38BA 3E FF                ld a,$FF

 608+ 38C1 3A 1B 3C     .lp:    ld a,(zb_mask)
 609+ 38C4 CB 3F                srl a
 610+ 38C6 32 1B 3C             ld (zb_mask),a

 616+ 38D5 CB 48        .z:     bit 1,b

 624+ 38E6 ED 52                sbc hl,de

 632+ 38F6 78                   ld a,b
 633+ 38F7 FE 04                cp 4
 634+ 38F9 20 C6                jr nz,.lp
 635+ 38FB 3A 1C 3C             ld a,(zb_h)
 636+ 38FE C9                   ret

 638+ 38FF              ; Junction decision, once per cell, 0.25-0.75 of the way through it
 639+ 38FF              zjunction:
 640+ 38FF DD 7E 01             ld a,(ix+1)
 641+ 3902 DD BE 08             cp (ix+8)

 645+ 390D C8                   ret z                           ; already decided in this cell
 646+ 390E DD 7E 04     .new:   ld a,(ix+4)
 647+ 3911 1F                   rra                             ; carry = moving along x
 648+ 3912 DD 7E 00             ld a,(ix+0)

 652+ 391C FE 80                cp 128

 666+ 393A CD F6 39             call zrand

 803+ 3A0B              ; Draws the zombies standing in that cell.  Needs the city page at $E000.

 805+ 3A0B              zombie_cell:
 806+ 3A0B 11 AB 7F             ld de,zcell-city_map
 807+ 3A0E 19                   add hl,de
 808+ 3A0F 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 809+ 3A10 B7                   or a
 810+ 3A11 C8                   ret z
 811+ 3A12 DD E5                push ix
 812+ 3A14 3D           .lp:    dec a
 813+ 3A15 32 DF 3D             ld (zcur),a
 814+ 3A18 5F                   ld e,a
 815+ 3A19 16 13                ld d,ZREC
 816+ 3A1B ED 30                mul d,e
 817+ 3A1D DD 21 4C 3C          ld ix,zombies
 818+ 3A21 DD 19                add ix,de
 819+ 3A23 CD 35 3A             call zombie_draw
 820+ 3A26 3A DF 3D             ld a,(zcur)
 821+ 3A29 21 C8 3D             ld hl,znext
 822+ 3A2C ED 31                add hl,a
 823+ 3A2E 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 824+ 3A2F B7                   or a
 825+ 3A30 20 E2                jr nz,.lp
 826+ 3A32 DD E1                pop ix
 827+ 3A34 C9                   ret
 828+ 3A35
 829+ 3A35              ; Project and draw zombie IX
 830+ 3A35              zombie_draw:
 831+ 3A35 DD CB 0A 46          bit 0,(ix+10)
 832+ 3A39 C8                   ret z                           ; killed this frame
 833+ 3A3A 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 834+ 3A3D 22 F6 3D             ld (fig_y),hl                   ; on the ground
 835+ 3A40 21 14 3B             ld hl,zfig_sel
 836+ 3A43 22 F8 3D             ld (fig_sel),hl
 837+ 3A46 3E 1B                ld a,ZOMBIE_COL
 838+ 3A48 32 BC 3B             ld (zspr_rows.col+1),a
 839+ 3A4B                      ; fall through
 840+ 3A4B
 841+ 3A4B              ; Project and draw a figure whose x, z are at (IX+0..3) like a zombie's:
 842+ 3A4B              ; feet at height fig_y, sprite frame chosen by the routine at fig_sel (it
 843+ 3A4B              ; sets zrow and zface, then jumps to zspr_rows), colour patched into
 844+ 3A4B              ; zspr_rows.  Used for zombies and for humans (human.asm).
 845+ 3A4B              fig_draw:
 846+ 3A4B DD 6E 00             ld l,(ix+0)
 847+ 3A4E DD 66 01             ld h,(ix+1)
 848+ 3A51 ED 5B 38 B6          ld de,(cam_x+1)
 849+ 3A55 B7                   or a
 850+ 3A56 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 851+ 3A58 22 2F FB             ld (t_dx),hl
 852+ 3A5B DD 6E 02             ld l,(ix+2)
 853+ 3A5E DD 66 03             ld h,(ix+3)
 854+ 3A61 ED 5B 3B B6          ld de,(cam_z+1)
 855+ 3A65 B7                   or a
 856+ 3A66 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 857+ 3A68 22 31 FB             ld (t_dz),hl
 858+ 3A6B CD E8 F3             call rot_yaw
 859+ 3A6E 22 E4 3D             ld (zt+0),hl
 860+ 3A71 CD 1C F4             call rot_pitch
 861+ 3A74 22 E6 3D             ld (zt+2),hl                    ; foot point (X, Y, Z)
 862+ 3A77 ED 53 E8 3D          ld (zt+4),de
 863+ 3A7B 2A F6 3D             ld hl,(fig_y)                   ; raised: + y * (cosP, sinP)
 864+ 3A7E 7C                   ld a,h
 865+ 3A7F B5                   or l
 866+ 3A80 28 20                jr z,.gnd
 867+ 3A82 E5                   push hl
 868+ 3A83 ED 5B B7 BA          ld de,(cos_p)
 869+ 3A87 CD FE B5             call fmul
 870+ 3A8A ED 5B E6 3D          ld de,(zt+2)
 871+ 3A8E 19                   add hl,de
 872+ 3A8F 22 E6 3D             ld (zt+2),hl
 873+ 3A92 E1                   pop hl
 874+ 3A93 ED 5B B5 BA          ld de,(sin_p)
 875+ 3A97 CD FE B5             call fmul
 876+ 3A9A ED 5B E8 3D          ld de,(zt+4)
 877+ 3A9E 19                   add hl,de
 878+ 3A9F 22 E8 3D             ld (zt+4),hl
 879+ 3AA2 21 E4 3D     .gnd:   ld hl,zt
 880+ 3AA5 CD 95 E8             call calc_oc
 881+ 3AA8 B7                   or a
 882+ 3AA9 28 48                jr z,.foot
 883+ 3AAB FE 10                cp 16
 884+ 3AAD C0                   ret nz                          ; only feet "below the bottom" allowed:
 885+ 3AAE 2A E6 3D             ld hl,(zt+2)                    ; project the head for the column
 886+ 3AB1 ED 5B E0 3D          ld de,(zh_y)
 887+ 3AB5 19                   add hl,de
 888+ 3AB6 22 E6 3D             ld (zt+2),hl
 889+ 3AB9 2A E8 3D             ld hl,(zt+4)
 890+ 3ABC ED 5B E2 3D          ld de,(zh_z)
 891+ 3AC0 19                   add hl,de
 892+ 3AC1 22 E8 3D             ld (zt+4),hl
 893+ 3AC4 21 E4 3D             ld hl,zt
 894+ 3AC7 CD 95 E8             call calc_oc
 895+ 3ACA B7                   or a
 896+ 3ACB C0                   ret nz
 897+ 3ACC 21 E4 3D             ld hl,zt
 898+ 3ACF CD B0 E7             call project
 899+ 3AD2 32 EA 3D             ld (zsx),a
 900+ 3AD5 79                   ld a,c
 901+ 3AD6 32 EB 3D             ld (zy),a                       ; head row
 902+ 3AD9 CD CC 3B             call zsize
 903+ 3ADC D8                   ret c
 904+ 3ADD                      ; Keep the feet anchor: the sprite is at most 16 pixels, so it stands
 905+ 3ADD                      ; on the (off-screen) feet, top = head + (true height - sprite height),
 906+ 3ADD                      ; exactly where the foot-anchored path would put it.
 907+ 3ADD 3A F3 3D             ld a,(zh)
 908+ 3AE0 4F                   ld c,a
 909+ 3AE1 3A F4 3D             ld a,(zhr)
 910+ 3AE4 91                   sub c
 911+ 3AE5 4F                   ld c,a
 912+ 3AE6 3A EB 3D             ld a,(zy)
 913+ 3AE9 81                   add a,c
 914+ 3AEA D8                   ret c
 915+ 3AEB FE 80                cp VIEW_H
 916+ 3AED D0                   ret nc                          ; whole sprite below the viewport
 917+ 3AEE 32 EB 3D             ld (zy),a
 918+ 3AF1 18 1D                jr zspr
 919+ 3AF3 21 E4 3D     .foot:  ld hl,zt
 920+ 3AF6 CD B0 E7             call project
 921+ 3AF9 32 EA 3D             ld (zsx),a
 922+ 3AFC 79                   ld a,c
 923+ 3AFD 32 EB 3D             ld (zy),a
 924+ 3B00 CD CC 3B             call zsize
 925+ 3B03 D8                   ret c
 926+ 3B04 3A F3 3D             ld a,(zh)
 927+ 3B07 4F                   ld c,a
 928+ 3B08 3A EB 3D             ld a,(zy)
 929+ 3B0B 91                   sub c
 930+ 3B0C 3C                   inc a
 931+ 3B0D 32 EB 3D             ld (zy),a                       ; top row = foot - h + 1
 932+ 3B10                      ; fall through
 933+ 3B10
 934+ 3B10              ; Draw the sprite: zsx = centre column, zy = top row (rows outside the
 935+ 3B10              ; viewport are skipped, so it can be negative or past the bottom)
 936+ 3B10              zspr:
 937+ 3B10 2A F8 3D             ld hl,(fig_sel)                 ; choose the frame
 938+ 3B13 E9                   jp (hl)
 939+ 3B14
 940+ 3B14              ; Zombie frame: walking, facing from its heading relative to the view
 941+ 3B14              zfig_sel:
 942+ 3B14                      ; facing relative to the view: ((heading*64 - yaw + 32) >> 6) & 3
 943+ 3B14 DD 7E 04             ld a,(ix+4)
 944+ 3B17 0F                   rrca
 945+ 3B18 0F                   rrca
 946+ 3B19 21 40 B6             ld hl,yaw
 947+ 3B1C 96                   sub (hl)
 948+ 3B1D C6 20                add a,32
 949+ 3B1F 07                   rlca
 950+ 3B20 07                   rlca
 951+ 3B21 E6 03                and 3
 952+ 3B23 21 FC 3B             ld hl,face_tab
 953+ 3B26 ED 31                add hl,a
 954+ 3B28 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 955+ 3B29 32 F5 3D             ld (zface),a
 956+ 3B2C                      ; sprite index (facing*4 + frame)*8 + level
 957+ 3B2C DD 7E 07             ld a,(ix+7)
 958+ 3B2F B7                   or a
 959+ 3B30 3E 00                ld a,0
 960+ 3B32 20 0B                jr nz,.still                    ; standing still: hold the frame
 961+ 3B34 3A DE 3D             ld a,(zanim)
 962+ 3B37 0F                   rrca                            ; frame advances every 2 frames for
 963+ 3B38 DD CB 0F 46          bit 0,(ix+15)                   ; a fast zombie (legs keep pace)
 964+ 3B3C 20 01                jr nz,.still
 965+ 3B3E 0F                   rrca                            ; every 4 for a zombie
 966+ 3B3F DD 86 05     .still: add a,(ix+5)
 967+ 3B42 E6 03                and 3
 968+ 3B44 4F                   ld c,a
 969+ 3B45 3A F5 3D             ld a,(zface)
 970+ 3B48 E6 01                and 1
 971+ 3B4A 87                   add a,a
 972+ 3B4B 87                   add a,a
 973+ 3B4C 81                   add a,c
 974+ 3B4D 87                   add a,a
 975+ 3B4E 87                   add a,a
 976+ 3B4F 87                   add a,a
 977+ 3B50 21 F1 3D             ld hl,zlvl
 978+ 3B53 86                   add a,(hl)
 979+ 3B54 87                   add a,a
 980+ 3B55 21 21 30             ld hl,spr_ptr
 981+ 3B58 ED 31                add hl,a
 982+ 3B5A 7E                   ld a,(hl)
 983+ 3B5B 23                   inc hl
 984+ 3B5C 66                   ld h,(hl)
 985+ 3B5D 6F                   ld l,a
 986+ 3B5E 22 EE 3D             ld (zrow),hl
 987+ 3B61                      ; fall through
 988+ 3B61
 989+ 3B61              ; Draw rows from zrow, mirrored if zface bit 1, at zsx / zy
 990+ 3B61              zspr_rows:
 991+ 3B61                      ; x0 = centre - w/2
 992+ 3B61 3A F2 3D             ld a,(zw)
 993+ 3B64 CB 3F                srl a
 994+ 3B66 5F                   ld e,a
 995+ 3B67 16 00                ld d,0
 996+ 3B69 3A EA 3D             ld a,(zsx)
 997+ 3B6C 6F                   ld l,a
 998+ 3B6D 26 00                ld h,0
 999+ 3B6F B7                   or a
1000+ 3B70 ED 52                sbc hl,de
1001+ 3B72 22 EC 3D             ld (zx0),hl
1002+ 3B75 3A F3 3D             ld a,(zh)
1003+ 3B78 32 F0 3D             ld (zcnt),a
1004+ 3B7B 2A EE 3D     .row:   ld hl,(zrow)
1005+ 3B7E 46                   ld b,(hl)                       ; row bits
1006+ 3B7F 23                   inc hl
1007+ 3B80 22 EE 3D             ld (zrow),hl
1008+ 3B83 3A EB 3D             ld a,(zy)
1009+ 3B86 FE 80                cp VIEW_H
1010+ 3B88 30 37                jr nc,.next                     ; above (negative) or below the viewport
1011+ 3B8A CD 20 E7             call seg_map                    ; A = y & 31 (preserves BC, DE, L)
1012+ 3B8D 67                   ld h,a
1013+ 3B8E 3A F5 3D             ld a,(zface)
1014+ 3B91 CB 4F                bit 1,a
1015+ 3B93 28 15                jr z,.nm
1016+ 3B95 68                   ld l,b                          ; mirror: reverse the bits, then
1017+ 3B96 7C                   ld a,h                          ; shift out the unused right columns
1018+ 3B97 26 34                ld h,high bitrev
1019+ 3B99 46                   ld b,(hl)
1020+ 3B9A 67                   ld h,a
1021+ 3B9B 3A F2 3D             ld a,(zw)
1022+ 3B9E D6 08                sub 8
1023+ 3BA0 28 08                jr z,.nm
1024+ 3BA2 ED 44                neg
1025+ 3BA4 4F                   ld c,a
1026+ 3BA5 CB 20        .sh:    sla b
1027+ 3BA7 0D                   dec c
1028+ 3BA8 20 FB                jr nz,.sh
1029+ 3BAA ED 5B EC 3D  .nm:    ld de,(zx0)
1030+ 3BAE 3A F2 3D             ld a,(zw)
1031+ 3BB1 4F                   ld c,a
1032+ 3BB2 CB 20        .px:    sla b
1033+ 3BB4 30 07                jr nc,.skip
1034+ 3BB6 7A                   ld a,d
1035+ 3BB7 B7                   or a
1036+ 3BB8 20 03                jr nz,.skip                     ; off the left or right edge
1037+ 3BBA 6B                   ld l,e
1038+ 3BBB 36 1B        .col:   ld (hl),ZOMBIE_COL              ; (colour patched per figure)
1039+ 3BBD 13           .skip:  inc de
1040+ 3BBE 0D                   dec c
1041+ 3BBF 20 F1                jr nz,.px
1042+ 3BC1 21 EB 3D     .next:  ld hl,zy
1043+ 3BC4 34                   inc (hl)
1044+ 3BC5 21 F0 3D             ld hl,zcnt
1045+ 3BC8 35                   dec (hl)
1046+ 3BC9 20 B0                jr nz,.row
1047+ 3BCB C9                   ret
1048+ 3BCC
1049+ 3BCC              ; Projected height from the last project's Z -> zlvl, zw, zh.
1050+ 3BCC              ; Carry set if too small to draw.
1051+ 3BCC              zsize:
1052+ 3BCC 21 1A 00             ld hl,ZOMBIE_H
1053+ 3BCF CD 45 E8             call scale_comp
1054+ 3BD2 32 F4 3D             ld (zhr),a                      ; true projected height (unclamped)
1055+ 3BD5 FE 03                cp 3
1056+ 3BD7 D8                   ret c
1057+ 3BD8 FE 11                cp 17
1058+ 3BDA 38 02                jr c,.h
1059+ 3BDC 3E 10                ld a,16
1060+ 3BDE 21 10 30     .h:     ld hl,lvl_of_h
1061+ 3BE1 ED 31                add hl,a
1062+ 3BE3 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1063+ 3BE4 32 F1 3D             ld (zlvl),a
1064+ 3BE7 21 00 30             ld hl,spr_w
1065+ 3BEA ED 31                add hl,a
1066+ 3BEC 4E                   ld c,(hl)
1067+ 3BED 21 08 30             ld hl,spr_h
1068+ 3BF0 ED 31                add hl,a
1069+ 3BF2 7E                   ld a,(hl)
1070+ 3BF3 32 F3 3D             ld (zh),a
1071+ 3BF6 79                   ld a,c
1072+ 3BF7 32 F2 3D             ld (zw),a
1073+ 3BFA B7                   or a                            ; clear carry
1074+ 3BFB C9                   ret
1075+ 3BFC
1076+ 3BFC              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1077+ 3BFC              ; Zombie page data
1078+ 3BFC              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1079+ 3BFC              ; relative facing (0 walking away, 1 right, 2 towards, 3 left) ->
1080+ 3BFC              ; bit 0 = left sprite (else forward), bit 1 = mirror
1081+ 3BFC 02 03 00 01  face_tab:   db %10, %11, %00, %01
1082+ 3C00
1083+ 3C00 0A           zspeed:     db ZSPEED_INIT              ; zombie speed, world units per frame
1084+ 3C01                                                      ; in 4.4 fixed point (16 = 1.0, max 119:
1085+ 3C01                                                      ; fast zombies go 2x): raise it on
1086+ 3C01                                                      ; later levels
1087+ 3C01 0A           zsteps:     db ZSTEPS_INIT              ; frames between each zombie's
1088+ 3C02                                                      ; decision checks (1..): zsteps*zspeed*2
1089+ 3C02                                                      ; (fast zombies) must stay under 128*16
1090+ 3C02                                                      ; so none can walk through a junction
1091+ 3C02 00           zstep:      db 0
1092+ 3C03 00 00        zlook:      dw 0
1093+ 3C05 80           zbias_t:    db 128                      ; junction bias towards the pad (/256)
1094+ 3C06 00           zcur_i:     db 0                        ; index of the zombie being moved
1095+ 3C07 00 00 00...  zatk_flags: ds NZOMB                    ; per zombie: TRUE while attacking a base
1096+ 3C1B                                                      ; tile (its map dot red), else FALSE
1097+ 3C1B 00           zb_mask:    db 0                        ; zbest
1098+ 3C1C 00           zb_h:       db 0
1099+ 3C1D 00 00        zb_best:    dw 0
1100+ 3C1F 00 00        zb_dx:      dw 0
1101+ 3C21 00 00        zb_dz:      dw 0
1102+ 3C23 01 02 04 08  zbits:      db 1, 2, 4, 8               ; heading -> bit
1103+ 3C27 00 01 01 00  zdir_step:  db 0, 1,  1, 0,  0, -1,  -1, 0  ; heading -> (cell dx, dz)
1103+ 3C2B 00 FF FF 00
1104+ 3C2F 00 00 00...  base_hp:    ds (2*TERRACE_R+1)*(2*TERRACE_R+1)  ; hit points, pad and terrace
1105+ 3C48 00 00        zlook_fast: dw 0                        ; (fast zombies: twice the walk)
1106+ 3C4A 00           zi_fast:    db 0                        ; zombie_init: fast zombies still to make
1107+ 3C4B              ; fast zombies per level (level 1 first)
1108+ 3C4B 01           zfast_lvl:  db 1
1109+ 3C4C 00 00 00...  zombies:    ds NZOMB*ZREC
1110+ 3DC8 00 00 00...  znext:      ds NZOMB
1111+ 3DDC 01 00        zseed:      dw 1
1112+ 3DDE 00           zanim:      db 0
1113+ 3DDF 00           zcur:       db 0
1114+ 3DE0 00 00        zh_y:       dw 0
1115+ 3DE2 00 00        zh_z:       dw 0
1116+ 3DE4 00 00 00...  zt:         ds 6
1117+ 3DEA 00           zsx:        db 0

1135  7000                      DISPLAY "Colours end:",/A,$
1136  7000                      ORG HUMAN_ADDR
1137  7000                      INCLUDE "human.asm"
# file opened: human.asm
   1+ 7000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ 7000              ; Z-POLICE - humans to rescue (page 7, $7000-$7FFF: kept mapped in slot 3,
   3+ 7000              ; after city_col)
   4+ 7000              ;
   5+ 7000              ; Up to HUMAN_MAX humans, nhumans of them placed at random at start-up (on a
   6+ 7000              ; road, or on a roof low enough to land on).  They stand still, always face
   7+ 7000              ; the player and wave their right arm.  On the city map each is a blue dot,
   8+ 7000              ; flashing 3 frames on, 3 off.
   9+ 7000              ;
  10+ 7000              ; Base: landed on the landing pad (map code PAD_CODE), the passengers get off
  11+ 7000              ; one every DISEMBARK frames (a second) and are counted as saved.
  12+ 7000              ;
  13+ 7000              ; Rescue: when the craft has landed (speed 0, resting on the road or on the
  14+ 7000              ; roof under it), carries fewer than PASSENGER_MAX and a human stands within
  15+ 7000              ; RESCUE_R at the same height (a roof of the same height, or the road), the
  16+ 7000              ; human runs to it at HUMAN_RUN units a frame and climbs in.  A human on a
  17+ 7000              ; taller or lower roof cannot be reached.
  18+ 7000              ;
  19+ 7000              ; Each human is an HREC-byte record:
  20+ 7000              ;   +0,+1  x  (world units: high byte = cell, as for zombies)
  21+ 7000              ;   +2,+3  z
  22+ 7000              ;   +4,+5  y  height of its feet (roof height, or 0 on the road)
  23+ 7000              ;   +6     state: 0 = gone (rescued or unused), 1 = waiting
  24+ 7000              ;   +7,+8  cell (x, z) of its map dot
  25+ 7000              ;   +9     1 = map dot shown
  26+ 7000              ;   +10    hit points left (HUMAN_HP; a rocket blast, weapons.asm)
  27+ 7000              ;
  28+ 7000              ; Drawing: draw_cell (city page) asks hum_here whether a cell holds humans
  29+ 7000              ; (a 64x64 bit table, hum_bits) and draws them with human_cell: after the
  30+ 7000              ; building on whose roof they stand, so the roof doesn't cover them.  With
  31+ 7000              ; OCCLUSION_CULL the cell goes on the draw list instead (as its city_col
  32+ 7000              ; address, $6000-$6FFF) and is drawn from there.
  33+ 7000              ;==============================================================================
  34+ 7000
  35+ 7000              HREC            equ 11
  36+ 7000
  37+ 7000                      ALIGN 256
  38+ 7000 00 00 00...  hum_bits:   ds GRID*GRID/8              ; 1 bit per cell: humans stand there
  39+ 7200
  40+ 7200                      INCLUDE "tables_human.inc"      ; hum_ptr, waving stick men

 374+ 75E9 CB 1D                rr l                            ; 3/8 min
 375+ 75EB D1                   pop de
 376+ 75EC 19                   add hl,de
 377+ 75ED 22 79 76             ld (hu_dist),hl
 378+ 75F0 11 01 02             ld de,RESCUE_R+1
 379+ 75F3 B7                   or a

 381+ 75F6 D0                   ret nc                          ; too far
 382+ 75F7 3E 01                ld a,1
 383+ 75F9 32 74 76             ld (hu_moved),a
 384+ 75FC 2A 79 76             ld hl,(hu_dist)
 385+ 75FF 11 0A 00             ld de,HUMAN_RUN+1
 386+ 7602 B7                   or a
 387+ 7603 ED 52                sbc hl,de
 388+ 7605 38 3C                jr c,.board                     ; reaches the craft this frame
 389+ 7607                      ; step HUMAN_RUN towards it: (dx, dz) * HUMAN_RUN / dist
 390+ 7607 ED 5B 79 76          ld de,(hu_dist)
 391+ 760B 21 00 09             ld hl,HUMAN_RUN*256
 392+ 760E CD 51 76             call udiv16                     ; HL = S7.8 fraction

 395+ 7617 ED 5B 7B 76          ld de,(hu_t)

 397+ 761E DD 5E 00             ld e,(ix+0)
 398+ 7621 DD 56 01             ld d,(ix+1)

 400+ 7625 DD 75 00             ld (ix+0),l
 401+ 7628 DD 74 01             ld (ix+1),h

 403+ 762E ED 5B 7B 76          ld de,(hu_t)
 404+ 7632 CD FE B5             call fmul
 405+ 7635 DD 5E 02             ld e,(ix+2)
 406+ 7638 DD 56 03             ld d,(ix+3)
 407+ 763B 19                   add hl,de
 408+ 763C DD 75 02             ld (ix+2),l
 409+ 763F DD 74 03             ld (ix+3),h

 411+ 7643 0E 00        .board: ld c,0                          ; in: dot off, gone, one more aboard
 412+ 7645 CD 81 78             call hum_dot

 414+ 764C 21 6A 76             ld hl,passengers

 418+ 7651              ; HL / DE -> HL, unsigned 16-bit
 419+ 7651              udiv16:

 421+ 7652 4D                   ld c,l
 422+ 7653 21 00 00             ld hl,0
 423+ 7656 06 10                ld b,16

 432+ 7664 10 F2                djnz .lp
 433+ 7666 67                   ld h,a
 434+ 7667 69                   ld l,c

# file opened: map.asm
   1+ 7759              ;==============================================================================
   2+ 7759              ; Z-POLICE - city map (page 7, after human.asm: kept mapped in slot 3)
   3+ 7759              ;
   4+ 7759              ; Everything that draws the GRID x GRID city map at the right of the
   5+ 7759              ; instrument panel (pixel columns MAP_X.., rows 128-191, one pixel per cell,
   6+ 7759              ; north up):
   7+ 7759              ;   * map_init / map_l2: the map itself, drawn once at start-up into both
   8+ 7759              ;     Layer 2 buffers, and the ULA set-up over it (called from ula_init);
   9+ 7759              ;   * map_player: the player's flashing white dot, on the ULA (draw_hud);
  10+ 7759              ;   * zblip / zblip_off: zombie blips, bright green (zombie.asm, weapons.asm);
  11+ 7759              ;   * hum_dots / hum_dot: humans' flashing blue dots (human.asm);
  12+ 7759              ;   * map_pix / map_under: set one map cell's pixel in both Layer 2 buffers,
  13+ 7759              ;     and the colour the map itself has there.
  14+ 7759              ; Page 7 is always mapped, so all the pages above can call in here.
  15+ 7759              ;==============================================================================
  16+ 7759
  17+ 7759              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  18+ 7759              ; City map: GRID x GRID cells at one pixel each (64x64), north up, at pixel
  19+ 7759              ; columns MAP_X.. and rows 128-191.  It is drawn on Layer 2, into both
  20+ 7759              ; buffers once (the viewport clear only touches rows 0-127), so each
  21+ 7759              ; building can be its own colour; zombie blips and human dots are written
  22+ 7759              ; into it too.  The ULA above it is transparent paper with white ink for the
  23+ 7759              ; player's flashing dot.
  24+ 7759              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  25+ 7759              map_init:
  26+ 7759 AF                   xor a                           ; no player dot drawn yet
  27+ 775A 32 DD 78             ld (mp_mask),a
  28+ 775D 21 18 9A             ld hl,ULA_ATTR+16*32+MAP_COL    ; map area: transparent paper,
  29+ 7760 0E 08                ld c,8                          ; white ink
  30+ 7762 06 08        .ar:    ld b,32-MAP_COL
  31+ 7764 E5                   push hl
  32+ 7765 36 07        .ac:    ld (hl),7
  33+ 7767 23                   inc hl
  34+ 7768 10 FB                djnz .ac
  35+ 776A E1                   pop hl
  36+ 776B 11 20 00             ld de,32
  37+ 776E 19                   add hl,de
  38+ 776F 0D                   dec c
  39+ 7770 20 F0                jr nz,.ar
  40+ 7772 21 18 90             ld hl,ULA_BMP+$1000+MAP_COL     ; no panel divider over the map
  41+ 7775 06 08                ld b,32-MAP_COL
  42+ 7777 36 00        .dv:    ld (hl),0
  43+ 7779 23                   inc hl
  44+ 777A 10 FB                djnz .dv
  45+ 777C 3E 12                ld a,PAGE_L2_A                  ; both Layer 2 buffers
  46+ 777E CD 83 77             call map_l2
  47+ 7781 3E 18                ld a,PAGE_L2_B
  48+ 7783                      ; fall through
  49+ 7783
  50+ 7783              ; A = first 8K page of a Layer 2 buffer: draw the map into it.  Streets are
  51+ 7783              ; MAP_BG, buildings their colour family's base shade.
  52+ 7783              map_l2:
  53+ 7783 32 DF 78             ld (ml_base),a
  54+ 7786 21 55 A0             ld hl,city_map
  55+ 7789 11 00 60             ld de,city_col
  56+ 778C 0E 00                ld c,0                          ; C = j (row), +Z
  57+ 778E 3E BF        .row:   ld a,191                        ; y = 191 - j  (north up)
  58+ 7790 91                   sub c
  59+ 7791 F5                   push af
  60+ 7792 07                   rlca
  61+ 7793 07                   rlca
  62+ 7794 07                   rlca
  63+ 7795 E6 07                and 7                           ; its 32-row section
  64+ 7797 47                   ld b,a
  65+ 7798 3A DF 78             ld a,(ml_base)
  66+ 779B 80                   add a,b
  67+ 779C ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a                   ; paged in at $0000
  68+ 779F F1                   pop af
  69+ 77A0 E6 1F                and 31
  70+ 77A2 DD 67                ld ixh,a
  71+ 77A4 DD 2E C0             ld ixl,MAP_X
  72+ 77A7 06 40                ld b,GRID
  73+ 77A9 7E           .px:    ld a,(hl)
  74+ 77AA B7                   or a
  75+ 77AB 3E 27                ld a,MAP_BG
  76+ 77AD 28 03                jr z,.st
  77+ 77AF 1A                   ld a,(de)
  78+ 77B0 F6 01                or SHADE_FACE
  79+ 77B2 DD 77 00     .st:    ld (ix+0),a
  80+ 77B5 DD 23                inc ix
  81+ 77B7 23                   inc hl
  82+ 77B8 13                   inc de
  83+ 77B9 10 EE                djnz .px
  84+ 77BB 0C                   inc c
  85+ 77BC 79                   ld a,c
  86+ 77BD FE 40                cp GRID
  87+ 77BF 20 CD                jr nz,.row
  88+ 77C1 C9                   ret
  89+ 77C2
  90+ 77C2              ; D = y, E = x -> HL = ULA byte (screen paged at ULA_BMP), A = pixel mask
  91+ 77C2              ula_pixel:
  92+ 77C2 ED 94                pixelad                         ; HL = $4000-based address
  93+ 77C4 7C                   ld a,h
  94+ 77C5 C6 40                add a,high (ULA_BMP-$4000)
  95+ 77C7 67                   ld h,a
  96+ 77C8 ED 95                setae                           ; A = $80 >> (x & 7)
  97+ 77CA C9                   ret
  98+ 77CB
  99+ 77CB              ; Flashing player position: undo last frame's XOR, then on alternate frames
 100+ 77CB              ; XOR the pixel under the player (1 map pixel per cell).
 101+ 77CB              map_player:
 102+ 77CB 2A DB 78             ld hl,(mp_addr)
 103+ 77CE 3A DD 78             ld a,(mp_mask)
 104+ 77D1 AE                   xor (hl)
 105+ 77D2 77                   ld (hl),a
 106+ 77D3 AF                   xor a
 107+ 77D4 32 DD 78             ld (mp_mask),a
 108+ 77D7 21 DE 78             ld hl,mp_frame
 109+ 77DA 34                   inc (hl)
 110+ 77DB CB 46                bit 0,(hl)
 111+ 77DD C8                   ret z                           ; off this frame
 112+ 77DE 3A 39 B6             ld a,(cam_x+2)                  ; cell x: one map pixel per cell
 113+ 77E1 C6 C0                add a,MAP_X
 114+ 77E3 5F                   ld e,a
 115+ 77E4 3A 3C B6             ld a,(cam_z+2)                  ; cell z
 116+ 77E7 ED 44                neg
 117+ 77E9 C6 BF                add a,191                       ; north up
 118+ 77EB 57                   ld d,a
 119+ 77EC CD C2 77             call ula_pixel
 120+ 77EF 22 DB 78             ld (mp_addr),hl
 121+ 77F2 32 DD 78             ld (mp_mask),a
 122+ 77F5 AE                   xor (hl)
 123+ 77F6 77                   ld (hl),a
 124+ 77F7 C9                   ret
 125+ 77F8
 126+ 77F8              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 127+ 77F8              ; Zombie blips: each zombie is one bright green (MAP_ZOMBIE) pixel at its cell,
 128+ 77F8              ; using record bytes +12,+13 (blip cell) and +14 (bit 0: plotted; bits 1-2:
 129+ 77F8              ; its place in the shade cycle).  Each time zblip draws it, the blip takes
 130+ 77F8              ; the next shade of zshades: bright, mid, dark, mid, bright...  Zombies only
 131+ 77F8              ; stand in street cells, so taking a blip off puts the street colour (MAP_BG)
 132+ 77F8              ; back.  (When two zombies share a cell and one leaves, the other's blip is
 133+ 77F8              ; gone until its own next check.)
 134+ 77F8              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 135+ 77F8              ; Move zombie IX's street map blip to its current cell, in its next shade
 136+ 77F8              zblip:
 137+ 77F8 CD 1F 78             call zblip_off
 138+ 77FB DD 5E 01             ld e,(ix+1)                     ; cell x
 139+ 77FE DD 56 03             ld d,(ix+3)                     ; cell z
 140+ 7801 DD 73 0C             ld (ix+12),e
 141+ 7804 DD 72 0D             ld (ix+13),d
 142+ 7807 DD 7E 0E             ld a,(ix+14)                    ; shade step (bits 1-2), plotted off
 143+ 780A 4F                   ld c,a
 144+ 780B C6 02                add a,2                         ; next step, and plotted
 145+ 780D E6 06                and 6
 146+ 780F 3C                   inc a
 147+ 7810 DD 77 0E             ld (ix+14),a
 148+ 7813 79                   ld a,c
 149+ 7814 0F                   rrca
 150+ 7815 E6 03                and 3
 151+ 7817 21 D7 78             ld hl,zshades
 152+ 781A ED 31                add hl,a
 153+ 781C 7E                   ld a,(hl)                       ; this step's shade
 154+ 781D 18 11                jr map_pix
 155+ 781F
 156+ 781F              ; Take zombie IX's blip off the map
 157+ 781F              zblip_off:
 158+ 781F DD CB 0E 46          bit 0,(ix+14)
 159+ 7823 C8                   ret z
 160+ 7824 DD CB 0E 86          res 0,(ix+14)                   ; (keeps its shade step)
 161+ 7828 DD 5E 0C             ld e,(ix+12)
 162+ 782B DD 56 0D             ld d,(ix+13)
 163+ 782E 3E 27                ld a,MAP_BG                     ; a street cell again
 164+ 7830                      ; fall through
 165+ 7830
 166+ 7830              ; Set map pixel for cell (E, D) to colour A in both Layer 2 buffers.  Pages
 167+ 7830              ; their section in at $0000 (slot 0, which drawing re-pages as it goes).
 168+ 7830              map_pix:
 169+ 7830 4F                   ld c,a
 170+ 7831 3E BF                ld a,191                        ; y = 191 - z  (north up)
 171+ 7833 92                   sub d
 172+ 7834 47                   ld b,a
 173+ 7835 07                   rlca
 174+ 7836 07                   rlca
 175+ 7837 07                   rlca
 176+ 7838 E6 07                and 7                           ; its 32-row section
 177+ 783A 57                   ld d,a
 178+ 783B 78                   ld a,b
 179+ 783C E6 1F                and 31
 180+ 783E 67                   ld h,a
 181+ 783F 7B                   ld a,e
 182+ 7840 C6 C0                add a,MAP_X
 183+ 7842 6F                   ld l,a                          ; HL = pixel in the section
 184+ 7843 3E 12                ld a,PAGE_L2_A
 185+ 7845 82                   add a,d
 186+ 7846 ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a
 187+ 7849 71                   ld (hl),c
 188+ 784A 3E 18                ld a,PAGE_L2_B
 189+ 784C 82                   add a,d
 190+ 784D ED 92 50             nextreg L2SEC_MMU,a
 191+ 7850 71                   ld (hl),c
 192+ 7851 C9                   ret
 193+ 7852
 194+ 7852              ; Zombie IX attacking: its blip red (MAP_ZOMBIE_ATK) at its current cell,
 195+ 7852              ; until zblip draws it normally again
 196+ 7852              zblip_red:
 197+ 7852 CD 1F 78             call zblip_off

 199+ 7858 DD 56 03             ld d,(ix+3)
 200+ 785B DD 73 0C             ld (ix+12),e
 201+ 785E DD 72 0D             ld (ix+13),d
 202+ 7861 DD CB 0E C6          set 0,(ix+14)                   ; plotted (keeps its shade step)
 203+ 7865 3E 13                ld a,MAP_ZOMBIE_ATK
 204+ 7867 C3 30 78             jp map_pix

 206+ 786A              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
 207+ 786A              ; Human dots: flashing blue (HUMAN_BLIP), using record bytes +7,+8 (dot cell)
 208+ 786A              ; and +9 (shown).
 209+ 786A              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------

 211+ 786A              hum_dots:

1148  0200                      DISPLAY "Sine end:  ",/A,$
1149  0200
1150  0200              ; Sprite pattern banks, swapped into slot 7 ($E000) while they load
1151  0200                      MMU SPRBANK_SLOT e, PAGE_SPR0, SPRBANK_ADDR
1152  E000                      ORG SPRBANK_ADDR
1153  E000                      INCLUDE "sprite_data.asm"
# file opened: sprite_data.asm
   1+ E000              ;==============================================================================
   2+ E000              ; Z-POLICE - sprite pattern banks (8K pages, assembled at $E000)
   3+ E000              ;
   4+ E000              ; Hardware sprite patterns are only needed when they are loaded into the
   5+ E000              ; sprite pattern memory, so they live in their own pages, out of the way,
   6+ E000              ; each with its own loader so that no resident memory is used:
   7+ E000              ;
   8+ E000              ;       nextreg CODE_MMU,PAGE_SPR0      ; the bank into slot 7
   9+ E000              ;       call spr_load_bank              ; copy its patterns
  10+ E000              ;
  11+ E000              ; Call it from outside slot 7 (resident code or a game logic page in slot
  12+ E000              ; 6), and page back whatever slot 7 had afterwards if it is still needed.
  13+ E000              ; spr_load_bank is the same address ($E000) in every bank.
  14+ E000              ;
  15+ E000              ; Each bank (SPRBANK_ADDR = $E000):
  16+ E000              ;   +0          spr_load_bank: the loader (SPRB_LOADER), the same in each
  17+ E000              ;   SPRB_COUNT  number of patterns (1..31: 256 bytes each, 8-bit 16x16)
  18+ E000              ;   SPRB_FIRST  the pattern number the first one loads to (0..63)
  19+ E000              ;   SPRB_DATA   the patterns, one after another
  20+ E000              ; More sprites: add a bank (the next free page, its own PAGE_SPRn in
  21+ E000              ; memory_bank_defs.asm, the MMU / ORG lines in zpolice.asm and a block here
  22+ E000              ; starting with SPRB_LOADER).
  23+ E000              ;==============================================================================
  24+ E000
  25+ E000              ; Copy this bank's patterns into the sprite pattern memory.  Runs from the
  26+ E000              ; bank itself: reads only, so it needs no variables.
  27+ E000                      MACRO SPRB_LOADER
  28+ E000 ~                    ld a,(SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_FIRST)  ; from this pattern
  29+ E000 ~                    ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT
  30+ E000 ~                    out (c),a
  31+ E000 ~                    ld a,(SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_COUNT)
  32+ E000 ~                    ld d,a
  33+ E000 ~                    ld hl,SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_DATA
  34+ E000 ~                    ld c,SPR_PAT_PORT
  35+ E000 ~            .lp:    ld b,0                          ; 256 bytes a pattern
  36+ E000 ~                    otir
  37+ E000 ~                    dec d
  38+ E000 ~                    jr nz,.lp
  39+ E000 ~                    ret
  40+ E000                      ENDM
  41+ E000
  42+ E000              spr_load_bank   equ SPRBANK_ADDR        ; the loader, in every bank
  43+ E000              SPRB_COUNT      equ spr_bank0_hdr-spr_bank0
  44+ E000              SPRB_FIRST      equ SPRB_COUNT+1
  45+ E000              SPRB_DATA       equ SPRB_COUNT+2
  46+ E000
  47+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  48+ E000              ; Bank 0 (PAGE_SPR0): the human status sprites, patterns 0-3
  49+ E000              ; (zpolice.asm maps PAGE_SPR0 to SPRBANK_SLOT, ORG SPRBANK_ADDR, first)
  50+ E000              ;------------------------------------------------------------------------------
  51+ E000              spr_bank0:
  52+ E000                      SPRB_LOADER
  52+ E000 3A 1A E0    >        ld a,(SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_FIRST)  ; from this pattern
  52+ E003 01 3B 30    >        ld bc,SPR_SLOT_PORT
  52+ E006 ED 79       >        out (c),a
  52+ E008 3A 19 E0    >        ld a,(SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_COUNT)
  52+ E00B 57          >        ld d,a
  52+ E00C 21 1B E0    >        ld hl,SPRBANK_ADDR+SPRB_DATA
  52+ E00F 0E 5B       >        ld c,SPR_PAT_PORT
  52+ E011 06 00       >.lp:    ld b,0                          ; 256 bytes a pattern
  52+ E013 ED B3       >        otir
  52+ E015 15          >        dec d
  52+ E016 20 F9       >        jr nz,.lp
  52+ E018 C9          >        ret
  53+ E019              spr_bank0_hdr:
  54+ E019 04                   db HSPR_COUNT                   ; SPRB_COUNT
  55+ E01A 00                   db 0                            ; SPRB_FIRST
  56+ E01B                      INCLUDE "tables_hspr.inc"       ; hspr_patterns (gentables.py):
# file opened: tables_hspr.inc
